### [1] [LitReview Arena: Evaluating Literature Review Agents with Battle-Style Peer Review Platform](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21374)
*Ruotong Zhao,Zhiyu Chen,Xurui Liu,Haidong Xue,Dong Liang,Jigao Fu,Wu YanBiao,Yuanyi Zhen,Fengli Xu,Yong Li*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 20 pages, ICML 2026

Selection reason: 面向文献综述agent的对战式评测平台，提供专家校准的agent评估方法，可迁移到coding agent评测。

TL;DR: 提出了LitReview Arena平台和LitJudge评估器，通过3000个专家判断发现现有系统在综述质量上远逊于人工，且LLM-as-a-judge与人类专家对齐不足，而LitJudge显著提升了对齐程度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动生成的文献综述难以严格评估，因为许多研究效用方面依赖专家判断而非参考重叠指标，需要更符合专家标准的评估协议和校准模型。

Method: 构建LitReview Arena对战式评估平台，邀请有AI论文写作经验的领域专家按五个综述标准进行维度级匿名评审；收集约3000个专家判断，并训练专家校准的LitJudge评估器。

Result: 最强系统对人工草稿胜率仅23.0%，智能体LLM比基础模型强60%以上；现有LLM-as-a-judge与专家Spearman rho=0.467，而LitJudge达到0.78，接近专家间一致性。

Conclusion: LitReview Arena和LitJudge为综述评估提供了更可靠的协议和评估器，代码和数据已公开，有助于推进自动综述评估研究。

中文摘要：文献综述对科学进步至关重要，但严格评估自动生成的综述仍然困难，因为研究效用的许多方面依赖于专家判断而非参考重叠度量。我们引入了LitReview Arena，一个对战式评估平台，其结构化协议专为文献综述质量定制：具有AI论文写作经验的领域专家比较匿名草稿，被匹配到其专业领域内的主题，并提供基于五个文献综述特定标准的维度级结果。通过该协议，我们收集了约3000个专家判断，每个包含五个维度级结果，并表明即使是最强大的现有系统，在整体效用方面对人工草稿的决胜局中仅赢得23.0%的胜率，而智能体LLM（如Sonar Deep Research）则显著优于基础语言模型60%以上。我们进一步发现，现有的LLM-as-a-judge方法与人类专家存在显著不一致（Spearman's rho=0.467），尤其是在综合密集型标准（如论文结构、研究建议）上。利用收集的偏好数据，我们提供了专家校准的评估器LitJudge，其一致性提高到Spearman's rho=0.78，与专家间一致性相当；代码和数据可在https://github.com/VanellopeAsher/LitReview-Arena公开获取。

Original abstract: Literature reviews are essential to scientific progress, but rigorously evaluating automatically generated reviews remains difficult because many aspects of research utility depend on expert judgment rather than reference-overlap metrics. We introduce LitReview Arena, a battle-style evaluation platform with a structured protocol tailored to literature review quality: domain experts with AI paper-writing experience compare anonymized drafts, are matched to topics within their expertise, and provide dimension-wise outcomes over five literature-review-specific criteria. From this protocol, we collect approximately 3k expert judgments, each containing five dimension-wise outcomes, and show that even the strongest current systems win only 23.0% of decisive matches against human drafts on overall utility, while agentic LLMs such as Sonar Deep Research substantially outperform base language models by over 60%. We further find that existing LLM-as-a-judge methods are substantially misaligned with human experts (Spearman's rho=0.467), especially on synthesis-heavy criteria such as paper structure and research suggestions. Using the collected preference data, we provide an expert-calibrated evaluator, LitJudge, which improves alignment to Spearman's rho=0.78, comparable to inter-expert consistency; code and data are publicly available at https://github.com/VanellopeAsher/LitReview-Arena.

</details>


### [2] [SchemaRouter: Field-Aware Tool Routing for Efficient Heterogeneous Agentic RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21375)
*Yong-eun Cho*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 13 pages, 4 figures, 6 tables. Code, benchmark, and fixtures: https://github.com/JDeun/SchemaRouter_research

Selection reason: 面向agentic RAG的工具路由与字段选择层，减少token和延迟，对coding agent工具调用设计有启发。

TL;DR: SchemaRouter是一个基于模式图的轻量级路由层，通过小型LLM提取意图和确定性字段选择，在材料科学基准上以较低令牌和延迟达到与贪婪获取相当的准确率，同时提供来源和许可依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG系统暴露所有工具描述或仅用向量相似性选择工具，导致过度获取（增加负载、令牌和延迟）和获取不足（遗漏关键字段），成本高昂且影响查询回答质量。

Method: 提出SchemaRouter，将工具、端点、参数、响应字段、领域概念、单位、来源和许可策略构建为模式图；使用小型LLM提取意图、概念和来源约束，通过意图组投影和概念-字段匹配（含别名层）进行确定性字段选择，生成可执行的工具计划。

Result: 在110个查询的材料科学基准上，SchemaRouter实现答案准确率0.71，与fetch-everything相当，超prompt-all的0.66；使用227个检索令牌（远低于fetch-everything的2066），端到端延迟比prompt-all低2.7倍；工具精确率0.93，参数有效性1.0；62%答案包含来源/许可信息（基线约0%）。此外，最小化字段数使准确率降至0.56，而保留召回率的投影可恢复最高准确率。

Conclusion: SchemaRouter在保持竞争性准确率的同时，显著提升效率、实现与模式大小无关的扩展性，并提供可验证的来源/许可依据；应避免过度压缩字段选择，而采用保留召回率的投影策略。

中文摘要：异构智能体检索增强生成（RAG）系统越来越多地编排外部API、内部数据库、向量存储和图存储。将全部工具描述暴露给LLM智能体，或仅通过向量相似性选择工具，会导致两类代价高昂的失败：过度获取（over-fetching），它增加了载荷大小、令牌使用和延迟；以及获取不足（under-fetching），它遗漏了回答查询所需的字段。我们提出了SchemaRouter，一个轻量级路由层，将工具、端点、参数、响应字段、领域概念、单位、来源（provenance）和许可策略表示为模式图（schema graph）。给定查询，SchemaRouter生成一个可执行的工具计划，指定要调用哪些工具以及要检索哪些字段。一个小型LLM提取意图、概念和来源约束，而字段选择通过意图组投影和带有别名层的概念-字段匹配在图上是确定性的。在110个查询的材料科学基准测试中，SchemaRouter实现了0.71的答案准确率，与fetch-everything在重叠置信区间内匹配，并超过prompt-all的0.66，尽管它们的区间重叠。它使用227个检索上下文令牌，而fetch-everything为2,066个，并且端到端延迟比prompt-all低2.7倍。它还获得了最佳的工具精确率0.93和参数有效性1.0。SchemaRouter在62%的答案中提供了来源和许可信息的基础，而所有基线约为0%。我们还发现，最小化所选字段数量是适得其反的：它将答案准确率降低到0.56，并且令牌节省可忽略不计，而保留召回率的投影恢复了最高准确率。SchemaRouter在竞争性准确率下提高了效率、与模式大小无关的扩展性，以及可验证的来源/许可依据的答案。

Original abstract: Heterogeneous agentic retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) systems increasingly orchestrate external APIs, internal databases, vector stores, and graph stores. Exposing all tool descriptions to an LLM agent, or selecting tools only by vector similarity, causes two costly failures: over-fetching, which increases payload size, token use, and latency, and under-fetching, which omits fields needed to answer the query.
  We present SchemaRouter, a lightweight routing layer that represents tools, endpoints, parameters, response fields, domain concepts, units, provenance, and license policies as a schema graph. Given a query, SchemaRouter emits an executable tool plan specifying which tools to call and which fields to retrieve. A small LLM extracts intent, concepts, and source constraints, while field selection is deterministic over the graph through intent-group projection and concept-field matching with an alias layer.
  On a materials-science benchmark of 110 queries, SchemaRouter achieves answer accuracy of 0.71, matching fetch-everything within overlapping confidence intervals and exceeding prompt-all's 0.66, though their intervals overlap. It uses 227 retrieved-context tokens versus 2,066 for fetch-everything and achieves 2.7x lower end-to-end latency than prompt-all. It also obtains the best tool-exact rate of 0.93 and parameter validity of 1.0. SchemaRouter grounds provenance and license information in 62 percent of answers, compared with approximately 0 percent for all baselines.
  We also find that minimizing selected-field count is counterproductive: it reduces answer accuracy to 0.56 with negligible token savings, while recall-preserving projection restores top accuracy. SchemaRouter improves efficiency, schema-size-independent scaling, and verifiable provenance/license-grounded answering at competitive accuracy.

</details>


### [3] [There Is No Neutral Harness: Modern LLM Leaderboards Are Manufactured by Config-Fragile Items](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21382)
*V. S. Raghu Parupudi*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 25 pages in total

Selection reason: 揭示LLM评测harness配置脆弱性，提示benchmark排序不可靠，对agent基准与speedup验证有方法论价值。

TL;DR: 将评估配置作为自变量，揭示其对单题答案和模型排名的影响，并指出评分选择是最关键的因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究只报告配置敏感性导致的整体分数方差，未分析方差具体落在哪些题目上，以及这些题目是否影响模型间的比较。作者希望将配置变化的影响精确到单个题目，并检验其对模型排名的决定性作用。

Method: 构建脆弱性网格：固定题目、权重和解码方式，仅改变评估框架（选项顺序、提示措辞、答案读取方式等），对12个开放权重模型在3679道题上应用26种配置，记录每个模型-题目-配置的正确性比特。

Result: 模型的得分呈现为区间（如gemma4-31b从31%到89%）；配置脆弱题目平均承载相邻模型差距的95.7%；12个模型中有4个可在某配置下排名第一；题目区分度与脆弱性相关系数为0.28；评分选择是影响最大的配置维度。

Conclusion: 评估配置本身可以决定排行榜的胜者；压缩方法倾向于保留脆弱题目；排行榜在报告排名前应使用脆弱性网格作为检查工具。作者发布所有数据和脚本以供验证。

中文摘要：多项选择基准固定了问题和正确答案，但没有固定评估框架（harness）：选项的顺序、提示的措辞，以及语言模型的答案是从生成文本中读取还是从每个选项的似然中读取。关于这种评估框架敏感性的研究将其报告为总体分数的方差，却未考察方差落在哪些题目上，以及这些题目是否是区分一个模型与下一个模型的题目。我们将大型语言模型（LLM）的评估框架视为一个自变量，并将其影响分解到单个题目。我们引入了脆弱性网格（fragility grid）：来自4个家族的12个开放权重指令微调LLM，在26种同样合理的评估框架配置下，回答了来自4个基准（ARC、HellaSwag、MMLU、TruthfulQA）的同一组3,679道题目，为每个模型、题目和配置记录一个正确性比特。由于题目、权重和贪心解码保持不变，只有评估框架变化，因此比较是匹配的。在网格下，模型的分数是一个区间而非一个点：仅取决于评估框架，gemma4-31b的得分在31%到89%之间。以下三项结果随之而来。在两个相邻模型都稳定回答的题目上，该对模型打成平手，而配置脆弱（config-fragile）的题目平均承载了该对模型差距的95.7%。12个模型中有4个在某种配置下达到排名第一，因此评估框架选择了胜者。题目区分度（benchmark-compression方法最大化的属性）与脆弱性的相关系数为0.28（95%置信区间0.25至0.30），因此压缩保留了脆弱题目而非移除它们。评分选择（而非协议通常固定的选项顺序）是关键的轴心。我们发布了逐题记录和分析脚本，每个数字都可以在CPU上几秒钟内重新生成，并将脆弱性网格定位为排行榜在报告顺序之前可以运行的一项检查。

Original abstract: Multiple-choice benchmarks fix the questions and the correct answers, but not the harness: the order of the options, the wording of the prompt, and whether a language model's answer is read from generated text or from per-option likelihoods. Work on this harness sensitivity reports it as aggregate score variance, leaving unexamined which items the variance falls on and whether they are the items that separate one model from the next. We treat the evaluation harness of large language models (LLMs) as an independent variable and resolve its effect to single items. We introduce the \textit{fragility grid}: 12 open-weight instruction-tuned LLMs from 4 families answer the same 3{,}679 items from 4 benchmarks (ARC, HellaSwag, MMLU, TruthfulQA) under 26 equally defensible harness configurations, recording one correctness bit for every model, item, and configuration. The comparison is matched, since the items, the weights, and the greedy decoding stay fixed while only the harness varies. Under the grid a model's score is a band rather than a point: gemma4-31b scores between 31 and 89 percent depending only on the harness. Three results follow. On the items that two adjacent models both answer stably the pair is tied, and config-fragile items carry 95.7 percent of a pair's gap on average. Four of the 12 models reach rank one under some configuration, so the harness selects the winner. Item discrimination, the property that benchmark-compression methods maximize, correlates with fragility at 0.28 (95 percent CI 0.25 to 0.30), so compression keeps the fragile items rather than removing them. The scoring choice, not the option order that protocols usually fix, is the load-bearing axis. We release the per-item records and the analysis script, from which every number regenerates on a CPU in seconds, and we position the fragility grid as a check a leaderboard can run before it reports an order.

</details>


### [4] [Let Credit Follow Computation: Architecture-Aware Credit Transport for Large Language Model Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21501)
*Qifan Shi,Zhaolu Kang,Chenghua Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 22 pages, 6 figures

Selection reason: LLM RL的计算感知信用分配与GAE改进，可迁移到coding agent的RL训练，但非直接agent框架。

TL;DR: 提出一种计算条件信用传输框架CCT和具体算法CompPO，通过将策略内部注意力集中度映射为逐token保留门来改进LLM RL中的信用分配，在多个基准上显著优于GRPO。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM RL的信用传输算子（如固定折扣GAE或组相对方法）与架构无关，未能利用Transformer策略自身的轨迹特定计算，从而限制了信用分配的效果。

Method: CompPO使用行为策略内部计算的分离统计量参数化因果核：将注意力集中度映射为有界的逐token保留门，用于单步自举和路径相关的广义优势迹（Comp-GAE），并设计传输对齐的评论家（TAC）复用actor的隐藏状态和路由信息。

Result: 在五个Qwen3-4B种子上，CompPO达到61.4%的留出准确率，对比GRPO的53.8%；与标准评论家或固定门消融相比均更优；在PPO网格运行中稳定性更高，并在Qwen3-4B和Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct上冻结评估分别提升4.3和3.9个宏平均点。

Conclusion: CompPO通过将信用传输与策略内部计算对齐，显著提升了LLM RL的样本效率和最终性能，且无需额外的大规模评论家模型，展示了轨迹特定信用分配的潜力。

中文摘要：在大语言模型强化学习（LLM RL）中，信用分配可以分解为三个对象：关于成功的证据、将该证据转换为词元级别优势的传输算子，以及将优势转化为策略更新的更新几何。近期工作大幅改进了证据、采样和更新几何，但传输算子通常与架构无关。固定折扣的GAE沿词元时间应用平稳几何核；组相对方法则在整个响应上广播结果统计量。这两种算子都无法表示Transformer策略本身所使用的轨迹特定计算。我们提出了计算条件信用传输（CCT），这是一个通用框架，其中行为策略内部计算的分离统计量参数化了因果核，该核将下游价值通过一次rollout进行传输。我们的具体算法CompPO将原生注意力集中度映射为有界的每词元保留门，在单步自举和路径相关的广义优势迹（Comp-GAE）中均使用该门，并协同设计了传输对齐的评论家（TAC），该评论家复用actor的隐藏状态和路由信息，而无需第二个同规模Transformer。任务奖励和裁剪的PPO策略目标保持不变；常数门可恢复固定系数GAE。在五个Qwen3-4B种子上，CompPO达到61.4%的最终留出准确率（95% CI [60.8,62.0]），而调优后的GRPO为53.8% [52.9,54.7]。无论是带标准评论家的Comp-GAE（55.2%）还是带固定门的TAC（56.4%）都无法与完整模型匹配（交互+2.4 [1.9,2.9]）。打乱和位置对照确认了轨迹特定对齐；CompPO在12次PPO网格运行中稳定10次，而GRPO稳定3次。冻结评估在Qwen3-4B和Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct上分别比GRPO提高4.3和3.9个贪心pass@1宏平均点。

Original abstract: Credit assignment in large-language-model reinforcement learning (LLM RL) can be separated into three objects: evidence about success, a transport operator that converts this evidence into token-level advantages, and an update geometry that turns advantages into policy changes. Recent work has greatly improved evidence, sampling, and update geometry, but the transport operator is usually architecture-agnostic. Fixed-discount GAE applies a stationary geometric kernel along token time; group-relative methods broadcast an outcome statistic across an entire response. Neither operator represents the trajectory-specific computation used by the Transformer policy itself. We introduce computation-conditioned credit transport (CCT), a general framework in which a detached statistic of the behavior policy's internal computation parameterizes the causal kernel that transports downstream value through a rollout. Our concrete algorithm, CompPO, maps native attention concentration to a bounded per-token retention gate, uses the gate in both the one-step bootstrap and a path-dependent generalized-advantage trace (Comp-GAE), and co-designs a transport-aligned critic (TAC) that reuses the actor's hidden states and routing information without a second same-scale Transformer. The task reward and clipped PPO policy objective remain unchanged; a constant gate recovers fixed-coefficient GAE. Across five Qwen3-4B seeds, CompPO reaches 61.4% final held-out accuracy (95% CI [60.8,62.0]) versus 53.8% [52.9,54.7] for tuned GRPO. Neither Comp-GAE with a standard critic (55.2%) nor TAC with a fixed gate (56.4%) matches the full model (interaction +2.4 [1.9,2.9]). Shuffle and position controls confirm trajectory-specific alignment; CompPO is stable in 10/12 PPO-grid runs versus 3/12. Frozen evaluation improves over GRPO by 4.3 and 3.9 greedy pass@1 macro points on Qwen3-4B and Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct.

</details>


### [5] [Data-Driven Dynamic Algorithm Dispatch with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21584)
*Rushil Shah,Emmanuel Lujan,Rabab Alomairy,Alan Edelman*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 3 pages, 2 figures. Accepted at the 2025 IEEE High Performance Extreme Computing Conference (HPEC). Outstanding Short Paper Award

Selection reason: LLM生成高性能线性代数算法分派启发式，直接相关于LLM驱动程序性能优化。

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于LLM的方法，利用LLaMA 3和性能数据库为高性能线性代数生成动态算法调度启发式，并在LU分解案例中验证了其复制专家策略的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高性能线性代数中的算法选择通常依赖专家设计的启发式规则，这些规则难以自动适应多样化的计算环境。本文旨在利用大型语言模型的模式识别能力，从性能数据中自动生成动态调度启发式，以提高算法选择的适应性和效率。

Method: 结合提示工程与LLaMA 3大型语言模型，使用经过整理的性能数据库进行学习，通过合成选择启发式规则来利用结构模式，从而识别快速的算法选择。以LU分解作为案例研究进行评估。

Result: 在LU分解案例中，模型成功复制了专家设计的调度策略，证明了该方法在生成有效算法启发式方面的可行性。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了LLM在算法发现中的潜力，为开发更具适应性和高性能的线性代数软件提供了新途径，是DARPA-MIT SmartSolve项目的一部分。

中文摘要：我们介绍了一种大型语言模型（LLM）驱动的方法，用于生成高性能线性代数中的动态算法调度启发式。通过将提示工程与LLaMA 3以及精选的性能数据库相结合，模型学会了综合选择启发式，利用结构模式来识别快速的算法选择。对LU分解的案例研究表明，该模型能够复现专家设计的策略。这项工作是DARPA-MIT SmartSolve项目的一部分，突显了LLM在算法发现以及开发更具适应性和快速线性代数软件方面的前景。

Original abstract: We introduce a large language model (LLM)-driven approach for generating dynamic algorithmic dispatch heuristics in high-performance linear algebra. By combining prompt engineering with LLaMA 3 and a curated performance database, the model learns to synthesize selection heuristics that exploit structural patterns to identify fast algorithmic choices. A case study on LU factorization demonstrates the model's ability to replicate expert-designed strategies. This work, developed as part of the DARPA-MIT SmartSolve project, highlights the promise of LLMs for algorithmic discovery and the development of more adaptive, fast linear algebra software.

</details>


### [6] [K-Bench: measuring model performance on real scientific agent requests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21601)
*Aubrey Brueckner,Darshil Patel,Yuhuan He,Timothy Kassis*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 48 pages, 17 figures

Selection reason: 真实科学agent请求的评测基准，提供多维度评分与过度声称分析，对agent评测方法有参考价值。

TL;DR: 基于真实科学请求的 K-Bench 01 评估显示，9个前沿模型均未达到专家接受阈值，主要失败标签是过度宣称。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有科学AI基准多是为评分而设计，无法反映真实科学请求的欠规格、带附件、无真值等特征，因此需要构建更贴近实际的评估方法。

Method: 从 K-Dense Web 实时用户流量中采样首轮请求，由九个前沿模型在相同沙箱中端到端运行，产生1602次智能体运行，并由三位盲审语言模型评委按八维评分标准进行评分。

Result: 没有模型在所有三位评委下通过8分阈值；gpt-5.6-sol 合并均值最高（8.04），但置信区间跨越阈值且评委排序不一致；47.6%的评分低于阈值；科学准确性得分低于沟通；过度宣称是最常见失败标签（31.4%）。

Conclusion: 科学智能体的评估应关注交付内容、声称内容与生成产物的联合分布，而非单一排行榜名次。

中文摘要：科学人工智能的基准大多是为评分而编写的：多项选择题、带有参考答案的精心策划的智能体任务，或具有已知生成结构的模拟器。真实的科学请求则不然。它们规格不足、带有附件，并且缺乏真实答案。我们报告了 K-Bench 01，这是一个基于从 K-Dense Web 实时用户流量中采样的首轮请求构建的评估，并由九个前沿模型在相同的沙箱中端到端运行，产生了 1,602 次完整的智能体运行。三位盲审语言模型评委根据一个八维评分标准对每次运行进行评分。在一个以 8 为锚点、指示评委“领域科学家会接受该工作（仅需少量修改）”的评分标准上，没有任何模型能在所有三位评委下通过该分数线。gpt-5.6-sol 的合并均值最高，为 8.04，但其 95% 置信区间 [7.80, 8.23] 跨越了阈值，并且三位评委中有两位将 claude-opus-5 排在第一位。因此，我们将系统的排序作为可复现的量，将绝对水平视为测量工具的属性，并将榜单榜首视为未决问题。在所有 39,934 个评分判断中——包括每个评估的八个维度评分和一个整体性评分，不包括不适用项——47.6% 低于 8 分阈值。难度在评分标准上并不均匀：科学准确性的平均分为 6.22，而沟通为 7.33，两者分母相同，并且在全部九个模型中的方向也相同。最主要的失败标签是过度宣称（overclaiming），出现在 31.4% 的评估中。我们认为，对科学智能体而言，有信息量的量不是排行榜名次，而是所交付内容、所声称内容以及所生成产物的联合分布。

Original abstract: Benchmarks for scientific artificial intelligence are mostly written to be scored: multiple-choice questions, curated agent tasks with reference solutions, or simulators with a known generative structure. Real scientific requests arrive differently. They are underspecified, they carry attachments, and lack ground truth. We report K-Bench 01, an evaluation built from first-turn requests sampled from live user traffic on K-Dense Web and run end to end by nine frontier models in identical sandboxes, yielding 1,602 completed agent runs. Three blinded language-model judges scored every run against an eight-dimension rubric. On a rubric whose 8-anchor instructs judges that a domain scientist would accept the work with minor edits, no model clears the line under all three judges. gpt-5.6-sol has the highest pooled mean, 8.04, but its 95% interval [7.80, 8.23] spans the threshold, and two of the three judges rank claude-opus-5 first instead. We therefore report the ordering of systems as the reproducible quantity, the absolute level as an attribute of the instrument, and the top of the table as unresolved. Across all 39,934 scored judgments -- the eight dimension scores plus a holistic overall for each assessment, excluding not-applicable cells -- 47.6% fall below the 8-point threshold. Difficulty is not uniform across the rubric: scientific accuracy averages 6.22 against 7.33 for communication, on identical denominators and in the same direction within every one of the nine models. The single leading failure tag is overclaiming, on 31.4% of assessments. We argue that the informative quantity for scientific agents is not a leaderboard position but the joint distribution of what was delivered, what was claimed, and what artifacts were produced.

</details>


### [7] [Semantic Compression Trees: Multi-Resolution Knowledge Retrieval via Hierarchical Semantic Residuals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21610)
*Junaid Farooq*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 分层语义残差检索树，可作为coding/RAG agent的上下文检索组件，但非agent方法本身。

TL;DR: 提出语义压缩树（SCT），用层次化残差索引替代扁平分块检索；实验表明在给定文档时SCT能以更少token匹配稠密检索，且扩展性更好，但自上而下的路由选择效果差，结论是残差表示有价值而树路由不可取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有检索增强生成多依赖扁平、固定粒度的文档分块索引，忽略源文档的层次结构，导致检索效率和效果受限。

Method: 构建语义压缩树（SCT），每个节点只存储相对父节点的语义残差；查询时从根节点逐步下降检索，使每次查询开销取决于树的深度而非集合大小。

Result: 在QASPER上，给定文档时零LLM压缩的SCT与稠密检索F1相当（0.274 vs 0.277），用30%更少上下文token且无LLM构建开销；残差存储优于完整摘要（0.274 vs 0.205）；集合扩大50倍时扁平检索打分工作量增48.9倍而SCT仅增6.4倍；但文档选择场景下逐级下降明显更差（0.122 vs 0.165），正确文档选择率仅20.2%（扁平为39.3%）。

Conclusion: 残差表示值得保留，但自上而下的树路由机制不成立；后续应探索保留残差的同时采用其他文档选择或路由方式。

中文摘要：检索增强生成主要依赖扁平、固定粒度的索引：文档被切成均匀块并通过相似性检索，丢弃了源文本的层次结构。我们提出语义压缩树（SCT），一种层次化索引，其中每个节点仅存储其语义残差——即在其父节点基础上新增的信息——检索通过从根节点开始逐级下降进行，因此每次查询的成本由树的深度决定，而非集合大小。我们在 QASPER（50 篇论文，173 个问题）上、在两种仅以基准是否提供相关文档为区别的协议下进行评估，并全程使用自助置信区间和配对显著性检验。结果是混合的，我们如实报告。当给定文档时，使用零 LLM 抽取式压缩器的 SCT 在答案质量上与稠密检索相当（0.274 对 0.277 F1，p = 0.37），同时使用少 30% 的上下文 token，且构建索引不需要 LLM 调用；残差存储优于在每个节点存储完整摘要（0.274 对 0.205，p < 0.001）。将集合扩大五十倍，扁平检索的每次查询打分工作量增加 48.9 倍，而 SCT 增加 6.4 倍。逐级下降本身不受支持。当给定文档时，不使用树检索相同的残差表现相同（p = 0.27）；而当系统必须选择文档时，逐级下降明显更差（0.122 对 0.165，p < 0.001）。路由准确性定位了原因：下降方法选择正确论文的几率为 20.2%，而扁平检索为 39.3%，因为该选择是从根残差——树中压缩程度最高的节点——做出的。我们得出结论：残差表示值得保留，而自上而下的路由则不值得。

Original abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation relies mostly on flat, fixed-granularity indexes: documents are cut into uniform chunks and retrieved by similarity, discarding the hierarchical structure of the source. We introduce Semantic Compression Trees (SCT), a hierarchical index in which each node stores only its semantic residual -- the information it adds beyond its parent -- and retrieval proceeds by progressive descent from the root, so that per-query cost is governed by tree depth rather than collection size.
  We evaluate on QASPER (50 papers, 173 questions) under two protocols differing only in whether the benchmark supplies the relevant document, with bootstrap confidence intervals and paired significance tests throughout. The results are mixed and we report them as such. When the document is given, SCT with a zero-LLM extractive compressor matches dense retrieval on answer quality (0.274 vs. 0.277 F1, $p = 0.37$) using 30% fewer context tokens and no LLM calls to build the index, and residual storage beats storing full summaries at each node (0.274 vs. 0.205, $p < 0.001$). Increasing the collection fifty-fold multiplies flat retrieval's per-query scoring work by 48.9x and SCT's by 6.4x.
  Progressive descent itself is not supported. Retrieving the same residuals without the tree performs identically when the document is given ($p = 0.27$), and descent is substantially worse when the system must select the document (0.122 vs. 0.165, $p < 0.001$). Routing accuracy localises the cause: descent selects the correct paper 20.2% of the time against 39.3% for flat retrieval, because that choice is made from the root residual, the most compressed node in the tree. We conclude that the residual representation is worth keeping and top-down routing is not.

</details>


### [8] [Context as an Environment: Programmatic Context Management for Long-Horizon Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21690)
*Yin Lin,Elaine Ang,Erkang Zhu,Bolin Ding,Jingren Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 将长程agent上下文管理转化为可执行Session Environment，是通用agent记忆/上下文管理的新机制。

TL;DR: Scroll是一个上下文管理器，将会话视为可执行环境，通过持久Python内核和事件日志实现无损历史与编程式上下文管理，在多个基准上显著超越现有系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体处理长历史时，常依赖压缩或固定记忆提取，在未知未来需求前过早决定保留内容，导致信息丢失或访问效率低下。

Method: 提出的Scroll将每次会话视为可执行会话环境，结合仅追加事件日志和持久Python内核；模型通过exec代码搜索、物化、转换状态，仅显式打印投影进入工作视图，并利用逐出索引直接定位已逐出的历史区域。

Result: 以Qwen3.8-Max为骨干，Scroll在LongMemEval_S达到94.8%，在BEAM_10M达到73.1%（超过最优秀已发布记忆系统5.1个点），在LOCA_256K达到86.7%（超过最优秀已发布长视野智能体37.4个点）。

Conclusion: 将上下文管理转化为编程任务，利用LLM的编码能力并保留无损历史，能显著提升长任务智能体的性能，为处理超长会话提供了新范式。

中文摘要：LLM智能体越来越多地承担长期运行的任务，其历史记录的增长远超单个模型上下文窗口的容量。现有方法压缩早期交互或将选定的信息提取到固定记忆表示中，在未来的需求已知之前就决定了要保留什么。我们提出Scroll，一个上下文管理器，将每个智能体会话视为一个可执行的会话环境。该环境由一个仅追加的事件日志和一个沙盒化的持久Python内核支持。内核在模型调用之间维护一个类型化命名空间，允许工具输出、检索到的历史和派生状态绑定到变量，而不是在每次调用时序列化到提示中。模型编写的代码通过exec搜索、物化和转换会话状态；只有显式打印的投影才会在下次调用时进入模型的工作视图。上下文管理因此成为一个编程任务，继承了LLM不断改进的编码能力，而事件日志保留了无损的历史事实。随着工作视图接近其预算，过期的跨度被逐出但仍可恢复：逐出索引保留与精确事件日志地址关联的紧凑地标，使智能体直接导航到已逐出的区域，而不是搜索整个日志。以Qwen3.8-Max为骨干，Scroll在LongMemEval_S上达到94.8%，在BEAM_10M上达到73.1%，超过最优秀的已发布记忆系统5.1个百分点；在LOCA_256K上达到86.7%，超过最优秀的已发布长视野智能体37.4个百分点。

Original abstract: LLM agents increasingly take on long-running tasks whose history grows far beyond a single model context window. Existing approaches compress earlier interactions or extract selected information into fixed memory representations, committing to what to preserve before future needs are known. We present Scroll, a context manager that treats each agent session as an executable Session Environment. The environment is backed by an append-only Event Log and a sandboxed, persistent Python kernel. The kernel maintains a typed namespace across model calls, allowing tool outputs, retrieved history, and derived state to be bound to variables rather than serialized into the prompt at each call. Model-written code searches, materializes, and transforms session state through exec; only explicitly printed projections enter the model's working view for the next call. Context management thus becomes a programming task that inherits the improving coding abilities of LLMs, while the Event Log preserves lossless historical ground truth. As the working view approaches its budget, stale spans are evicted but remain recoverable: an eviction index keeps compact landmarks tied to exact Event Log addresses, so that the agent navigates directly to evicted regions instead of searching the full log. With Qwen3.8-Max as the backbone, Scroll achieves 94.8% on LongMemEval_S; 73.1% on BEAM_10M, surpassing the best published memory system by 5.1 points; and 86.7% on LOCA_256K, exceeding the best published long-horizon agent by 37.4 points.

</details>


### [9] [ATHENA: Knowledge-guided agentic neural architecture search for AutoFormer-based electronic health record modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21712)
*Deyi Li,Qi Xu,Lingyao Li,Tiansheng Wang,Muxuan Liang,Mei Liu*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: LLM多智能体引导NAS，含验证反馈与跨机构知识迁移，方法可迁移到agent搜索与优化。

TL;DR: 提出ATHENA，一个知识引导的智能体NAS框架，利用跨医院架构先验和权重共享超网络，在EHR建模中实现高效且稳健的Transformer架构搜索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于Transformer的EHR模型需要大量手动架构调整，且最优配置因任务和医院而异；传统NAS计算成本高，LLM引导的NAS虽减少手动设计但未跨医院复用知识，导致搜索效率低下且结果不稳定。

Method: ATHENA框架包括：1) 权重共享超网络，每医院预训练一次，候选架构作为子网络通过微调评估；2) 两层跨医院架构先验，第一层检索源站点高性能架构，第二层通过SHAP元回归估计组件影响；3) 多智能体LLM搜索结合目标医院验证反馈。

Result: 在6项临床预测任务和2个独立医疗系统中，以30搜索预算，ATHENA在12项医院-任务评估中9项达到或超过4个NAS基线，并表现出更一致的架构选择。

Conclusion: ATHENA提供了一种实用方法，可减少基于Transformer的EHR建模中手动架构调整，并跨医院复用架构知识，提升搜索效率和稳定性。

中文摘要：基于Transformer的模型广泛用于从电子健康记录（EHR）中进行临床预测，但其架构仍需大量手动调整，且最佳配置可能因任务和医院而异。神经架构搜索（NAS）可自动化架构设计，但传统方法对于基于Transformer的EHR模型计算成本高昂。近期的大语言模型（LLM）引导的NAS方法减少了手动搜索设计，但通常独立进行每次搜索，未跨医院复用架构知识。在本研究中，我们提出ATHENA（跨医院EHR神经架构搜索的智能体迁移），一个知识引导的智能体NAS框架，用于基于Transformer的EHR建模。ATHENA使用权重共享的超网络，每个医院预训练一次，允许候选架构实例化为继承的子网络，并通过微调而非独立预训练进行评估。它还结合了两层跨医院架构先验：第一层基于任务描述符从源站点检索高性能架构示例，第二层使用基于SHapley Additive exPlanations（SHAP）的元回归估计架构组件的影响。这些先验与目标医院的验证反馈一起引导多智能体LLM搜索。在六个临床预测任务和两个独立医疗系统中，ATHENA在30的搜索预算下，在12项医院-任务评估中有9项达到或超过四个NAS基线。它还在重复搜索中显示出更一致的架构选择。ATHENA为减少基于Transformer的EHR建模中手动架构调整提供了一种实用方法。代码公开于https://github.com/GatorAIM/ATHENA。

Original abstract: Transformer-based models are widely used for clinical prediction from electronic health records (EHRs), yet their architectures still require substantial manual tuning, and the optimal configuration may vary across tasks and hospitals. Neural architecture search (NAS) automates architecture design, but conventional methods are computationally costly for Transformer-based EHR models. Recent large language model (LLM)-guided NAS methods reduce manual search design but typically conduct each search independently, without reusing architecture knowledge across hospitals. In this study, we propose ATHENA (Agentic Transfer across Hospitals for EHR Neural Architecture Search), a knowledge-guided agentic NAS framework for Transformer-based EHR modeling. ATHENA uses a weight-sharing supernet that is pretrained once per hospital, allowing candidate architectures to be instantiated as inherited subnetworks and evaluated through fine-tuning rather than independent pretraining. It also incorporates a two-layer cross-hospital architecture prior. The first layer retrieves high-performing architecture examples from source sites based on task descriptors, while the second estimates the effects of architectural components using SHapley Additive exPlanations (SHAP)-based meta-regression. These priors guide a multi-agent LLM search together with validation feedback from the target hospital. Across six clinical prediction tasks and two independent health systems, ATHENA matches or outperforms four NAS baselines in 9 of 12 hospital-task evaluations at a search budget of 30. It also shows more consistent architecture selection across repeated searches. ATHENA provides a practical approach for reducing manual architecture tuning in Transformer-based EHR modeling. Code is publicly available at https://github.com/GatorAIM/ATHENA.

</details>


### [10] [ECHO: A Cognitively Inspired, Auditable Memory Plane for Long-Horizon Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21755)
*Yu Qian,Hong Miao,Boyang Guo,Tingyi Jiang,Shan Zhao,Tianxing Le,Lintian Li,Meng Liu*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 面向长程agent的认知启发可审计记忆架构，直接属于通用agent记忆设计。

TL;DR: ECHO是一个受认知过程启发的可审计记忆架构，在开发集上表现出高检索性能，但其查询扩展规则存在来源泄露，因此结果仅能作为描述性开发指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程智能体需要能够识别相关经验、解决修订并展示可核查来源的记忆。

Method: 提出ECHO记忆架构，从情节编码、巩固、上下文重激活、再巩固和执行控制中汲取功能灵感，但并非神经等效；重点在于检索与上下文构建。

Result: 在LoCoMo和LongMemEval-S开发集上分别达到96.29%/73.64%和97.60%/88.84%/88.71%的检索指标；与Mem0对比中ECHO得分较低（41.76% vs 64.84%），且事后审计发现查询扩展规则有来源特定短语，导致结果非独立确认。

Conclusion: 尽管开发测量显示高检索性能，但由于查询扩展规则的来源泄露，这些分数不能视为独立验证；需要进一步审计和改进以保证可审计性与可靠性。

中文摘要：长时程（long-horizon）智能体需要记忆能力，以识别相关经验、解决修订问题并展示可核查的来源。我们提出了ECHO（具身上下文与历史编排，Embodied Context and History Orchestration），一种受情节编码、巩固、上下文重激活、再巩固和执行控制启发的可审计记忆架构与服务原型。这是功能上的启发，而非神经等效；实证分析聚焦于检索和上下文构建。开发运行在1,536个LoCoMo类别1-4问题上达到96.29%的Hit@10和73.64%的轮次Recall@5，在所有500个LongMemEval-S问题上达到97.60%的Hit@10、88.84%的轮次Recall@5和88.71%的会话Recall@5。一个五历史的BEAM门控失败，在一个单独的匹配91问题QA样本中，Mem0 OSS得分64.84%，而ECHO为41.76%（精确McNemar p = 0.00107），历史聚类区间跨越零。事后审计发现查询扩展规则中存在特定于来源的短语。尽管运行时未进入金牌答案字段，扩展启用的检索分数因此是描述性开发测量，而非独立确认。

Original abstract: Long-horizon agents need memory that identifies relevant experience, resolves revisions, and exposes checkable provenance. We present ECHO (Embodied Context and History Orchestration), an auditable memory architecture and service prototype inspired by episodic encoding, consolidation, contextual reinstatement, reconsolidation, and executive control. This is functional inspiration, not neural equivalence; the empirical analysis focuses on retrieval and context construction. Development runs reach 96.29% Hit@10 and 73.64% turn Recall@5 on 1,536 LoCoMo category 1-4 questions, and 97.60% Hit@10, 88.84% turn Recall@5, and 88.71% session Recall@5 on all 500 LongMemEval-S questions. A five-history BEAM gate fails, and in a separate matched 91-question QA sample Mem0 OSS scores 64.84% versus ECHO's 41.76% (exact McNemar p = 0.00107), with a history-cluster interval crossing zero. A post-hoc audit found source-specific phrases in the query-expansion rules. Although no gold answer field entered the runtime, expansion-enabled retrieval scores are therefore descriptive development measurements, not independent confirmation.

</details>


### [11] [Composable Trust Infrastructure for Manufacturing Knowledge Graphs: Cross-System Provenance, Temporal Reasoning, and Decision Traceability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21418)
*Grama Chethan*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: cs.SE 论文按全量策略收录。

TL;DR: 提出一种可组合的信任基础设施，通过共享标识符整合SHACL验证、PROV-O溯源、双时态版本控制和图原生决策对象四种能力，使制造业知识图谱获得单一能力无法提供的涌现信任属性；消融实验表明四种能力同等关键，全链路可审计性为不可约的四维属性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 制造业知识图谱整合异构工业系统数据时存在信任赤字，消费者无法验证数据的有效性、来源、决策时状态及后续处理，单一信任能力不足以解决该问题。

Method: 设计可组合的信任基础设施，将SHACL验证、PROV-O溯源、领域感知双时态版本控制和图原生决策对象集成到统一RDF架构，通过共享实体URI、摄取活动标识符和时态关联键实现能力组合，支持跨四维度的复合查询，并在11个工业来源的测试平台上进行验证。

Result: 消融实验显示移除任一能力恰好导致6个组合查询中的3个失败；高阶组合分析发现4个三方属性和1个不可约的四方属性（全链路可审计性，31ms）；统一图包含8,743条三元组、5个命名图和81条owl:sameAs边，在89类ISA-95对齐本体下验证。

Conclusion: 四种信任能力通过共享关联标识符组合后产生涌现信任属性，且每种能力都同等关键；该基础设施在模拟但结构真实的数据上验证有效，具备支持工业实际部署的潜力。

中文摘要：整合来自异构工业系统的数据的制造业知识图谱面临信任赤字：消费者无法确定查询到的数据是否有效、在做出决策时是否有效、数据源自何处，或者数据是如何被处理的。我们认为，四种信任能力——SHACL验证、PROV-O溯源、领域感知的双时态版本控制，以及图原生的决策对象——通过共享的关联标识符进行组合，产生任何单一能力都无法单独提供的涌现信任属性。我们提出了一种可组合的信任基础设施，将这四种能力整合到一个统一的RDF架构中。这些能力通过共享的实体URI、摄取活动标识符和时态关联键进行组合，支持跨越所有四个维度的复合查询。一项实验性消融研究证实，移除任何单一能力都会恰好导致六个组合查询中的三个失败，这表明四种能力同等重要。对高阶组合的分析揭示了四种涌现的三方属性和一种不可约的四方属性（全链路可审计性，31毫秒执行时间）。该基础设施在一个集成十一个工业来源的测试平台上得到验证——OPC UA、TIA Portal、eClass、AAS、ISA-95、ISA-18.2、SAP S/4HANA、Teamcenter、Opcenter EX、Insights Hub和SCM——采用一个包含89个类、与ISA-95对齐的本体。统一图包含跨越五个命名图的8,743条三元组，由81条owl:sameAs身份边缝合。评估使用来自专用模拟器的模拟但结构真实的数据；数据结构和跨系统链接模式代表了真实的工业安装。

Original abstract: Manufacturing knowledge graphs that integrate data from heterogeneous industrial systems face a trust deficit: consumers cannot determine whether queried data is valid, whether it was valid when a decision was made, where it originated, or how it was acted upon. We argue that four trust capabilities -- SHACL validation, PROV-O provenance, domain-aware bi-temporal versioning, and graph-native decision objects -- compose through shared correlation identifiers to produce emergent trust properties that no single capability delivers alone. We present a composable trust infrastructure that integrates these four capabilities into a unified RDF architecture. Capabilities compose through shared entity URIs, ingestion activity identifiers, and temporal correlation keys, enabling compound queries spanning all four dimensions. An experimental ablation confirms that removing any single capability causes exactly three of six composition queries to fail, demonstrating that all four are equally load-bearing. Analysis of higher-order compositions reveals four emergent three-way properties and one irreducible four-way property (full-chain auditability, 31ms execution). The infrastructure is validated on a testbed integrating eleven industrial sources -- OPC UA, TIA Portal, eClass, AAS, ISA-95, ISA-18.2, SAP S/4HANA, Teamcenter, Opcenter EX, Insights Hub, and SCM -- under an 89-class ISA-95-aligned ontology. The unified graph contains 8,743 triples across five named graphs, stitched by 81 owl:sameAs identity edges. Evaluation uses simulated but structurally realistic data from purpose-built emulators; data structures and cross-system linkage patterns are representative of real industrial installations.

</details>


### [12] [Repo2Skill-Evo: Repository Skills Go Stale in Silence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21964)
*Chenyuan Duan,Ge Shi,Zineng Mao,Ge Zhang,Hao Liang,Yinzhu Piao,Yuchen Wu,Zhixin Yao,Kaiyu Huang,Wenhao Huang,Linzhuang Sun,Shen Yan,Wentao Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: cs.SE 论文按全量策略收录。

TL;DR: 提出Repo2Skill-Evo评估智能体维护仓库技能的能力，发现前沿智能体在版本更新后维持技能时效性表现不佳，技能会无声过时。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体依赖仓库特定的程序性知识，技能外部化可提升性能但其持久性未知；版本特异性使技能容易过时且无显式信号，因此需要研究智能体能否维持外部化知识的时效性。

Method: Repo2Skill-Evo将版本转换视为技能维护任务：给定V1技能集和官方V1→V2补丁，要求智能体更新过时内容并保留仍有效的指导，并通过基于补丁的移除指标评估。

Result: 在57个仓库和105个版本转换上，所有转换都使部分V1技能失效；六个前沿智能体avg@3宏F1仅29.9%-69.7%；主要错误包括受影响文件覆盖不全（漏更新）和过度编辑（高召回低精确）。

Conclusion: 仓库技能会在无声中过时，即使前沿智能体也无法可靠维护；需要更好的技能维护机制。

中文摘要：大语言模型（LLM）智能体越来越多地操作于不断演进的软件仓库中，其成功取决于仓库特定的程序性知识：调用哪些API、运行哪些脚本，以及当前版本期望的约定。智能体技能将这些知识外部化为可复用的单元，先前工作表明它们可以提升智能体性能。尚不清楚的是这种提升是否持久。使技能有用的同一版本特异性也使其变得脆弱：在版本发布后，技能可能在没有明确信号的情况下变得过时，同时继续提供过时的指导。因此，将知识外部化为技能可能使其衰减不可见。我们研究智能体能否保持这些外部化知识的时效性。Repo2Skill-Evo将每次版本转换视为一项技能维护任务：给定V1技能集和官方V1到V2补丁，智能体必须更新过时的技能内容，同时保留仍然有效的指导。在57个真实世界仓库和105个选定的版本转换中，每个评估的转换都会使V1技能集的一部分失效。然而，六个前沿智能体在一种基于补丁的移除指标下仅达到29.9%-69.7%的avg@3宏F1，该指标平衡了过时内容召回率与过度编辑精确率。在多次运行中，两种对立的错误占主导：技能集中受影响文件的不完全覆盖使过时内容未被触及，而过度宽泛的编辑与更高的召回率但更低的精确率相关。仓库技能在无声中过时，即使是前沿智能体也无法可靠地维护它们。

Original abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents increasingly operate over evolving software repositories, where success depends on repository-specific procedural knowledge: which APIs to call, which scripts to run, and which conventions the current release expects. Agent skills externalize this knowledge into reusable units, and prior work shows that they can improve agent performance. What remains unclear is whether that improvement is durable. The same version specificity that makes a skill useful also makes it fragile: after a release, it may become stale without raising any explicit signal, while continuing to provide obsolete guidance. Externalizing knowledge into a skill can therefore make its decay invisible.
  We study whether agents can keep this externalized knowledge current. Repo2Skill-Evo casts each release transition as a skill-maintenance task: given a V1 skill set and the official V1-to-V2 patch, an agent must update obsolete skill content while preserving guidance that remains valid. Across 57 real-world repositories and 105 selected release transitions, every evaluated transition invalidates part of the V1 skill set. Yet six frontier agents reach only 29.9%-69.7% avg@3 macro F1 under a patch-grounded removal metric that balances stale-content recall against over-editing precision. Across runs, two opposing errors dominate: incomplete coverage of affected files in the skill set leaves stale content untouched, while overbroad editing is associated with higher recall but lower precision. Repository skills go stale in silence, and even frontier agents cannot reliably maintain them.

</details>


### [13] [Disagree to Explore, Agree to Commit: Routing-Guided Test-Time Scaling for Software Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22191)
*Kang Chen,Junjie Nian,Yixin Cao,Yugang Jiang*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: cs.SE 论文按全量策略收录。

TL;DR: 提出Risa，利用MoE路由器轨迹的引导和仲裁能力，无需外部评判或测试执行，在SWE-bench Verified上显著提升软件工程代理的解决率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件工程代理在仓库级任务中依赖长随机工具使用轨迹，重复尝试虽可提高成功率，但测试时扩展困难：补丁缺乏规范答案形式，且共享前缀的兄弟动作相关。现有方法需要外部评判或执行测试，成本高。因此需要一种无需外部信号的引导与选择机制。

Method: 分析原生MoE路由器轨迹，发现其能提供稳健的行为角色信号。提出Risa框架：轨迹内，利用路由鼓励多样化探索并在补丁提交阶段控制收敛；轨迹间，在信息丰富的补丁位置通过协议选择最终候选。无需外部评判或选择时测试执行。

Result: 在SWE-bench Verified上，对gpt-oss系列（不同规模与推理努力）均匀采样基线宏平均解决率44.9%，Risa仲裁提升至48.2%，匹配文本共识方法且无需答案字符串匹配；在Qwen3.6上迁移有效，全500任务基准优于均匀选择并匹配文本共识。

Conclusion: Risa证明原生MoE路由轨迹可作为有效的测试时引导与仲裁信号，替代昂贵的外部评判或测试执行，提升软件工程代理的仓库级任务解决性能，并跨模型泛化。

中文摘要：软件工程代理通过长时间、随机的工具使用轨迹来解决仓库级任务，并且重复尝试往往能发现单次运行遗漏的修复。测试时扩展很困难，因为补丁缺乏规范的答案形式，而来自共享前缀的兄弟动作是相关的。我们研究了原生MoE路由器轨迹是否可以在没有外部评判者或选择时测试执行的情况下指导引导和选择。我们的分析表明，路由提供了稳健的行为角色信号；令牌级读出和决策匹配的比较集将其转化为有效的控制。因此，我们引入了Risa（路由信息引导和仲裁）：在轨迹内，路由鼓励多样化探索并在补丁提交期间控制收敛；在跨独立采样的轨迹中，在信息丰富的补丁位置上的协议选择最终候选者。我们使用不同规模和推理努力设置下的开放权重稀疏MoE代理在SWE-bench Verified上进行了评估。Risa的路由仲裁将gpt-oss系列在均匀采样下的宏平均解决率从44.9%提高到48.2%，匹配了文本共识而无需答案字符串匹配，并且它迁移到Qwen3.6，在完整的500任务基准上优于均匀选择并匹配文本共识。

Original abstract: Software-engineering agents solve repository-level tasks through long, stochastic tool-use trajectories, and repeated attempts often find fixes missed by one run. Test-time scaling is difficult because patches lack canonical answer forms, while sibling actions from a shared prefix are correlated. We study whether native MoE router traces can guide steering and selection without an external judge or selection-time test execution. Our analysis shows that routing provides a robust behavioral role signal; token-granular readouts and decision-matched comparison sets turn it into effective control. We therefore introduce Risa (Routing-Informed Steering and Arbitration): within trajectories, routing encourages diverse exploration and controlled convergence during patch commitment; across separately sampled trajectories, agreement at informative patch positions selects a final candidate. We evaluate on SWE-bench Verified using open-weight sparse MoE agents across scales and reasoning-effort settings. Risa's routing arbitration raises the macro-average resolved rate from 44.9% under uniform sampling to 48.2% on the gpt-oss family, matching text consensus without answer-string matching, and it transfers to Qwen3.6, where it improves on uniform choice and matches text consensus on the full 500-task benchmark.

</details>


### [14] [HIRA: A Human-in-the-Loop Retrieval-Augmented Cascade for Document Classification in Regulated Industries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21792)
*Shangxuan Tian,Yanhui Chen,Carlos Queiroz*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: CIKM 2026

Selection reason: 检索+LLM验证器+人工反馈+记忆更新的级联流程，选择性反馈对agent记忆设计有启发。

TL;DR: HIRA是一种免训练的本地检索增强级联方法，通过选择性人工反馈和检索记忆自适应，在受监管文档分类中显著提升性能，减少LLM调用和人工审核。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受监管行业的文档分类面临数据驻留、冷启动标签少、审核能力有限和模型治理成本高等约束，传统模型重训练方法不切实际，需要一种无需训练且能持续改进的替代方案。

Method: HIRA结合BM25、密集文本嵌入和图像级表示，通过验证校准的加权倒数秩融合进行检索；置信度低的文档传给本地LLM验证器，利用OCR文本、检索示例、标签描述和混淆词；仍不确定则转人工审核，并将更正存储为检索示例和混淆图更新。

Result: 在30,233篇贸易融资语料上，仅需6.4%人工审核将Macro-F1从0.6218提至0.8548；在Tobacco-3482上达到0.9423 Macro-F1，超过零样本基线17.4个百分点，减少约60% LLM调用；少量人工更正即可匹配完全标记的预言机。

Conclusion: 选择性人工反馈和检索记忆自适应是受监管部署中长尾文档分类的有效实用方案，可替代重复模型重训练，兼顾性能与合规需求。

中文摘要：受监管行业中的文档分类受到数据驻留、有限的冷启动标签、稀缺的审核能力以及昂贵的模型治理程序的约束。我们提出了HIRA，一种用于受监管部署中文档分类的免训练、本地检索增强级联方法，它通过验证校准的加权倒数秩融合，将基于OCR文本的BM25、密集文本嵌入和图像级表示相结合。置信度高的文档直接通过检索分类；不确定或视觉上易混淆的文档则传递给本地托管的LLM验证器，该验证器接收OCR文本、检索到的示例、标签描述和混淆特定术语。当验证器仍然不确定时，文档被发送给人工审核。每次更正都作为边际加权检索示例存储，并更新经过Dirichlet平滑的混淆图，使系统无需更新模型权重即可改进。在私有的80类贸易融资语料库上，HIRA处理了完整的30,233篇文档生产流，同时仅请求对1,945篇文档（6.4%）进行人工更正，将Macro-F1从0.6218提高到0.8548。在修正后的Tobacco-3482基准上，HIRA使用本地托管的DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-32B验证器达到0.9423的Macro-F1，比零样本LLM基线高出17.4个百分点，同时仅对约40%的文档调用验证器，并将LLM调用减少了约60%。凭借518次人工更正（占池的24.8%），HIRA匹配了完全标记的池预言机，其中所有2,086篇池文档都使用其真实标签进行索引。这些结果表明，选择性人工反馈和检索记忆自适应可以成为受监管部署中长尾文档分类中重复模型再训练的实际替代方案。

Original abstract: Document classification in regulated industries is constrained by data residency, limited cold-start labels, scarce review capacity, and costly model-governance procedures. We present HIRA, a training-free, on-premises retrieval-augmented cascade for document classification in regulated deployments that combines BM25 over OCR text, dense text embeddings, and image-level representations through validation-calibrated weighted reciprocal-rank fusion. Confident documents are classified directly by retrieval; uncertain or visually confusable documents are passed to a locally hosted LLM verifier, which receives the OCR text, retrieved exemplars, label descriptions, and confusion-specific terms. When the verifier remains uncertain, the document is sent to human review. Each correction is stored as a margin-weighted retrieval exemplar and updates a Dirichlet-smoothed confusion graph, letting the system improve without updating model weights.
  On a private 80-class trade-finance corpus, HIRA processes the full 30,233-document production stream while requesting human correction for only 1,945 documents (6.4%), improving Macro-F1 from 0.6218 to 0.8548. On the corrected Tobacco-3482 benchmark, HIRA reaches 0.9423 Macro-F1 with a locally hosted DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-32B verifier, 17.4 percentage points above the zero-shot LLM baseline, while invoking the verifier for only about 40% of documents and reducing LLM calls by approximately 60%. With 518 human corrections (24.8% of the pool), HIRA matches the fully labelled pool oracle, in which all 2,086 pool documents are indexed with their ground-truth labels. These results show that selective human feedback and retrieval-memory adaptation can be a practical alternative to repeated model retraining for long-tail document classification in regulated deployments.

</details>


### [15] [Concepts for Securing Agentic AI Coding and the Terok Environment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22930)
*Jiří Vyskočil,Franz Pöschel,Andreas Knüpfer*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: to be published in the proceedings of the AGENSYS workshop (Workshop on Knowledge Discovery, Maintenance and Distributed Intelligence in Multi-Agent Systems) at ECML PKDD 2026 conference in Sept. 2026

Selection reason: cs.SE 论文按全量策略收录。

TL;DR: 本文评估了代理式AI在软件开发中的IT安全风险，提出了缓解概念并展示了其实现，旨在实现负责任的采用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代理式AI为软件开发带来巨大潜力，但引入了严重且被加剧的IT安全风险；在积极探索的同时必须主动应对这些风险，以支持安全且负责任的使用。

Method: 提出三部分内容：安全风险评估、不破坏收益的缓解概念、以及该概念的实现概述。

Result: 提供了一套概念及其实现，虽非最终答案，但代表了负责任使用代理式AI的实质性进展，使社区能够评估其潜力而免受安全风险。

Conclusion: 这项工作是对代理式AI软件开发安全问题的及时贡献，为在实际风险下安全利用其收益提供了基础，呼吁社区进一步评估和完善。

中文摘要：代理式人工智能是软件开发领域一种引人入胜的新工具。与“传统”的人工智能辅助编码相比，这是一个巨大的进步，而后者此前也已是一项重大突破。通过大语言模型提供的人工智能支持是一个年轻且发展极为迅速的领域。“传统”（非代理式）形式在2025年初（约18个月前）变得有用且高效，代理式形式则在2025年秋季（约9个月前）紧随其后。除了其诸多益处和潜力之外，它还给IT安全带来了一些根本性风险。代理式方法在使其他风险变得更加危险的同时，也增加了非常严重的风险。在探索这一迷人新工具的动力驱使下，我们不应忽视风险，而应积极应对。我们提出了（I）对IT安全风险的评估，（II）一个在不破坏其益处的前提下缓解这些风险的概念，以及（III）关于我们概念实现情况的概述。在这个高度动态的领域中，这可能不是对所识别问题的最终且一劳永逸的答案，但仍是在负责任地使用代理式人工智能进行软件开发方面迈出的坚实一步。它还应为社区做出贡献，使人们能够尽早且急切地评估代理式人工智能在软件开发中的潜力，而实际上不会承受其隐含的IT安全风险。

Original abstract: Agentic AI is a fascinating new tool for software development. It is a huge step forward compared to "conventional" AI assisted coding, which in turn was a considerable breakthrough earlier. AI support through LLMs is a young and very fast-moving field. The "conventional" (non-agentic) flavor became useful and productive in early 2025 (around 18 months ago) and the agentic flavor followed in fall 2025 (approximately 9 months ago). Besides all its benefits and potential, it also carries some fundamental risks for IT security. And the agentic approach added very severe risks while making others much more dangerous.
  With all the motivation to explore this fascinating new tool we should not ignore the risks but actively address them. We present (I) an assessment of the IT security risks, (II) a concept for mitigating them without breaking its benefits, and (III) an overview about an implementation of our concept. In this very dynamic field this is likely not the final and once-and-for-all answer to the identified issues but still a substantial step forward in responsible usage of Agentic AI for software development. It should also be a contribution to the community to allow early and eager evaluation of the potential of agentic AI for software development without actually suffering from its implied IT security risks.

</details>


### [16] [Hints, Critics, and Teachers: Prior Injection for Sparse-Reward RL in Vision-Language Math Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21811)
*Qiqian Fu*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 稀疏奖励RL中注入hint/teacher/critic的机制及评测陷阱，可迁移到coding agent的RL训练。

TL;DR: 在稀疏奖励下，视觉-语言数学推理的强化学习因零梯度而失效；只有先验被有效传递的方法才能提升性能；域内评估切片与跨域迁移存在反转，最难的切片能准确预测跨域性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉-语言数学推理中的强化学习面临稀疏奖励问题，大部分GRPO rollout组完全错误且贡献零梯度，导致学习过程“饿死”。作者旨在探究注入不同先验（文本、分布、值）是否能缓解该问题，并揭示评估方式对方法排名的误导性影响。

Method: 在20,830道视觉数学题和Qwen2-VL-2B模型上，于相同条件下训练11种方法，分别注入文本提示、7B教师模型的在线蒸馏，以及使用MSE或HL-Gauss损失的值评论家先验。通过池化域内指标和跨域DynaMath评估方法性能，并计算各域内切片与跨域迁移的Spearman相关。

Result: 六个先验有效传递的试验臂与其余五个（无先验或先验传递失败）在域内和跨域上完全分离；域内一个常用切片与跨域迁移呈负相关（rho=-0.74），而最难的域内切片则高度正相关（rho=+0.89）；提示引导的探索而非UFT辅助损失带来提示增益，HL-Gauss损失相比MSE在域内提升14.4分。

Conclusion: 先验的传递效率决定了强化学习在稀疏奖励下是否有效；评估时需谨慎选择域内切片，接近随机的多项选择子集会严重扭曲对方法真实跨域能力的判断。HL-Gauss损失和提示引导探索是有效的改进方向。

中文摘要：强化学习用于视觉-语言数学推理在稀疏奖励下会“饿死”：在20,830道视觉数学题中，Qwen2-VL-2B只有3.6%的rollout正确，85-97%的GRPO rollout组完全错误且贡献零梯度。我们在相同条件下训练了十一种方法，每种方法注入不同的先验：文本（参考解答提示）、分布（来自7B教师模型的在线蒸馏）和值（带有MSE或HL-Gauss类别损失的预训练值评论家）。先验恰好在其被传递时起作用：六个先验有效到达策略的试验臂与其余五个（无先验基线以及四个先验被教师截断、门控移除或丢失于错误参数化评论家的试验臂）在池化域内指标和跨域迁移（DynaMath）上均无重叠地分离。然而，核心发现涉及评估：域内池的一个切片——长期用作该项目的一般分布检查——与真正的跨域迁移呈负相关（Spearman rho = -0.74, n = 11个试验臂，置换p = 0.011），而最难的域内切片则紧密预测它（rho = +0.89, p < 0.001）。我们将这种反转归因于一个接近随机的多项选择子集，它奖励那些“未改变”的模型；透过它来看，最佳跨域方法显得平庸，而最差的方法却像冠军。在这些方法中，提示引导的探索——而非UFT的辅助损失——驱动了提示增益，而用HL-Gauss交叉熵替换评论家的MSE损失在域内价值+14.4分。所有准确率均经盲评，并采用配对精确检验。

Original abstract: Reinforcement learning for vision-language math reasoning starves under sparse reward: on a pool of 20,830 visual-math problems where Qwen2-VL-2B answers 3.6% of rollouts correctly, 85-97% of GRPO rollout groups are entirely wrong and contribute zero gradient. We train eleven methods under identical conditions in this regime, each injecting a different prior: text (reference-solution hints), distribution (on-policy distillation from a 7B teacher), and value (a value-pretrained critic with an MSE or HL-Gauss categorical loss). A prior helps exactly when it is delivered: the six arms whose prior effectively reaches the policy separate with no overlap from the remaining five -- the no-prior baseline and four arms whose prior is teacher-capped, gated away, or lost to a mis-parameterized critic -- both on the pooled in-domain metric and on cross-domain transfer (DynaMath). The central finding, however, concerns evaluation: one slice of the in-domain pool -- long used as this project's general-distribution check -- anti-correlates with genuine cross-domain transfer (Spearman rho = -0.74, n = 11 arms, permutation p = 0.011), while the hardest in-domain slice predicts it closely (rho = +0.89, p < 0.001). We attribute the inversion to a near-chance multiple-choice subset that rewards models for not having changed; read through it, the best cross-domain method looked mediocre and the worst looked like the champion. Among the methods, hint-guided exploration -- not UFT's auxiliary loss -- drives hint gains, and replacing the critic's MSE loss with HL-Gauss cross-entropy is worth +14.4 points in-domain. All accuracies are blind-judged, with paired exact tests.

</details>


### [17] [PatchWrite: One Line, Not One Section -- Compile-Gated, Validity-Preserving Editing for AI-Drafted Manuscripts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23001)
*Weiwei Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 12 pages, 7 figures

Selection reason: cs.SE 论文按全量策略收录。

TL;DR: PatchWrite通过证据锁和编译门限限制编辑提交，相比整槽重写，在24稿件×8故障测试中完全保留无关内容（192/192 vs 0/192），且生成编辑的接受率为75%，修复率93.75%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动稿件流水线在修复局部缺陷时常重新生成整个章节，导致无关指标和引用意外变化，即使PDF构建成功。需要一种机制来确保编辑只改变目标内容，不引入幻觉或无关修改。

Method: PatchWrite复用有界EDIT N M编辑和回滚，但添加两个门限：致命日志检查（编译接受）和证据锁（每个引用键和实验数值必须由参考注册表或实验日志证明）。失败的候选被拒绝并保留原HEAD。

Result: 在Oracle压力测试中，PatchWrite在192/192例中保留无关行，而整槽重写为0/192。移除编译门限接受率降至0，移除证据门限允许幻觉引用。生成编辑任务中，75%候选被接受，93.75%修复故障；盲评中PatchWrite在保留事实方面得分5.0 vs 2.0，散文质量评分相近。

Conclusion: PatchWrite通过证据和编译约束，能有效防止无关内容变异和幻觉引用，同时保持较高的修复成功率，为自动稿件编辑提供了更可靠的提交协议。

中文摘要：自动稿件流水线通常为了修复局部缺陷而重新生成整个章节，即使生成的PDF仍能构建，也可能导致无关的指标和引用发生变化。PatchWrite则限制候选编辑如何成为已提交的稿件状态：它复用有界的EDIT N M编辑和回滚，但通过致命日志检查收紧编译接受标准，并增加证据锁，要求每个被引用的键和实验数值标记必须得到参考注册表或实验日志的证明。未通过任一检查的候选将被拒绝，并保留之前的HEAD。在24篇稿件×8种故障的Oracle压力测试（768个任务，平均分为编译破坏型和内容-only故障）中，整槽重写在每种情况下都使无关的“12层”行发生变异（0/192保留；数值Jaccard 0.6667），而PatchWrite在192/192的情况下保留了它。移除编译门限使接受率降至0，而移除证据门限则允许幻觉引用通过。相同的模式在所有八种故障中均成立。为了测试该协议在生成而非Oracle编辑下的表现，我们使用写作模型提出编辑，重新运行了192个任务。模型的候选在75%的情况下被接受；几乎所有拒绝都来自一种可复现的失败模式，即模型尝试使用当前语法不支持的空替换来删除一行。每个被接受的候选均通过了两个门限，并且93.75%修复了注入的故障；其余情况涉及一个技术上有效但句子不当的引用和一个改变标记的近似失败。在十六对PDF的盲评中，两位评分者均更偏好PatchWrite在保留实验室基础事实方面的表现（C1 Likert 5.0 vs. 2.0），而对散文质量的评分几乎相同。来自193个产品内起草任务的日志显示，同类失败在实践中也会发生。

Original abstract: Automated manuscript pipelines often regenerate an entire section to repair a local defect, allowing unrelated metrics and citations to change even when the resulting PDF still builds. PatchWrite instead constrains how candidate edits become committed manuscript states: it reuses bounded EDIT N M editing and rollback, but tightens compilation acceptance with fatal-log checks and adds evidence locks that require every cited key and experimental numeric token to be attested by a reference registry or experimental log. Candidates that fail either check are rejected and the previous HEAD is retained. On a 24-manuscript x 8-fault oracle stress test (768 jobs, evenly split between compile-breaking and content-only faults), whole-slot rewriting mutated an unrelated "12-layer" line in every case (0/192 preserved; numeric Jaccard 0.6667), whereas PatchWrite preserved it in 192/192 cases. Removing the compile gate reduced acceptance to 0, while removing the evidence gate allowed a hallucinated citation to pass. The same pattern held across all eight faults. To test the protocol with generation rather than oracle edits, we reran the 192 jobs with the writer model proposing the edits. The model's candidates were accepted in 75% of cases; nearly all rejections came from one reproducible failure mode in which the model attempted to delete a line using an empty replacement unsupported by the current grammar. Every accepted candidate passed both gates, and 93.75% fixed the injected fault; the remaining cases involved a technically valid but sentence-inappropriate citation and one markup-changing near-miss. In a blind evaluation of sixteen PDF pairs, both raters preferred PatchWrite for preserving lab-grounded facts (C1 Likert 5.0 vs. 2.0), while rating prose quality nearly identically. Logs from 193 in-product drafting tasks show the same classes of failures occurring in practice.

</details>


### [18] [AutoSaddler: Automatic Harness Optimization with Durable Updates from Agent Execution Traces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23041)
*Sungho Park,Wonjoong Kim,Rongyuan Tan,Jue Zhang,Wook-Shin Han,Pengfei Gao,Chanyoung Park,Yongqiang Yao,Rao Fu,Elsie Nallipogu,Qingwei Lin,Saravan Rajmohan,Dongmei Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 44 pages, 15 figures. Project website and code: https://aka.ms/AutoSaddler-website

Selection reason: cs.SE 论文按全量策略收录。

TL;DR: AutoSaddler通过将harness优化视为离线学习问题，利用失败轨迹诊断和结构化补丁生成，在多个基准上显著提升了LLM智能体的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时段任务中LLM智能体的小错误会累积导致失败，而手动设计外部harness成本高且需要搜索大量配置空间，因此需要自动化的harness优化方法。

Method: 提出AutoSaddler框架，将harness改进形式化为离线学习问题，通过从小批量失败信号中迭代更新；结合失败轨迹诊断、将harness作为代码进行结构化补丁生成，以及基于验证的更新选择。

Result: 在GAIA2、SWE-Bench Pro和Terminal-Bench 2.0上相对于基础harness分别取得9.0、9.6和10.0个百分点的性能提升；消融实验表明深度调试、针对性修改和泛化感知选择是关键因素。

Conclusion: 自动harness优化是提升智能体系统性能和可靠性的有效且有前景的途径。

中文摘要：LLM智能体在长时段任务中仍然不可靠，其中小的局部失败会在长时间交互中累积，并导致整体任务失败。尽管外部harness可以大幅提升鲁棒性，但harness设计仍然是一个手动且昂贵的过程，需要在巨大的提示词（prompts）、工具配置和控制逻辑空间中进行搜索。我们提出了AutoSaddler，一种自动harness优化框架，将harness改进形式化为离线学习问题，并利用小批量的失败信号迭代地更新harness。AutoSaddler结合了失败轨迹诊断、将harness视为代码的结构化补丁生成，以及基于验证的更新选择。在GAIA2、SWE-Bench Pro和Terminal-Bench 2.0上的实验表明，AutoSaddler在相应基础harness上大幅提升了智能体性能，分别取得了9.0、9.6和10.0个百分点的增益。消融研究进一步表明，有效的harness优化得益于三个要素：深度调试而非浅层反思，针对性修改而非无约束编辑，以及泛化感知选择而非轨迹特定修复。总之，这些结果表明，自动harness优化是迈向更高性能和更可靠智能体系统的一条有前景的路径。

Original abstract: LLM agents remain unreliable on long-horizon tasks, where small local failures can compound over extended interactions and lead to overall task failure. Although external harnesses can substantially improve robustness, harness design remains a manual and expensive process that requires searching over a large space of prompts, tool configurations, and control logic. We propose AutoSaddler, an automatic harness optimization framework that formulates harness improvement as an offline learning problem and iteratively updates the harness using failure signals from mini-batches. AutoSaddler combines failure-trace diagnosis, structured patch generation that treats the harness as code, and validation-based update selection. Experiments on GAIA2, SWE-Bench Pro, and Terminal-Bench 2.0 show that AutoSaddler substantially improves agent performance over the corresponding base harnesses, achieving gains of 9.0, 9.6, and 10.0 percentage points, respectively. Ablation studies further suggest that effective harness optimization benefits from three ingredients: deep debugging rather than shallow reflection, targeted modifications rather than unconstrained editing, and generalization-aware selection rather than trajectory-specific repair. Together, these results suggest that automatic harness optimization is a promising path toward more performant and reliable agent systems.

</details>


### [19] [Beyond Success and Failure: Length-Aware Contrastive Learning for GUI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21830)
*Chengyang Gu,Le Zhang,Jingbo Zhou,Yize Chen,Yu Shi,Siqi Bao,Zheng-Fan Wu,Hua Wu,Hui Xiong*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: GUI agent的轨迹级对比RL训练方法，对通用agent训练有直接启发。

TL;DR: 提出LACL-GUI，一种结合轨迹级质量信号的对比RLVR框架，用于GUI智能体训练，通过结构化偏好改进优化稳定性与性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对比式RLVR方法主要依赖结果级监督，忽略同一结果类别内轨迹质量的细粒度差异，导致学习信号不够精确。

Method: LACL-GUI在成功和失败轨迹中引入结构化偏好，鼓励简洁成功执行，并按与成功轨迹的偏离程度区分失败质量，同时保持优化稳定性。

Result: 在GUI智能体基准上，LACL-GUI提供更有效的学习信号，持续优于先前方法，提升智能体性能。

Conclusion: 轨迹级监督在对比式RLVR中具有重要价值，LACL-GUI验证了其有效性。

中文摘要：由多模态大语言模型（MLLM）驱动的图形用户界面（GUI）智能体在跨多种数字环境自动化任务方面展现出强大潜力，其中强化学习（RL）已成为主导的训练范式。然而，广泛使用的方法如组相对策略优化（GRPO）存在奖励梯度错位问题，导致优化低效且不稳定。近期工作通过将可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）重新表述为对比式或分类式目标来解决该问题，通过消除有问题的梯度行为来提高稳定性。尽管取得了这些进展，现有的对比式RLVR方法主要依赖结果级监督，未能捕捉同一结果类别内轨迹质量的细粒度差异。在本文中，我们提出面向GUI智能体的长度感知对比学习（LACL-GUI），这是一种将轨迹级质量信号纳入策略优化的对比式RLVR框架。LACL-GUI在成功和失败的轨迹中都引入了结构化偏好，鼓励简洁的成功执行，并根据与成功轨迹的偏离程度区分失败质量，同时保持优化稳定性。在GUI智能体基准上的实验表明，LACL-GUI提供了更有效的学习信号，并持续优于先前方法提升智能体性能，突显了轨迹级监督在对比式RLVR中的价值。

Original abstract: Graphical User Interface (GUI) agents powered by Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have shown strong potential for automating tasks across diverse digital environments, where reinforcement learning (RL) has become a dominant training paradigm. However, widely used methods such as Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) suffer from reward-gradient misalignment, leading to inefficient and unstable optimization. Recent work addresses this issue by reformulating RL with verifiable rewards (RLVR) as contrastive or classification-based objectives, which improve stability by eliminating problematic gradient behaviors. Despite this progress, existing contrastive RLVR methods rely primarily on outcome-level supervision and fail to capture fine-grained differences in trajectory quality within the same outcome category. In this paper, we propose Length-Aware Contrastive Learning for GUI Agents (LACL-GUI), a contrastive RLVR framework that incorporates trajectory-level quality signals into policy optimization. LACL-GUI introduces structured preferences within both successful and failed trajectories, encouraging concise successful executions and differentiating failure quality based on divergence from successful trajectories, while preserving optimization stability. Experiments on GUI agent benchmarks show that LACL-GUI provides more effective learning signals and consistently improves agent performance over prior methods, highlighting the value of trajectory-level supervision in contrastive RLVR.

</details>


### [20] [Prime Agent: A Self-Improving RLM Harness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23552)
*Seth Karten,Alex L. Zhang,Kevin Thomas,Sebastian Müller,Elie Bakouch,Daniel Auras,Mika Senghaas,Fares Obeid,Konstantin Dunas,Johannes Hagemann,Sami Jaghouar*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 16 pages, 10 figures. Technical report. Code: https://github.com/PrimeIntellect-ai/prime-agent

Selection reason: cs.SE 论文按全量策略收录。

TL;DR: Prime Agent 是一个开源框架，通过持久 REPL、递归子代理和持续记忆管理，显著提升长视野代理性能，在 ARC-AGI-3 等任务上大幅超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型顺序处理能力有限，长视野任务需要外部信息和计算支持，现有框架常因执行环境问题导致模型失败，无法准确衡量模型真实能力。

Method: 提出 Prime Agent 框架，包含持久 IPython REPL 实现程序化上下文和测试时计算，持续框架保存跨轨迹信息，递归子代理通过直接通信协调，并提供人类可管理的守护进程会话，同时标准化执行、恢复、验证和资源核算。

Result: ARC-AGI-3 RHAE Best@1 从 30% 提升至 95.5%，在长上下文编码、GPU 内核生成、模拟器构建和 nanoGPT 速跑等任务中匹配或超越现有框架，Factorio 中实现连续技術进步和并行子代理工作。

Conclusion: Prime Agent 通过将框架故障与模型故障分离，释放模型真实能力，为长视野评估和编码代理提供了高效、稳健的解决方案，代码已开源。

中文摘要：语言模型是顺序处理器，但长视野代理需要超越模型权重和活动上下文的外部信息与计算。Prime Agent 是一个用于长视野评估和编码代理工作流的开源框架。一个持久的 IPython REPL 遵循递归语言模型抽象，用于程序化上下文处理和测试时计算，而持续性框架（Continual Harness）跨轨迹保存历史、记忆、技能、提示和子代理规范。递归子代理通过直接的代理间通信进行协调，代理视图（Agents View）允许人类检查和管理守护进程支持的会话。Prime Agent 标准化了执行、恢复、验证和资源核算，同时将策略构建留给模型。这种低摩擦、富有表现力的膜防止框架故障变成模型故障，并将测量推向模型真实的最大潜在能力。Prime Agent 将 ARC-AGI-3 RHAE Best@1 从 30% 提升到 95.5%，并在长上下文编码、GPU 内核生成、模拟器构建和自主 nanoGPT 速跑中匹配或超过原生和流行的框架。在 Factorio 中，我们发现细化允许连续的技术进步，而专门的子代理能够实现并行化工作。代码可在 https://github.com/PrimeIntellect-ai/prime-agent 获取。

Original abstract: Language models are sequential processors, but long-horizon agency requires external information and computation beyond model weights and active context. Prime Agent is an open-source harness for long-horizon evaluation and coding-agent workflows. A persistent IPython REPL follows the Recursive Language Model abstraction for programmatic context processing and test-time compute, while Continual Harness preserves histories, memories, skills, prompts, and subagent specifications across trajectories. Recursive subagents coordinate through direct agent-to-agent communication, and the Agents View lets humans inspect and manage daemon-backed sessions. Prime Agent standardizes execution, recovery, verification, and resource accounting while leaving strategy construction to the model. This low-friction, expressive membrane prevents harness failures from becoming model failures and pushes measurement toward the model's true maximal underlying capability. Prime Agent raises ARC-AGI-3 RHAE Best@1 from 30% to 95.5% and matches or exceeds native and popular harnesses across long-context coding, GPU-kernel generation, emulator construction, and autonomous nanoGPT speedruns. On Factorio, we find refinement allows for continuous technology progression and dedicated subagents enable parallelized work. Code is available at https://github.com/PrimeIntellect-ai/prime-agent.

</details>


### [21] [GameXpert-Bench: How Far Are Coding Agents from Expert Game Development?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21833)
*Kun Chen,Haorong Hong,Peizhong Gao,Jianfeng Lin,Tongxu Luo,Yuxuan Xie,Chenxu Liu,Jieling He,Zhongyuan Liu,Zeno Zeng*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 面向coding agent的游戏开发评测基准，覆盖生成、缺陷修复与多轮优化全生命周期。

TL;DR: GameXpert-Bench是一个针对LLM编码代理游戏开发能力的基准，涵盖生成、修复和优化三个阶段，发现当前代理在发现缺陷和保持功能方面较弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准仅评估最终产物或单一开发阶段，无法全面反映LLM在完整游戏开发生命周期中的能力，因此需要覆盖生成、修复和优化的综合基准。

Method: 通过分析完整的人机代理开发轨迹，提出GameXpert-Bench，包含三个轨道：GameGen（单请求生成完整游戏）、GameFix（缺陷诊断与修复）和GameOpt（多轮累积优化）。每个轨道采用实时游戏交互、确定性行为测试或最终产品标准评估，并包含大量任务和注入缺陷。

Result: 基准包含97个生成任务（11个类型）、100个修复任务（50个关卡，每关19-27个缺陷）和17个优化链（6轮102请求）。实验发现代理在生成可玩基础和实现明确需求上更可靠，但在发现缺陷、验证运行时行为和保持功能方面较弱。

Conclusion: 当前LLM代理的游戏开发能力仍不均衡，需要在缺陷发现、运行时验证和变更保全方面加以改进；GameXpert-Bench为系统评估这些能力提供了基础。

中文摘要：近期的大型语言模型（LLM）能够作为编码代理，从自然语言请求构建完整的游戏。游戏开发尤其具有挑战性，因为程序逻辑、视觉和音频内容、界面、交互和可玩性必须在一个可执行产物中协同工作。因此，衡量这种能力需要对游戏产品和开发过程进行评估。现有基准通常通过评估最终产物或孤立的开发阶段来评估LLM的游戏开发能力。我们对完整的人机代理开发轨迹的分析确定了三个阶段，它们共同涵盖了使用编码代理进行游戏开发的生命周期：初始游戏生成、缺陷诊断与修复，以及多轮优化。因此，我们引入了GameXpert-Bench，将这三个生命周期阶段操作化为三个互补的基准轨道。GameGen评估在空工作区中从单个请求创建完整游戏。GameFix评估在报告缺陷或留待代理发现缺陷时的诊断与修复。GameOpt通过由用户与代理之间的真实开发轨迹生成的请求链评估累积优化。我们使用实时游戏交互、确定性行为测试或带回归检查的最终产品标准来评估每个轨道。该套件包含11个类型的97个生成任务；来自50个经人工验证的游戏关卡的100个修复任务，每个关卡有19-27个注入缺陷；以及17个优化链，包含6轮和102个请求。在这三个轨道中，当前代理在生成可玩基础和实现明确需求方面，比在发现缺陷、验证运行时行为以及在更改中保持功能方面更可靠。

Original abstract: Recent large language models (LLMs) can operate as coding agents that build complete games from natural language requests. Game development is especially demanding because program logic, visual and audio content, interfaces, interaction and playability must function together in one executable artifact. Measuring this capability therefore requires evaluation of both game product and the development process. Existing benchmarks often assess the game development capabilities of LLMs by evaluating the final artifact or an isolated development stage. Our analysis of complete human-agent development trajectories identifies three stages that together span the lifecycle of game development with a coding agent: initial game generation, bug diagnosis and repair, and optimization over multiple turns. Therefore, we introduce GameXpert-Bench, which operationalizes the three lifecycle stages as three complementary benchmark tracks. GameGen evaluates complete game creation from a single request in an empty workspace. GameFix evaluates diagnosis and repair when defects are reported or left for the agent to discover. GameOpt evaluates cumulative optimization through request chains seeded by real development trajectories between users and agents. We evaluate each track using live game interaction, deterministic behavioral tests, or final product criteria with regression checks. The suite contains 97 generation tasks across 11 genres; 100 repair tasks from 50 game levels verified by humans, each with 19-27 injected bugs; and 17 optimization chains with six turns and 102 requests. Across the three tracks, current agents are more reliable at producing playable foundations and implementing explicit requirements than at discovering defects, verifying runtime behavior, and preserving functionality across changes.

</details>


### [22] [LLM4LLM: Bridging Kernel Benchmarks and Real Deployment via Closed-Loop Agentic Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21836)
*Hui Zeng,Pengfei Yang,Yanxin Chen,Fusong Ju,Xinran Wei*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: accepted by EMNLP 2026 main conference

Selection reason: 部署感知的闭环agentic kernel优化框架，直接对应SemOpt目标并有显著加速结果。

TL;DR: 提出LLM4LLM，一种部署感知的闭环优化框架，直接优化语言模型推理脚本，在真实工作负载上实现端到端延迟大幅加速，并弥合了内核基准与部署行为之间的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有内核优化基准测试（如KernelBench）与实际语言模型推理部署存在差距，独立测试中正确的内核在实际推理工作负载中可能表现不同，导致性能或安全风险。

Method: 从目标推理脚本出发，提取阶段感知优化任务，使用经验引导的episodic智能体进行搜索，并通过模型内验证接受补丁，形成闭环优化。

Result: 在A100和H100上的十个LM推理工作负载中，所有模型端到端延迟均改善，几何平均加速比分别为3.91×和6.98×；KernelBench Level 2上获得最高2.745×几何平均加速。

Conclusion: LLM4LLM表明部署感知的优化框架能有效提升真实推理性能，弥补基准到部署的差距，并支持内核级证据。

中文摘要：大型语言模型已成为低层代码和内核优化的日益强大的智能体，但孤立的内核基准测试仅能代表语言模型推理中实际部署行为的近似。我们发现了一个从基准测试到部署的差距：在独立测试框架中看似正确且快速的候选内核，在集成到真实推理工作负载后，可能表现出不同的性能、安全性或阶段行为。我们引入了LLM4LLM，一个部署感知的闭环优化框架，它从目标推理脚本开始，提取阶段感知的优化任务，使用经验引导的 episodic 智能体进行搜索，并通过模型内验证接受补丁。在A100和H100 GPU上的十个语言模型推理工作负载中，LLM4LLM显著改善了每个评估模型的端到端延迟，在A100/H100上实现了3.91×/6.98×的几何平均加速；作为支持性的内核级证据，它还在KernelBench Level 2上达到了高达2.745×的几何平均加速。

Original abstract: Large language models have become increasingly capable agents for low-level code and kernel optimization, but isolated kernel benchmarks provide only a proxy for the deployment behavior that matters in language-model inference. We identify a benchmark-to-deployment gap: candidate kernels that appear correct and fast in standalone harnesses can exhibit different performance, safety, or phase behavior after integration into a real inference workload. We introduce LLM4LLM, a deployment-aware closed-loop optimization framework that starts from a target inference script, extracts phase-aware optimization tasks, searches with an experience-guided episodic agent, and accepts patches through in-model validation. Across ten language-model inference workloads on A100 and H100 GPUs, LLM4LLM improves end-to-end latency for every evaluated model, achieving 3.91$\times$/6.98$\times$ geometric-mean speedups on A100/H100; as supporting kernel-level evidence, it also attains up to 2.745$\times$ GeoMean speedup on KernelBench Level 2.

</details>


### [23] [MemGuard: Persisting Verifier Signals for LLM-Agent Memory Governance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21867)
*Haoyu Wang,Guangyuan Dong,He Liang,Zijing Zhang,Jiachen Luo,Chuang Liu,Chao Xue,Hao Tang*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 30 pages, 7 figures

Selection reason: 将verifier信号作为记忆生命周期元数据，解决agent记忆准入与漂移，直接可迁移。

TL;DR: MemGuard通过将验证器输出转化为持久生命周期元数据，解决LLM智能体记忆中的不可靠准入和记忆漂移问题，在多个基准上显著超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有记忆系统在长期任务流中因不可靠准入和记忆漂移导致存储经验不可靠，检索无法修复，亟需生命周期管理。

Method: MemGuard将多标准评分令牌验证转换为奖励、置信度、标签和不确定性描述符，作为持久元数据附加到每个候选，并在检索、冲突解决、总结和归档中重复使用。

Result: 在16种骨干-基准设置中均取得最佳成功率和最低步骤数，最大提升：WebArena 7.9成功率点，Mind2Web 5.6步骤成功率点，终端与软件工程基准2.4-3.5点。

Conclusion: MemGuard验证了将验证器输出作为生命周期元数据能显著提升长期记忆的可靠性与智能体性能，代码已开源。

中文摘要：LLM智能体正从单提示词使用转向长任务流，在这些任务流中，可复用记忆成为终端、软件工程和网络任务的核心能力。这种记忆只有在存储的经验在数百次交互中保持可靠时才有用，但两种故障模式在实践中打破了这一假设。第一种是不可靠准入：失败轨迹、偶然成功和误导性观察结果因看起来相关而进入记忆，随后误导后续决策。第二种是记忆漂移：长期运行的记忆库积累重复、过时和冲突的记录，仅靠检索无法修复。MemGuard的关键区别在于将验证器输出视为持久的生命周期元数据，而非一次性过滤器。它将多标准评分令牌验证转换为奖励、置信度、标签和不确定性描述符，这些描述符在每个候选激活前附加，并在检索、冲突解决、总结和归档中重复使用。我们在Terminal-Bench 2.0、SWE-Bench Verified、WebArena和Mind2Web上，使用四种骨干网络评估MemGuard，在匹配运行时预算下与四个记忆基线及一个仅验证器对照组进行比较。在五个种子的平均结果中，MemGuard在所有16种骨干-基准设置中取得了最佳成功指标和最低平均步骤数，相对于我们评估的记忆方法中最强的先前基线ReasoningBank，最大提升为WebArena上7.9个成功率点、Mind2Web上5.6个步骤成功率点，以及终端和软件工程基准上2.4-3.5个点。代码可在https://github.com/whyyyyy123/MemGuard获取。

Original abstract: LLM agents are moving from single-prompt use to long task streams in which reusable memory becomes a core capability for terminal, software-engineering, and web tasks. Such memory is useful only when stored experience remains reliable across hundreds of interactions, but two failure modes break that assumption in practice. The first is unreliable admission: failed trajectories,accidental successes, and misleading observations enter memory because they appear relevant, then mislead later decisions. The second is memory drift: long-running banks accumulate duplicate, stale, and conflicting records that retrieval alone cannot repair. MemGuard's key distinction is to treat verifier output not as a one-shot filter, but as persistent lifecycle metadata. It converts multi-criteria score-token verification into reward, confidence, label, and uncertainty descriptors that are attached to every candidate before activation and reused during retrieval, conflict resolution, summarization, and archival. We evaluate MemGuard on Terminal-Bench 2.0, SWE-Bench Verified, WebArena, and Mind2Web across four backbones, comparing against four memory baselines plus a verifier-only control under matched runtime budgets. Averaged over five seeds, MemGuard achieves the best success metric and lowest average steps in all 16 backbone-benchmark settings, improving over ReasoningBank, the strongest prior baseline among the memory methods we evaluate, with a largest gain of 7.9 success-rate points on WebArena, 5.6 step-success-rate points on Mind2Web, and 2.4-3.5 points on terminal and software-engineering benchmarks. Code is available at https://github.com/whyyyyy123/MemGuard.

</details>


### [24] [HiMA-MDD: A Hierarchical Multi-Agent Harness for Interpretable Multimodal Depression Detection in Clinical Interviews](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21868)
*Ao Chen,Xiaojiang Peng*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 分层多agent协调框架与证据追踪，虽用于医疗但编排机制可迁移到其他agent系统。

TL;DR: 提出一种层级化多智能体框架HiMA-MDD，通过三层结构协调证据路由、症状评估与审计，在E-DAIC上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM系统要么整体处理访谈，要么分配通用角色，缺少跨层级的显式编排来协调证据访问、评分权威、反馈和状态记录。

Method: HiMA-MDD采用三层智能体：预处理构建QA单元；第一层识别QA-条目关系并路由证据；第二层分配因子专家产生暂定分值；第三层审计并修订，生成最终PHQ-8画像，同时产生层级证据追踪。

Result: 在E-DAIC数据集上基于Qwen2.5-72B-Instruct的实验表明，HiMA-MDD优于对比的最先进方法。

Conclusion: 层级化多智能体编排有效提升多模态临床访谈抑郁评估的准确性与可审计性。

中文摘要：从多模态临床访谈中进行抑郁评估，需要将来自多个症状的分散证据整合为一个连贯的PHQ-8画像。这一过程是层级化的：相关证据在局部问答交流中通常是稀疏且依赖上下文的，多个交流共同支持症状层面的判断，最终评估依赖于完整症状画像的连贯性。现有的LLM系统要么整体性地处理访谈，要么将工作分配给通用智能体角色；这两种设计都不一定能提供一种显式的编排机制，来协调跨这些层级的证据访问、条目评分权威、有界反馈和状态记录。为弥补这一空白，我们引入了HiMA-MDD，一种层级化多智能体框架，将这一评估层级与三个智能体层对齐。在非智能体预处理构建保留上下文的的多模态QA单元之后，第1层识别候选的QA到条目的关系，并支持有界的、基于条目的证据路由。第2层将症状组分配给操作性的因子专家，每个专家负责一个暂定条目评分。第3层审计完整的暂定画像，请求至多一轮有针对性的修订，并重建经过验证的PHQ-8画像。这种分层设计自然产生了层级化证据追踪，保留了所有中间证据、判断和修订以供审计。最终的条目评分随后确定性地产生总分和筛查决策。使用Qwen2.5-72B-Instruct作为框架骨干，我们在E-DAIC上的实验表明，HiMA-MDD优于所比较的最先进方法。

Original abstract: Depression assessment from multimodal clinical interviews requires integrating dispersed evidence from multiple symptoms into a coherent PHQ-8 profile. This process is hierarchical: relevant evidence is often sparse and context-dependent within local question-answer exchanges, multiple exchanges jointly support symptom-level judgments, and the final assessment depends on the coherence of the complete symptom profile. Existing LLM systems either process interviews holistically or distribute work across generic agent roles; neither design necessarily provides an explicit orchestration mechanism that coordinates evidence access, item-score authority, bounded feedback, and state recording across these levels. To address this gap, we introduce HiMA-MDD, a hierarchical multi-agent harness that aligns this assessment hierarchy with three agent layers. After non-agentic preprocessing constructs context-preserving multimodal QA units, Layer 1 identifies candidate QA-to-item relations and supports bounded item-grounded evidence routing. Layer 2 assigns symptom groups to operational factor specialists, with one specialist responsible for each provisional item score. Layer 3 audits the complete provisional profile, requests at most one round of targeted revision, and reconstructs the verified PHQ-8 profile. This layered design naturally yields a Hierarchical Evidence Trace, preserves all intermediate evidence, judgments, and revisions for auditability. The final item scores then deterministically produce the total score and screening decision. Using Qwen2.5-72B-Instruct as the harness backbone, our experiments on E-DAIC demonstrate that HiMA-MDD outperforms the compared state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [25] [From Solver Feedback to Faithful Plans: Multi-Role Reinforcement Learning for Symbolic Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21897)
*Chenghao Zhang,Yikai Mao,Shanqi Liu,Haoyu Gao,SaiSai Hu,Dan Roth*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 利用solver反馈进行Actor/Judge/Editor多角色RL，与代码优化agent的验证修复闭环高度相关。

TL;DR: 提出一种基于求解器反馈的多角色强化学习框架，让单个语言模型兼任Actor、Judge和Editor，无需人工演示即可实现自然语言到PDDL的忠实转换，在PlanBench上显著提升成功率并降低语义漂移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在将自然语言指令转换为PDDL规范时，依赖昂贵的人工PDDL标注，且可能利用求解器成功但语义不忠实，因此需要一种无需人工演示、仅依赖求解器反馈的方法来学习忠实的形式化。

Method: 提出求解器验证的多角色强化学习框架，使用单个语言模型扮演三个角色：Actor生成PDDL规范，Judge基于求解器结果提供校准的质量信号，Editor根据有界诊断条件进行细化修复，通过强化学习优化生成、验证和修复过程。

Result: 在PlanBench基准上，平均成功率从LLM+P的35.5%提升至70.8%，忠实成功率达到66.3%，语义漂移降至6.4%。

Conclusion: 将求解器反馈组织为生成、验证和修复三种角色，能够实现更可扩展且无需标注的忠实符号规划，验证了该框架的有效性。

中文摘要：可靠的规划需要将自然语言指令转换为可执行的符号规范，然而大型语言模型在没有昂贵的PDDL标注的情况下仍然脆弱，并且可能以语义不忠实的方式利用求解器的成功。我们研究如何仅使用求解器反馈来学习忠实的自然语言到PDDL的形式化，而无需人工编写的演示。我们提出了一种基于求解器的多角色强化学习框架，其中单个语言模型充当Actor、Judge和Editor，分别用于生成、验证和修复。Actor提出PDDL规范，Judge提供经求解器校准的质量信号，Editor执行有界诊断条件下的细化。在PlanBench上，我们的方法将LLM+P的平均成功率从35.5%提高到70.8%，实现了66.3%的忠实成功率，并将语义漂移降低到6.4%。这些结果表明，将求解器反馈组织为生成、验证和修复角色，能够实现更可扩展且无需标注的忠实符号规划。

Original abstract: Reliable planning requires converting natural-language instructions into executable symbolic specifications, yet large language models remain brittle without costly PDDL annotations and may exploit solver success in semantically unfaithful ways. We study how to learn faithful natural-language-to-PDDL formalization using only solver feedback, without human-written demonstrations. We propose a solvergrounded multi-role reinforcement learning framework where a single language model acts as an Actor, Judge, and Editor for generation, verification, and repair. The Actor proposes PDDL specifications, the Judge provides a solver-calibrated quality signal, and the Editor performs bounded diagnostic-conditioned refinement. On PlanBench, our method improves average success from 35.5% for LLM+P to 70.8%, achieves 66.3% faithful success, and reduces semantic drift to 6.4%. These results show that organizing solver feedback into generation, verification, and repair roles enables more scalable and faithful annotation-free symbolic planning

</details>


### [26] [Training Needs Trustworthy Worlds: Verified Synthetic Web Environments for Agent Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21898)
*Chenghao Zhang,Canran Xiao,SaiSai Hu,Dan Roth*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 构建验证性合成环境训练 web agent，环境可执行性与缺陷修复思路对 coding agent 的合成环境与可靠验证有直接迁移价值。

TL;DR: 提出一种可执行、可审计且基于后端状态的合成Web环境生成框架，通过验证和修复缺陷显著提升智能体训练效果与迁移性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有合成Web环境仅表面逼真，存在链接损坏、状态不一致和任务不可行等问题，限制了智能体训练的可扩展性和可信度。

Method: 将网站表示为结构化脚手架，包含页面、导航链接、数据库记录、状态变更标记和任务约束；训练前验证修复结构、语义、一致性和可行性缺陷；交互时确定性执行UI转换，仅通过验证的状态变更标记触发后端更新，并利用验证的任务进度谓词编译密集奖励。

Result: 在500个合成环境、六个领域上，任务阻塞缺陷减少，可行任务率从48.6%提升至94.8%；训练出的PPO策略更强，且无需LLM调用即可提升对WebArena、WebShop和MiniWoB++的迁移。

Conclusion: 验证过的合成环境可作为紧凑型Web智能体可扩展且可靠的训练基础，将学习从表面合理性转向可执行、状态接地监督。

中文摘要：Web 智能体有望自动化复杂数字工作流，但其训练仍受限于合成环境——这些环境看似合理，却隐藏着损坏的链接、不一致的状态或不可行的任务。我们通过构建可执行、可审计且基于后端状态的合成 Web 环境，弥合了可扩展环境生成与可信智能体学习之间的差距。我们的框架将每个生成的网站表示为页面、导航链接、数据库记录、状态变更标记和任务约束的结构化脚手架，然后在策略训练前验证并修复结构、语义、一致性和可行性缺陷。在交互过程中，普通 UI 转换被确定性地执行，而持久化后端更新仅通过经过验证的状态变更标记来调用，从而能够从经过验证的任务进度谓词编译出密集奖励。在跨越六个领域的 500 个合成环境中，我们的方法减少了任务阻塞缺陷，并将可行任务率从 48.6% 提升至 94.8%，同时产生了更强的 PPO 策略，并在评估时无需 LLM 调用即可改善对 WebArena、WebShop 和 MiniWoB++ 的迁移。这些结果表明，经过验证的合成环境可以作为紧凑型 Web 智能体的可扩展且可靠的训练基础，将合成 Web 智能体学习从表面层面的合理性转向可执行的、基于状态的监督。

Original abstract: Web agents promise to automate complex digital workflows, but their training remains limited by synthetic environments that look plausible while hiding broken links, inconsistent states, or infeasible tasks. We address the gap between scalable environment generation and trustworthy agent learning by constructing synthetic web environments that are executable, auditable, and grounded in backend state. Our framework represents each generated website as a structured scaffold of pages, navigation links, database records, state-change markers, and task constraints, then verifies and repairs structural, semantic, consistency, and feasibility defects before policy training. During interaction, ordinary UI transitions are executed deterministically, while persistent backend updates are invoked only through validated state-change markers, enabling dense rewards compiled from verified task-progress predicates. Across 500 synthetic environments spanning six domains, our method reduces task-blocking defects and improves feasible-task rate from 48.6% to 94.8%, while producing stronger PPO policies and improving transfer to WebArena, WebShop, and MiniWoB++ without LLM calls at evaluation time. These results show that verified synthetic environments can serve as a scalable and reliable training substrate for compact web agents, shifting synthetic webagent learning from surface-level plausibility toward executable, state-grounded supervision.

</details>


### [27] [GuardianBench: A Same-Scene Instruction-Contrastive Benchmark for Latent Contextual Risk in Embodied AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21928)
*Zhesheng Zhang,Jiahao Lu,Wei Liu,Cong Pan,Jianhua Yang,Yixiang Chen,Hongyuan Yu,Mengqi Zhang,Kailin Lyu,Zhumin Chen,Keji He*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 21 pages, 4 figures

Selection reason: 同场景指令对比的安全评测基准与轻量后训练目标，对 agent 安全推理评测和可靠性改善有潜在迁移价值。

TL;DR: GuardianBench通过同场景安全/不安全指令对比对，揭示并评估VLM在潜在情境风险下的安全推理缺陷，并提出VLOS方法显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有具身安全研究多关注视觉上下文变化或执行时动态，忽略了固定场景下仅改变指令这一维度，而潜在安全风险恰恰可能在指令与场景组合时出现。

Method: 构建GuardianBench，基于国际安全标准生成3,024个同场景安全/不安全指令对比对；对VLM进行基准测试，并引入系统性理由审计和VLOS后训练方法。

Result: 主要VLM的平均配对准确率仅24.1%，模型对同一场景下的安全与不安全指令判定不敏感，且失败根源是无法绑定指令相关线索；VLOS在开放权重模型上显著提升性能。

Conclusion: GuardianBench作为受控评估套件，能够暴露并改进VLM在潜在情境风险下的安全推理能力，并为后续判定校准提供基准支持。

中文摘要：在具身人工智能中，安全风险可能是潜在的：一条良性的指令和一个安全的场景只有在组合时才变得危险。先前的工作通过改变视觉上下文或评估执行时动态来推进具身安全，但固定场景且仅改变指令这一互补轴仍未得到充分探索。我们引入了GuardianBench，一个基于国际安全标准的指令对比基准，通过3,024个指令-场景示例（组织为跨多种危害类别的同场景安全/不安全对比对）来隔离这种潜在的情境风险。对最先进的视觉语言模型（VLM）进行基准测试揭示了指令不敏感的判定：模型在给定场景下不成比例地批准两种指令；在主要模型中，平均配对准确率仅为24.1%。我们的系统性理由审计定位了主要失败：模型未能绑定区分安全与不安全组合的指令相关线索。作为训练后案例研究，Verdict Log-Odds Supervision（VLOS）——一种轻量级的判定级目标——在开放权重基座模型上显著提升了性能。综上所述，我们的潜在情境风险任务制定、基于标准的对比基准构建、配对级和理由级失败诊断以及基准驱动的判定校准，使GuardianBench成为一个受控评估套件，用于在潜在情境风险下暴露和改进对指令-场景组合的安全推理。

Original abstract: In embodied AI, safety risk can be latent: a benign instruction and a safe scene become hazardous only when composed. Prior work has advanced embodied safety by varying visual contexts or evaluating execution-time dynamics, but the complementary axis of fixing the scene and varying only the instruction remains underexplored. We introduce GuardianBench, an instruction-contrastive benchmark grounded in international safety standards that isolates this latent contextual risk through 3,024 instruction-scene examples organized as same-scene Safe/Unsafe contrastive pairs across various hazard categories. Benchmarking state-of-the-art vision-language models (VLMs) reveals instruction-insensitive verdicts: models disproportionately approve both instructions under a given scene; across the primary models, average pair accuracy is only 24.1%. Our systematic rationale audit localizes the dominant failure: models fail to bind the instruction-relevant cues that differentiate safe from unsafe compositions. As a post-training case study, Verdict Log-Odds Supervision (VLOS), a lightweight verdict-level objective, substantially improves performance on open-weight backbones. Together, our latent contextual risk task formulation, standards-grounded contrastive benchmark construction, pair-level and rationale-level failure diagnosis, and benchmark-enabled verdict calibration establish GuardianBench as a controlled evaluation suite for exposing and improving safety reasoning over instruction-scene compositions under latent contextual risk.

</details>


### [28] [TessIndex: Capability Verified Identity System for the Agent Economy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21942)
*Mehul Goenka,Tejas Pathak,Siddharth Asthana*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 面向 agent 经济体的能力验证身份系统，涉及 agent 可靠性与安全控制，但偏基础设施而非直接方法。

TL;DR: TessIndex 是一个面向智能体原语的能力验证身份系统，利用区块链与中心化服务器结合的双平面架构，提供持久身份、可验证能力证明和代币化价值捕获，以支撑智能体经济。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
