An inconsistent return leaves the schema channel untouched and registers in the likelihood comparison on the field whose true value the context already carries verbatim, not on the one whose contradiction runs through the domain policy. The action distribution gives each condition a distinct signature, but orders them by how far the return bears on the next action rather than by fault family. Recognition is therefore asymmetric: each condition is legible in some channel, and no channel is legible on all of them.

</details>


### [56] [CacheRouter: A Dual-Path Tool Routing Architecture with Cache-Preserving Main-Model Isolation for Long-Tail Tool Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22708)
*Donghui Zha,Lingwei Xu,Linxiao Wu,Yixue Dong,Haochen Li*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 双路径工具路由与缓存保持架构，优化长尾工具发现，对 agent 工具调用与缓存设计有启发。

TL;DR: 提出双路径路由架构，将工具选择与传递分离，使主模型请求前缀固定，从而兼顾渐进式披露与提示缓存，实现高缓存命中率和成本降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 渐进式披露需要动态改变可见工具列表，导致请求前缀变化，使提示缓存失效；需要一种请求架构在保持工具披露灵活性的同时维持稳定的缓存前缀。

Method: 设计双路径路由：主模型使用固定的小型核心工具集，其他工具通过独立路由通道由路由器子模型选择并执行；工具注册自动化并支持运行时更新，不改变主模型请求前缀。

Result: 在55个功能性查询和30轮对话上，token级缓存命中率达90.99%和95.2%，输入成本分别降至无缓存基线的约12.0%和8.0%（DeepSeek定价下）。

Conclusion: 该设计有效化解了渐进式披露与提示缓存的冲突，通过稳定的主模型前缀和独立路由通道实现可扩展的工具集，显著降低成本并保持披露能力。

中文摘要：LLM 系统中的工具使用面临结构性权衡。渐进式披露通过仅展示与当前任务相关的工具来保持 prompt 较小，而提示缓存则奖励在多次调用中保持固定的请求前缀；对可见工具列表的任何更改都会使缓存的前缀失效。本文将这一权衡视为请求架构问题，并提出一种双路径路由设计，将工具选择与工具传递分配到独立的通道。主模型始终看到一个小型、固定的核心工具集，因此其请求头部在多次调用中保持不变；所有其他工具通过一个独立的路由通道访问，在该通道中，路由器子模型搜索完整工具列表，选择一个工具，执行它，并返回结果。工具注册从源代码自动生成并支持运行时更新，因此工具集可以在不修改主模型请求前缀的情况下增长。该设计推广了渐进式披露：能力通过路由通道披露，而主模型的前缀保持稳定。一个原型实现已在 55 个功能性查询和 30 轮对话上进行了测试；token 级缓存命中率达到 90.99% 和 95.2%，在 DeepSeek 的定价下（缓存命中的输入 token 成本约为缓存未命中 token 的 1/30），将输入成本降低到无缓存基线的约 12.0% 和 8.0%。

Original abstract: Tool use in LLM systems faces a structural trade-off. Progressive disclosure keeps the prompt small by showing only the tools relevant to the current task, while prompt caching rewards a request prefix that stays fixed across calls; every change to the visible tool list invalidates the cached prefix. This paper treats the trade-off as a problem of request architecture and proposes a dual-path routing design that assigns tool selection and tool delivery to separate channels. The main model always sees a small, fixed set of core tools, so the head of its request is unchanged across calls; all other tools are reached through an independent routing channel, in which a router sub-model searches the full tool list, selects one tool, executes it, and returns the result. Tool registration is automated from source code and supports runtime updates, so the tool set can grow without modifying the main model's request prefix. The design generalizes progressive disclosure: capabilities are disclosed through the routing channel, and the main model's prefix stays stable. A prototype implementation was exercised on 55 functional queries and a 30-turn dialogue; token-level cache hit rates reached 90.99% and 95.2%, cutting input cost to about 12.0% and 8.0% of a no-cache baseline under DeepSeek's pricing, where cache-hit input tokens cost roughly 1/30 of cache-miss tokens.

</details>


### [57] [The Compaction Cliff in Long-Running AI Agent Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22752)
*Saber Zerhoudi,Jelena Mitrovic,Michael Granitzer*

Main category: cs.AI

Journal reference: Proceedings of the 35th ACM International Conference on Information and Knowledge Management (CIKM 2026)

DOI: [10.1145/3799682.3840567](https://doi.org/10.1145/3799682.3840567)

Selection reason: 研究长程 agent 记忆压缩中的安全规则丢失，提出按类型分流的记忆保持框架，对 agent 上下文管理有直接价值。

TL;DR: 提出知识分流（Knowledge Triage）框架，通过按类型分类和定制保留策略，解决AI代理上下文压缩中安全规则快速丢失的“压缩悬崖”问题，大幅提升规则保留率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有压缩方法对安全规则和事件日志同等地进行总结，但安全规则需要精确措辞，导致压缩后规则快速失效；Claude Code在一轮后仅保留53%，五轮后仅保留10%，表明存在严重的规则丢失问题。

Method: 提出知识分流框架，将代理知识库逐行分类，并通过三个确定性操作符（TypeCompact、TypeDecompose、TypeRetrieve）分别处理就地压缩、大主题分解和外部检索，每种类型采用专属保留策略。

Result: 在五个公共语料库上，TypeCompact的安全规则保留量比最强的单次LLM压缩器多2-4倍，五轮召回率达96%；TypeDecompose达到0%局部性违规，TypeRetrieve达到100%的recall@50；在三个下游行为基准上均显著优于生产基线。

Conclusion: 知识分流显著提升了压缩过程中安全规则的保留率，并在多个领域和基准上验证了有效性，同时发布了大型语料库和实现，为AI代理上下文管理提供了新方向。

中文摘要：在AI代理的上下文中，一条安全规则和一段事件日志竞争相同的令牌。当预算溢出时，两者以相同的速率被总结；但只有规则需要精确的措辞才能保持可执行性。在20个生产代理配置上，Claude Code的/compact提示在Sonnet 4.6上在一轮压缩后保留了53%的安全规则，在五轮后保留了10%。我们将此命名为“压缩悬崖”（Compaction Cliff）。我们通过知识分流（Knowledge Triage）来解决这个问题，该框架将代理知识库的每一行按类型分类，并让每种类型通过其自身的保留策略进行路由。三个确定性操作符在三种上下文管理操作中实现这种分流：TypeCompact按类型保真度就地重写条目，TypeDecompose将过大而无法安全压缩的主题分区，并将范围内的安全规则复制到各分区，TypeRetrieve从外部存储中获取条目，并将范围内的规则固定在相关性之前。在五个公共语料库上，TypeCompact在每个比率下比最强的单次LLM压缩器多保留2--4倍的安全规则，在五轮中达到96%的召回率。TypeDecompose达到0%的局部性违规，而统一分区为93%。TypeRetrieve达到100%的recall@50，而最佳单次LLM检索器为73%。在三个下游行为基准上，我们在医疗合规性（配对McNemar p < 10^-8，N = 200）上优于生产Sonnet压缩器，在零售任务通过率上优于完整策略和分层基线（p < 0.01，N = 115），在航空领域优于分层压缩（p = 0.024）。我们发布了AgentArtifactCorpus（来自54,628个公共GitHub仓库的396,934个代理配置）、分类器和参考实现。

Original abstract: A safety rule and an episodic log compete for the same tokens in an AI agent's context. When the budget overflows, both are summarized at the same rate; only the rule needs exact wording to remain enforceable. On 20 production agent configurations, Claude Code's /compact prompt on Sonnet 4.6 preserves 53\% of safety rules after one compaction round and 10\% after five. We name this the Compaction Cliff. We address it with Knowledge Triage, a framework that classifies each line of an agent's knowledge base by type and routes each type through its own retention policy. Three deterministic operators implement this triage across the three context-management operations: TypeCompact rewrites items in place under per-type fidelity, TypeDecompose partitions a topic too large to compact safely, replicating in-scope safety rules across partitions, and TypeRetrieve fetches items from external storage with in-scope rules pinned ahead of relevance. On five public corpora, TypeCompact preserves 2--4$\times$ more safety rules than the strongest single-shot LLM compactor at every ratio, with 96\% recall over five rounds. TypeDecompose reaches 0\% locality violations against 93\% under uniform partitioning. TypeRetrieve reaches 100\% recall@50 against 73\% for the best single-shot LLM retriever. On three downstream behavioral benchmarks, we outperform the production Sonnet compactor on medical compliance (paired McNemar $p < 10^{-8}$ on preservation, $N = 200$), the full-policy and hierarchical baselines on retail task pass rate ($p < 0.01$, $N = 115$), and the hierarchical compaction on the airline domain ($p = 0.024$). We release AgentArtifactCorpus (396{,}934 agent configurations from 54{,}628 public GitHub repositories), the classifier, and the reference implementation.

</details>


### [58] [Compositional Chain-of-Relations for Faithful Knowledge Graph Question Answering with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22762)
*Chenhui Liu,Jianpeng Zhou,Jiahai Wang*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 提出关系中心探索与组合关系链，增强 KGQA agent 的候选检索与约束验证的可信性。

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种以关系为中心的探索范式，并基于该范式设计了CCoR框架，通过主链和约束链分别接地候选检索与约束处理，解决了现有方法中实体剪枝不可靠和约束处理无根据的问题，在四个KGQA基准上提升了准确率、忠实性和效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于智能体的KGQA方法在候选检索阶段使用以实体为中心的探索，需要将实体剪枝为固定大小子集，导致有效实体被丢弃；同时约束处理依赖LLM内部知识而非知识图谱，产生幻觉。这两个缺陷使得复杂多跳KGQA的忠实性难以保证。

Method: 引入以关系为中心的探索范式，以关系为搜索单元，避免实体剪枝。提出CCoR框架，包含两条关系链：主链负责候选检索，约束链通过显式知识图谱探索来验证查询约束，使两个阶段都基于知识图谱。

Result: 在四个KGQA基准上，CCoR相比强基线在准确率、忠实性和效率上均持续提升，尤其在复杂查询上提升更明显。

Conclusion: 以关系为中心的探索和双链设计能够有效解决KGQA中的实体剪枝和约束无根据问题，提升复杂查询的推理质量，为KG增强LLM的忠实推理提供了新思路。

中文摘要：知识图谱问答（KGQA）是评估基于知识图谱增强的大型语言模型（LLM）的关键任务，而需要多跳推理的复杂KGQA尤其具有挑战性。解决复杂查询涉及两个耦合阶段：候选检索（在知识图谱上定位答案候选）和约束处理（根据查询约束过滤这些候选）。忠实推理要求两个阶段都基于知识图谱。然而，现有的基于智能体的方法通过以实体为中心的探索来接地候选检索，而将约束处理留给LLM的内部知识，这导致了两个关键缺陷。(1) 不可靠的实体剪枝：以实体为中心的探索将实体作为搜索单元，并且必须在每一跳将它们剪枝为固定大小的子集。由于知识图谱中的实体信息往往不完整，且固定大小的子集无法保留所有有效实体，这种剪枝不可避免地会丢弃有效实体，最终导致错误答案。(2) 无根据的约束处理：查询约束是从LLM的内部知识而非知识图谱中解析出来的，使得最终答案无法验证且容易产生幻觉。为了解决这些缺陷，本文引入了一种以关系为中心的探索范式，该范式使用关系而非实体作为搜索单元，从而避免了不可靠的实体剪枝。在此范式的基础上，本文提出了组合关系链（CCoR），一个简单而有效的框架，通过两条关系链将两个阶段都接地在知识图谱上：一条用于候选检索的主链和一条通过显式知识图谱探索验证查询约束的约束链。在四个KGQA基准上的实验表明，CCoR在准确率、忠实性和效率上持续优于强基线，在复杂查询上的提升更为显著。

Original abstract: Knowledge graph question answering (KGQA) is a key task for evaluating KG-augmented Large Language Models (LLMs), and complex KGQA that requires multi-hop reasoning is especially challenging. Solving a complex query involves two coupled phases: candidate retrieval, which locates answer candidates over the KG, and constraint handling, which filters these candidates against the query constraints. Faithful reasoning requires grounding both phases in the KG. However, existing agent-based methods ground candidate retrieval through entity-centric exploration, while leaving constraint handling to the LLM's internal knowledge, which leads to two critical limitations. (1) Unreliable entity pruning: entity-centric exploration uses entities as search units and must prune them to a fixed-size subset at each hop. Because entity information in KGs is often incomplete and a fixed-size subset cannot retain all valid entities, such pruning inevitably drops valid entities and ultimately leads to wrong answers. (2) Ungrounded constraint handling: query constraints are resolved from the LLM's internal knowledge rather than the KG, leaving the final answers unverifiable and prone to hallucination. To address these limitations, this paper introduces a relation-centric exploration paradigm, which uses relations rather than entities as search units and thus avoids unreliable entity pruning. Built on this paradigm, this paper proposes Compositional Chain-of-Relations (CCoR), a simple and effective framework that grounds both phases in the KG with two relation chains: a main chain for candidate retrieval and a constraint chain that verifies query constraints through explicit KG exploration. Experiments on four KGQA benchmarks show that CCoR consistently improves accuracy, faithfulness, and efficiency over strong baselines, with more pronounced gains on complex queries.

</details>


### [59] [The Retriever Should Remember: Experience-Amortized Reranking for Long-Term Agent Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22767)
*Qi Feng,Chris Ding,Jicong Fan*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 提出经验分摊的重排序机制，将历史检索经验用于长程 agent 记忆，显著减少 LLM rerank 开销。

TL;DR: 提出EARM框架，将历史LLM重排序分数作为可复用经验，通过矩阵补全混合估计分数，显著降低推理开销并提升长期记忆检索的答案准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期语言模型智能体的记忆检索器不积累检索经验；语义检索虽快但相关性判断不准，而LLM重排序虽准确但每次查询都无状态重复评分，成本高昂。

Method: EARM将查询-记忆相关性分数存入在线矩阵，利用因果矩阵补全学习共享结构，并用少量新观测分数与估计分数混合，对剩余候选进行重排序。

Result: 在长期对话记忆实验中，混合观测与估计的重排序相比语义检索将答案准确率最高提升6.62%，且仅17.5%候选需要直接LLM评分时仍有效，大幅减少LLM重排序推理开销。

Conclusion: 长期智能体应不仅记忆内容本身，还应记忆内容在检索中的有用性，将检索经验纳入记忆范畴。

中文摘要：长期语言模型智能体会在多次交互中积累记忆，但其检索器通常不会积累检索经验。语义检索虽然高效，但嵌入相似度并不总能反映某条记忆是否包含与当前查询相关的证据。大语言模型（LLM）重排序器能提供更强的查询条件相关性分数，但无状态重排序会反复对大型候选池进行评分，并在每次查询后丢弃这些分数。我们提出了EARM，一种经验摊销重排序框架，将先前获取的LLM相关性分数视为可复用的检索经验。EARM将稀疏的查询-记忆相关性分数存储在一个在线矩阵中，通过因果矩阵补全学习其共享结构，并将少量新观察到的分数与估计分数相结合，对剩余候选进行重排序。随着经验积累，评分预算逐渐减少，将LLM重排序从反复的每次查询开销转变为在智能体生命周期中习得的检索能力。在长期对话记忆上的实验表明，混合观测与估计的重排序相比语义检索，答案准确率最高提升6.62%，且当仅17.5%的候选获得直接LLM相关性分数时仍然有效，从而大幅降低了LLM重排序的推理开销。这些结果促使我们对智能体记忆有更广阔的视角：一个长期存活的智能体不仅应记住过去的内容，还应记住该内容在检索中如何被证明是有用的。

Original abstract: Long-term language-model agents accumulate memories across interactions, but their retrievers typically do not accumulate retrieval experience. Semantic retrieval is efficient, but embedding similarity does not always reflect whether a memory contains evidence relevant to the current query. Large language model (LLM) rerankers provide stronger query-conditioned relevance scores, yet stateless reranking repeatedly scores a large candidate pool and discards these scores after each query. We introduce EARM, an experience-amortized reranking framework that treats previously acquired LLM relevance scores as reusable retrieval experience. EARM stores sparse query--memory relevance scores in an online matrix, learns their shared structure through causal matrix completion, and combines a small set of newly observed scores with estimated scores to rerank the remaining candidates. The scoring budget decreases as experience accumulates, changing LLM reranking from a repeated per-query expense into a retrieval capability learned over an agent's lifetime. Experiments on long-term conversational memory show that mixed observed-and-estimated reranking improves answer accuracy over semantic retrieval by up to 6.62% and remains effective when only 17.5% of candidates receive direct LLM relevance scores, thereby substantially reducing the inference overhead of LLM reranking. These results motivate a broader view of agent memory: a long-lived agent should remember not only past content, but also how that content has proved useful for retrieval.

</details>


### [60] [Beyond the Harness: End-to-End Optimization of Context Artifacts for Enterprise Text-to-SQL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22830)
*Kate Gwimm,Carson Eisenach*

Main category: cs.AI

Journal reference: COLM 2026 Workshop - Context Beyond the Window

Selection reason: 从历史查询构造可复用 SQL 参考上下文制品，对上下文工程与 harness 优化有借鉴意义。

TL;DR: 针对企业Text-to-SQL，提出从历史使用中构建并优化知识库上下文（如SQL参考卡），在内部生产基准上显著优于仅优化检索框架；在公开BEAVER基准上增益有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业级Text-to-SQL部署瓶颈在于模型接收的上下文而非模型本身：业务逻辑跨越数千张表，模型无法一次读取完整目录。因此最有效的干预点是知识库上下文，且该上下文应从历史使用中构建，而非作为固定输入。

Method: 使用查询DAG分解（从生产SQL恢复的中间表示）比较oracle查询图与检索到的知识库上下文的价值；进一步优化蒸馏流程，将历史查询画像转化为可复用的SQL参考卡，并联合优化检索框架与上下文工件。

Result: 在5176条生产查询上，优化上下文工件带来约12–25%的AST相似度提升，优于优化检索框架的约3–12%。在公开BEAVER基准上结果更混合：仅表卡与原始历史SQL相当；最佳优化变体同时检索卡片和原始SQL，在N=300子集上得分9.00% vs 基线6.33%（p=0.12），且无智能体循环。

Conclusion: 从历史使用构建并优化的知识库上下文能有效提升企业Text-to-SQL，在内部生产环境增益明显；但在缺乏生产使用信号的公开基准上优势有限，未来可探索结合更多上下文信号或检索与卡片优化的协同。

中文摘要：部署LLM用于企业级Text-to-SQL的瓶颈主要不在于模型本身，而在于模型接收到的上下文：业务逻辑跨越数千张表，没有模型能一次读取完整目录。因此我们认为，最有效的干预点是模型消费的“知识库上下文”，并且该上下文应从历史使用记录中构建，而非作为固定输入进行调优。我们使用查询DAG分解（与BEAVER等企业基准标注的中间表示同属一类，此处从生产SQL中恢复），比较了oracle查询图与检索到的知识库上下文的价值。在该消融实验中，检索到的知识库上下文在添加到完整oracle图时提供了最大的边际改进。在此基础上，我们优化了一个蒸馏流程，将历史查询画像转化为可复用的SQL参考卡。在来自一家大型在线零售商的5176条生产查询基准上，优化这些上下文工件带来的增益（约12–25%的AST相似度）大于优化检索框架（约3–12%）。在公开的BEAVER基准上，由于缺少内部设置中可用的生产使用信号，结果更为复杂：仅使用表卡与使用原始历史SQL的表现大致相同。最佳优化变体同时检索卡片和原始SQL，在留出的N=300子集上得分为9.00%，而可比基线为6.33%（p值0.12），使用了检索上下文和框架改动，但没有采用智能体循环。

Original abstract: Deploying LLMs for enterprise Text-to-SQL is bottlenecked less by the model than by what context reaches it: business logic spans thousands of tables, and no model can ingest a full catalog at once. We argue that the most effective place to intervene is therefore the \emph{knowledge-base context} the model consumes, and that this context should be \emph{constructed} from historical usage rather than tuned for as a fixed input. Using a query-DAG decomposition--the same family of intermediates that enterprise benchmarks like BEAVER annotate, here recovered from production SQL--we compare the value of oracle query graphs versus retrieved knowledge-base context. In this ablation, retrieved knowledge-base context provides the largest marginal improvement when added to the full oracle graph. Building on this, we optimize a distillation procedure that turns historical query profiles into reusable SQL reference cards. On a benchmark of 5176 production queries from a major online retailer, optimizing these context artifacts yields larger gains (${\sim}12$--$25\%$ AST similarity) than optimizing the retrieval harness (${\sim}3$--$12\%$). On the public BEAVER benchmark, which lacks the production-usage signals available in our internal setting, the picture is more mixed: table cards alone perform about the same as raw historical SQL. The best optimized variant retrieves both cards and raw SQL, scoring $9.00\%$ versus $6.33\%$ (p-value $0.12$) for the comparable baseline on a held-out $N{=}300$ subset, using retrieved context and harness changes but no agentic loop.

</details>


### [61] [GSAR: Goal-State-Anchor Rewards for Mobile GUI Agents with Self-Evolving Data Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22847)
*Long Zhang,Yuhan Chen,Chaoran Zhang,Wanxia Cao,Kun Huang,Pengzhi Gao,Wei Liu,Jian Luan,Chenliang Li,Lixin Zou*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 为 GUI agent 设计目标状态锚定奖励与自进化数据合成，可迁移到 agent 的 RL 训练与奖励设计。

TL;DR: 提出GSAR奖励框架，通过自进化数据合成和状态锚定机制实现可扩展任务生成与可靠奖励信号，使VLM-based GUI智能体在RL训练中取得高效稳定性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GUI智能体在线RL训练受限于数据合成依赖特定环境、多样性不足，以及评估器可扩展性差或奖励信号不可靠。

Method: 引入GSAR框架，包含自进化数据合成（通过任务执行生成多个环境并产生多样任务与目标状态）和状态锚定机制（自动标注目标状态中相关UI元素作为参考锚点），在RL训练中提供准确可扩展的奖励。

Result: 离线轨迹验证准确率超90%，最接近基于规则的方法；在AndroidWorld和自建基准上训练出的智能体表现强。

Conclusion: GSAR为GUI智能体训练提供了一种可扩展且高效的方法，解决了数据多样性和奖励可靠性的关键瓶颈。

中文摘要：基于视觉语言模型（VLM）的GUI智能体有望从在线强化学习（RL）中显著获益。然而，其训练受到两个基本问题的制约：当前用于GUI智能体的数据合成方法依赖于特定环境，难以生成多样化的数据；而现有的评估器要么可扩展性有限，要么提供不准确且不可靠的奖励信号。为克服这些挑战，我们引入了GSAR（目标状态锚定奖励），一种支持可扩展任务生成并提供可靠奖励信号的RL奖励框架，以实现稳定且高效的政策优化。我们的方法具有自进化数据合成的特点，通过任务执行生成多个环境，并生成多样化的任务和目标状态。作为补充，状态锚定机制自动将成功目标状态中与任务相关的UI元素标注为参考锚点。在RL训练期间，这些参考锚点提供准确、可扩展的奖励信号，显著提升效率。大量评估表明，我们的框架在离线轨迹验证上实现了超过90%的准确率，并且最接近基于规则的方法。此外，使用我们的奖励框架训练的智能体在AndroidWorld和我们构建的基准测试上均表现出强大的性能，为GUI智能体训练建立了一种可扩展的方法。

Original abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) based GUI agents stand to benefit significantly from online reinforcement learning (RL). However, their training is bottlenecked by two fundamental issues: current data synthesis methods for GUI Agents rely on specific environments and struggle to generate diverse data, while existing evaluators either suffer from limited scalability or provide inaccurate and unreliable reward signals. To overcome these challenges, we introduce GSAR (Goal-State-Anchor Reward), a RL reward framework that supports scalable task generation and delivers reliable reward signals for stable and efficient policy optimization. Our approach features self-evolving data synthesis, which produces multiple environments through task execution and generates diverse tasks and goal states. Complementing this, a state-anchor mechanism automatically annotates task-relevant UI elements in successful goal states as reference anchors. During RL training, these reference anchors provide accurate, scalable reward signals that substantially enhance efficiency. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that our framework achieves over 90% accuracy on offline trajectory verification and performs closest to rule-based methods. Furthermore, agents trained using our reward framework exhibit strong performance on both AndroidWorld and our constructed benchmark, establishing a scalable approach for GUI agent training.

</details>


### [62] [Your AI, On a Dial: Controlling Investment Bias in LLMs with a Single Neuron](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22852)
*Sahong Park,Suhwan Park,Hoyoung Lee,Gakyung Kwon,Wonbin Ahn,Jaewon Choi,Alejandro Lopez-Lira,Yoon Kim,Chanyeol Choi,Hyeongwoo Kong,Yongjae Lee*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 推理时单神经元干预控制模型投资倾向，在 agent 检索中影响信息搜索与证据选择，属行为控制方向。

TL;DR: 我们提出一个推理时干预单个神经元的“投资偏见拨盘”，能够连续校准LLM的整体投资立场（买入/卖出倾向），且不依赖提示或参数修改。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前研究表明LLM在投资决策中表现出系统性且模型特定的投资偏好，但缺乏一种可精确控制整体投资立场的方法，因此需要一种推理时干预手段来校准模型的投资倾向。

Method: 引入投资偏见拨盘，在推理时对单个神经元进行干预，连续调整模型层面的决策先验；使用匹配的正负证据评估五个开源权重LLM，并在响应、智能体检索、长上下文和回测环境中验证效果。

Result: 拨盘产生投资立场的单调变化，且不修改提示或参数；在响应层面改变决策和理由证据重点；在智能体检索中改变搜索信息、证据选择和最终分析；在长上下文中保持稳定控制，而系统提示指令则逐渐失效；变化还传播到证券排名和投资组合构成。

Conclusion: LLM的总体投资立场可以在推理时被校准到指定目标，为投资决策中的模型行为控制提供了有效工具。

中文摘要：大型语言模型（LLM）越来越多地被用于投资决策，然而先前的研究表明，它们表现出系统性、模型特定的投资偏好。我们研究一个模型的整体投资立场是否可以被校准到指定的方向和强度。我们引入了一个投资偏见拨盘（investment-bias dial），这是一种推理时的干预，作用于单个神经元，连续调整模型层面的决策先验——其总体买入或卖出的倾向——而不针对特定公司或投资属性。使用匹配的正向和负向证据，我们评估了五个开源权重LLM，发现该拨盘在不修改提示或模型参数的情况下，对投资立场产生单调变化。在响应层面，拨盘在相同输入下同时改变了投资决策和生成理由的证据强调。在智能体检索环境中，拨盘还改变了模型搜索的信息、选择的证据以及最终分析中反映的证据。在长上下文评估中，拨盘随着上下文长度的增加保持稳定的立场控制，而匹配的系统提示指令则逐渐减弱。我们进一步表明，拨盘的变化会传播到证券排名和探索性回测中的下游投资组合构成。总体而言，我们的结果表明，LLM的总体投资立场可以在推理时校准到指定的目标。

Original abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used in investment decision-making, yet prior work shows that they exhibit systematic, model-specific investment preferences. We study whether a model's overall investment stance can be calibrated to a specified direction and strength. We introduce an investment-bias dial, an inference-time intervention on a single neuron that continuously adjusts a model-level decision prior---its overall tendency toward buying or selling---without targeting specific firms or investment attributes. Using matched positive and negative evidence, we evaluate five open-weight LLMs and find that the dial produces monotonic changes in investment stance without modifying prompts or model parameters. At the response level, the dial shifts both investment decisions and the evidential emphasis of generated rationales under identical inputs. In an agentic retrieval setting, the dial also changes what information the model searches for, which evidence it selects, and which evidence is reflected in its final analysis. In a long-context evaluation, the dial maintains stable stance control as context length increases, whereas a matched system-prompt instruction progressively attenuates. We further show that changes in the dial propagate to security rankings and downstream portfolio composition in an exploratory backtest. Overall, our results show that an LLM's aggregate investment stance can be calibrated toward a specified target at inference time.

</details>


### [63] [CDEG: Learning Decision-Critical Evidence for Long-Horizon Diagnostic Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22899)
*Xiwei Dai,Zijie Meng,Zhiting Fan,Yixuan Tang,Ziru Niu,Zuozhu Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 9 pages, 5 figures

Selection reason: 从诊断轨迹中对比成败样本学习决策关键证据并构建图，超越轨迹级经验复用，可迁移至长程 agent 证据学习。

TL;DR: 提出CDEG，通过对比成功与失败轨迹识别并验证决策关键证据，构建诊断-证据-行动图，在长程诊断中显著提升准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医生智能体在长程诊断中常因关键证据缺失或未融入推理而失败；历史轨迹重用未验证证据的实际作用，且含噪声，导致提升有限。

Method: CDEG对比同一案例的成功/失败轨迹识别候选证据，用反事实干预验证其诊断影响，构建结构化图；推理时跟踪证据状态，检索关系以指导证据获取或重评。

Result: 在域内和分布外基准上，CDEG在多个医生智能体骨干上最高提升11.5%的准确率。

Conclusion: 可靠的长程诊断需超越轨迹级经验重用，转向证据级学习，聚焦真正影响临床决策的因素。

中文摘要：与静态医学问答不同，长程诊断捕捉了临床实践的序列特性：证据在多次交互中逐步获取、整合和评估，直到达成最终诊断。然而，现有的医生智能体常常在关键证据未被获取或未被充分纳入诊断推理时失败。近期的智能体方法尝试通过重用历史轨迹或提炼记忆来解决这些失败，但其诊断增益仍然受限，因为此类经验可能包含噪声或偶然信息，且通常未经验证哪些证据真正驱动诊断决策。为解决这一局限，我们提出了CDEG，一种基于图的框架，从历史诊断轨迹中学习可复用的决策关键证据。CDEG对比同一案例的成功与失败轨迹以识别候选证据，通过受控反事实干预验证其诊断影响，并将由此产生的诊断-证据-行动关系组织成结构化图。在推理时，CDEG跟踪演变的患者证据状态以检索相关诊断关系，并选择性指导缺失证据的获取或被忽视证据的重新评估。在包含多个医生智能体骨干的域内和分布外基准测试中，CDEG持续提升诊断性能，最高比原始智能体获得11.5%的准确率提升。这些结果表明，可靠的长程诊断需要超越轨迹层面的经验重用，转向证据层面的学习，关注真正影响临床决策的因素。

Original abstract: Unlike static medical question answering, long-horizon diagnosis captures the sequential nature of clinical practice: evidence is progressively acquired, integrated, and evaluated over multiple rounds of interaction before reaching a final diagnosis. However, existing doctor agents often fail when critical evidence is either not acquired or not adequately incorporated into diagnostic reasoning. Recent agentic approaches attempt to address these failures by reusing historical trajectories or distilled memories. But their diagnostic gains remain constrained because such experience may contain noisy or incidental information and is typically reused without validating which evidence actually drives diagnostic decisions. To address this limitation, we introduce CDEG, a graph-based framework that learns reusable decision-critical evidence from historical diagnostic trajectories. CDEG contrasts successful and failed trajectories from the same case to identify candidate evidence, validates their diagnostic impact through controlled counterfactual interventions, and organizes the resulting diagnosis--evidence--action relations into a structured graph. During inference, CDEG tracks the evolving patient evidence state to retrieve relevant diagnostic relations and selectively guide missing evidence acquisition or overlooked evidence reappraisal. Across in-domain and out-of-distribution benchmarks with multiple doctor agent backbones, CDEG consistently improves diagnostic performance, achieving up to an 11.5% accuracy gain over vanilla agents. These results demonstrate that reliable long-horizon diagnosis requires moving beyond trajectory-level experience reuse toward evidence-level learning of the factors that truly shape clinical decisions.

</details>


### [64] [What Process Evaluation of Coding Agents Actually Measures: Action, Task, and Step Are Three Different Levels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22960)
*Jiawei He,Mengyu Shi,Jie jia,Xikai Yang,Dong Sun*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 38 pages, 8 figures

Selection reason: 直接研究 coding agent 过程评估，区分动作/任务/步骤三级并做因果归因，对评测方法有直接价值。

TL;DR: 提出一种编码智能体过程评估的测量框架，通过SCAE实例化步骤级因果归因，实验表明当前评估多测语义相关性而非因果贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有过程级评估混淆了动作预测、任务不确定性和步骤归因，导致评估目标不明确，需要区分这些概念并提供清晰的测量框架。

Method: 引入测量框架，结合前缀条件识别、重放/干预式估计和受控评判信息操作，并提出基于结构因果模型的重放式因果估计器SCAE。

Result: 在12个代码库的499个文件定位片段上，发现下一步动作主要由执行来源驱动，执行不确定性在任务层面结构化，全轨迹评判存在系统性碰撞偏差。

Conclusion: 当前过程评估往往衡量语义相关性而非认证的因果贡献，需要更严格的因果归因方法。

中文摘要：编码智能体评估不仅关注是否解决任务，还关注如何执行任务。然而，现有的过程级评估常常将动作预测、任务不确定性和步骤归因视为同一问题，这使此类评估实际测量的内容变得不明确。在本文中，我们引入了一个用于编码智能体过程评估的测量框架，并通过SCAE（一种源自智能体执行结构因果模型的重放式估计器）实例化步骤级因果归因。我们的框架结合了前缀条件识别、重放/干预式估计以及受控的评判信息操作，以在动作、任务和步骤层面研究过程评估。在来自12个代码库的499个文件定位片段上的实验表明，下一步动作主要由执行来源而非代码图转换驱动，执行不确定性在任务层面而非步骤层面结构化，并且全轨迹评判者表现出系统性的碰撞偏差，这表明当前的过程评估通常衡量语义相关性而非经过认证的因果贡献。

Original abstract: Coding agents are increasingly evaluated not only by whether they solve a task, but also by how they execute it. However, existing process-level evaluations often treat action prediction, task uncertainty, and step attribution as if they were the same problem, which makes it unclear what such evaluations actually measure. In this paper, we introduce a measurement framework for process evaluation in coding agents and instantiate step-level causal attribution with SCAE, a replay-based estimator derived from a structural causal model of agent execution. Our framework combines prefix-conditioned identification, replay/intervention-based estimation, and controlled judge-information manipulation to study process evaluation at the action, task, and step levels. Experiments on 499 file-localization episodes from 12 repositories show that next actions are driven primarily by execution provenance rather than code-graph transitions, execution uncertainty is structured at the task rather than step level, and full-trace judges exhibit systematic collider bias, suggesting that current process evaluation often measures semantic relevance rather than certified causal contribution.

</details>


### [65] [Buried in Textual Debt: Context Pruning with Visual Evidence Preservation for MLLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22963)
*Yuchen Huang,Sijia Li,Jun Zhang,Yi R. Fung*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 14 pages, 2 figures, 4 tables

Selection reason: 针对多模态 agent 长轨迹文本债务提出 KL 引导的上下文剪枝，保留视觉证据，是通用上下文管理方法。

TL;DR: SPARE通过KL引导的摘要剪枝来移除多模态工具智能体中冗余的推理文本，在保持或提升准确率的同时显著减少上下文占用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态LLM作为多步智能体时，显式推理会累积自生成文本，长期轨迹中文本主导上下文并抑制视觉证据，形成“文本债务”；冗余推理和过时假设会误导后续推断，而现有剪枝方法可能丢失视觉证据。

Method: 提出SPARE框架，使用紧凑任务状态摘要作为特权诊断上下文；对每个候选片段在同一模型的原始与摘要条件下重放，通过反向KL散度（来自on-policy自蒸馏）判断摘要是否充分覆盖且不干扰未来推理；并用监督微调优化摘要器以支持更紧凑、覆盖更广和更激进的剪枝。

Result: 在多步视觉工具使用基准上，SPARE在剪枝方法中取得最高平均准确率，同时移除37.89%–64.58%的推理token，实现有利的准确率-上下文权衡。

Conclusion: 减少文本主导性可以恢复模型对视觉证据的依赖，缓解对自生成语言的过度条件化，从而在压缩上下文的同时提升或保持任务性能。

中文摘要：多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）越来越多地被部署为多步骤智能体，其中显式推理支持任务分解和工具协调，但也会累积自生成的文本。在较长的轨迹中，这些文本可能主导上下文并抑制视觉证据，从而产生“文本债务”（textual debt）。我们观察到，一旦任务相关的视觉证据被落地（grounded），推理就会变得冗余；而当落地仍然不确定时，过时的假设可能误导后续推理。因此，剪枝必须移除冗余文本，同时不丢弃视觉证据。我们提出SPARE，一种基于Kullback-Leibler（KL）散度的框架，用于剪枝多模态工具使用智能体中累积的推理。SPARE使用紧凑的任务状态摘要作为特权诊断上下文。对于每个候选片段，它在原始上下文和摘要条件上下文下重放同一模型。然后，来自on-policy自蒸馏（OPSD）的反向KL散度测试摘要是否充分覆盖该片段而不会破坏未来推理。我们进一步用监督微调（SFT）微调摘要器，使其能够生成更紧凑的摘要、更广泛的覆盖范围以及更激进的剪枝。在多项多步视觉工具使用基准上，SPARE在剪枝方法中实现了最高的平均准确率，同时移除了37.89%–64.58%的推理token。这种有利的准确率-上下文权衡表明，减少文本主导性恢复了对视觉证据的依赖，并缓解了对自生成语言的过度条件化。

Original abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) are increasingly deployed as multi-step agents, where explicit reasoning supports task decomposition and tool coordination but also accumulates self-generated text. Over long trajectories, this text can dominate the context and suppress visual evidence, creating textual debt. We observe that reasoning becomes redundant once task-relevant visual evidence is grounded, while stale hypotheses can misguide later inference when grounding remains uncertain. Pruning must therefore remove redundant text without discarding visual evidence. We propose SPARE, a Kullback--Leibler (KL)-guided framework for pruning accumulated reasoning in multimodal tool-use agents. SPARE uses a compact task-state summary as privileged diagnostic context. For each candidate segment, it replays the same model under the original and summary-conditioned contexts. Reverse-KL divergence from on-policy self-distillation (OPSD) then tests whether the summary sufficiently covers the segment without disrupting future reasoning. We further fine-tune the summarizer with supervised fine-tuning (SFT), enabling more compact summaries, broader coverage, and more aggressive pruning. Across multi-step visual tool-use benchmarks, SPARE achieves the highest average accuracy among pruning methods while removing 37.89--64.58\% of reasoning tokens. This favorable accuracy--context trade-off shows that reducing textual dominance restores reliance on visual evidence and mitigates over-conditioning on self-generated language.

</details>


### [66] [ParallelWorld: Test-Time Scaling for Embodied Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22971)
*Min Chen,Shengjun Zhang,Yuxin Li,Zhang Zhang,Xin Fei,Chong Xia,Yueqi Duan*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: Project Page: https://chen-min-22.github.io/ParallelWorld-page/

Selection reason: 多步前瞻树搜索与验证器引导的测试时扩展，可迁移至复杂 agent 规划。

TL;DR: ParallelWorld是一个用于具身推理的多水平测试时扩展框架，通过验证器引导的树搜索进行多步前瞻模拟，在复杂遮挡环境中优于单步贪心方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有主动推理方法缺乏长期规划，测试时扩展框架仅使用短视的单步前瞻，难以解决复杂遮挡空间中的延迟反馈问题。

Method: 提出ParallelWorld框架，采用验证器引导的树搜索范式：从当前状态并行展开多条未来轨迹，逐步骤用验证器评估中间状态转换，剪枝低价值分支并优先高信息增益路径，综合多步模拟结果后选择最优动作序列，再由答案智能体生成最终推理。

Result: 在ESI-Bench上的大量实验表明，ParallelWorld持续提升了主动感知和推理性能。

Conclusion: ParallelWorld通过多步前瞻并行模拟和验证器引导的树搜索，有效解决了单步前瞻的短视问题，显著增强了具身智能体的推理与感知能力。

中文摘要：具身推理构成了具身智能的基本能力，是自主感知、推理和在物理环境中交互的基础。近期研究已将具身推理的范式从静态感知转向动态探索，其中智能体通过与环境的交互获取任务相关信息。然而，现有的主动推理方法通常在没有长期规划的情况下增量生成探索轨迹。即使是最近出现的测试时扩展框架，也常常采用短视的单步前瞻，难以解决复杂、遮挡空间环境中固有的延迟反馈。为了解决这一局限，我们提出了ParallelWorld，一个用于具身推理的多水平测试时扩展框架。不同于贪婪的单步尝试，ParallelWorld使智能体能够在采取行动之前并行模拟和评估多步未来轨迹。具体来说，我们引入了一种验证器引导的树搜索范式。从当前状态开始，ParallelWorld分支为多条并行轨迹，并在多步水平上连续展开。在每个模拟步骤中，验证器智能体评估中间状态转换，动态剪枝无前途的分支，并优先考虑信息增益最高的路径。一旦多步前瞻模拟完成，智能体综合长期结果以确定最优动作序列。最后，答案智能体对选定轨迹进行推理以产生最终推理。在ESI-Bench上的大量实验表明，ParallelWorld持续改善了主动感知和推理性能。

Original abstract: Embodied Reasoning constitutes a fundamental capability of embodied intelligence, serving as the basis for autonomous perception, reasoning, and interaction within physical environments. Recent studies have shifted the paradigm of embodied reasoning from static perception toward dynamic exploration, where agents acquire task-relevant information through interactions with the environment. However, existing active reasoning approaches generally generate exploration trajectories incrementally without long-horizon planning. Even recently emerged test-time scaling frameworks often resort to myopic, single-step lookaheads, which struggle to resolve the delayed feedback inherent in complex, occluded spatial environments. To address this limitation, we propose ParallelWorld, a multi-horizon test-time scaling framework for embodied reasoning. Instead of greedy, single-step trials, ParallelWorld empowers agents to simulate and evaluate multi-step future trajectories in parallel before committing to an action. Specifically, we introduce a verifier-guided tree-search paradigm. Starting from the current state, ParallelWorld branches into multiple parallel trajectories and rolls them out continuously across a multi-step horizon. At each simulation step, a verifier agent evaluates the intermediate state transitions, dynamically pruning unpromising branches and prioritizing paths with the highest information gain. Once the multi-step prospective simulation is complete, the agent synthesizes the long-horizon outcomes to commit to the optimal action sequence. Finally, an answer agent performs reasoning over the selected trajectory to produce the final reasoning. Extensive experiments on ESI-Bench demonstrate that ParallelWorld consistently improves active perception and reasoning performance.

</details>


### [67] [Toward Effective and Reliable LLM Agents via Dynamic Ontology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22974)
*Xiaohui Zhang,Zequn Sun,Chengyuan Yang,Yuanning Cui,Lingbing Guo,Wei Hu*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 动态构造任务本体并迭代优化，为 LLM agent 提供结构化知识，增强多步推理可靠性。

TL;DR: OaK 是一个动态构建和细化面向任务本体的框架，通过显式化概念与关系来增强 LLM 代理的证据基础和推理可靠性，在多个基准上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 代理过度依赖参数化知识或非结构化上下文，导致领域任务中语义连接隐式化，造成证据使用不完整和决策脆弱；现有本体构建需专家参与且难以规模化，自动构建又缺乏对决策所需关系结构的保证。

Method: OaK 框架根据任务需求和训练数据动态构建本体及知识图谱，生成图推理的自适应函数，并通过评判反馈迭代优化本体和函数，使相关概念和关系显式化以支撑检索和多步决策。

Result: 在 TravelPlanner、CRMArenaPro 和 ToolQA 上的实验表明，OaK 相比标准 LLM 代理显著提升性能，强化了证据基础，并增强了多步推理的可靠性。

Conclusion: OaK 验证了将本体作为内核动态集成到 LLM 代理中的可行性，为提升领域任务的语义理解与决策质量提供了一种可扩展且有效的解决方案。

中文摘要：大型语言模型（LLM）代理在很大程度上依赖于模型参数中编码的知识或作为非结构化上下文呈现的信息。在特定领域的任务中，这会使重要的语义连接变得隐含，常常导致证据使用不完整和脆弱的多步决策。本体论提供了一种将领域概念和关系外部化为机器可解释结构的方法，但传统上构建任务可用的本体需要领域专家的大量努力，且难以扩展。自动构建也面临挑战：一个看起来语义上合理的本体可能不包含实际决策所需的 relational 结构。我们提出了 OaK，一个将本体作为内核（ontology-as-a-kernel）的框架，能够为 LLM 代理动态构建和细化面向任务的本体。给定任务需求和训练数据，OaK 构建本体及其知识图谱，生成用于图推理的任务自适应函数，并利用评判反馈迭代优化两者。通过使相关概念和关系显式化，本体为知识检索和多步决策提供了基础。我们在 TravelPlanner、CRMArenaPro 和 ToolQA 上评估了 OaK。结果表明，OaK 改进了标准 LLM 代理，增强了证据基础，并提高了多步推理的可靠性。

Original abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents rely heavily on knowledge encoded in model parameters or presented as unstructured context. In domain-specific tasks, this leaves important semantic connections implicit. This often results in incomplete evidence use and brittle multi-step decisions. Ontologies offer a way to externalize domain concepts and relations as machine-interpretable structures, but constructing task-usable ontologies traditionally requires substantial effort from domain experts and is difficult to scale. Automatic construction is also challenging: an ontology that appears semantically plausible may not contain the relational structures needed for actual decision making. We present OaK, an ontology-as-a-kernel framework that dynamically constructs and refines task-oriented ontologies for LLM agents. Given task requirements and training data, OaK constructs an ontology and its knowledge graph, generates task-adaptation functions for graph reasoning, and uses judge feedback to iteratively refine both. By making relevant concepts and relations explicit, the ontology grounds knowledge retrieval and multi-step decision making. We evaluate OaK on TravelPlanner, CRMArenaPro, and ToolQA. Results show that OaK improves standard LLM agents, strengthens evidence grounding, and boosts the reliability of multi-step reasoning.

</details>


### [68] [Budget-Constrained Embodied Perception: Four Resource Walls and a Pre-Registered Evaluation of Access-Structured Perception on Open Models at less than 31B](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22975)
*Defu Lin,Wenhui Chen,Ziyao Lin,Jianlin Chen,Peiji Long,Chi Man Vong*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 面向固定 token 预算的具身感知 agent，提出无训练访问机制与预注册评测，对资源受限 agent 的设计有参考价值

TL;DR: ASP 在固定 token 预算下通过查询条件访问显著提升检索准确率，但完整架构的在线压缩组件未通过预设证伪检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身多模态代理在固定预算下处理增长观察流面临四类资源约束，现有方法缺少对这种约束的系统形式化和训练无关的解决方案。

Method: 提出 ASP：免训练包装器，结合有上限结构化状态、逐字情景索引和查询条件预算分配与迭代访问；在 SEW-Bench 上按预注册协议评估 7 个模型。

Result: 预算 4,096 token 时 ASP 的检索准确率 75-94%，远高于同等预算的查询无关采样 3-19%；预算重分配优于四倍采样预算。但移除压缩状态反而使旗舰均值从 35.4 升至 58.0，且 ASP 未超过仅逐字基线，触发两项证伪标准。

Conclusion: 固定预算下决定性因素是查询条件访问机制而非参数规模或单纯上下文增长；提示式在线压缩未体现价值。

中文摘要：具身多模态代理必须在每步决策的固定 token 预算下，从不断增长的观察流中进行回答。我们通过四堵资源墙来形式化这一约束：用于有界状态的感知香农墙、用于与查询无关的帧选择的视界墙、用于非自适应检索的轮次墙，以及用于固定深度推理的条件组合墙。我们提出 ASP，一种用于冻结多模态模型的免训练包装器，它结合了有上限的结构化状态、逐字逐句的情境索引，以及带迭代访问的查询条件预算分配。遵循预先注册的协议，我们在 SEW-Bench 上评估了七个从 3B 到 31B 的开权重模型；SEW-Bench 是一个无许可证的合成长时程漫游基准，专为实例化这些墙而构建。已注册的自然视频基准未运行，因为其帧需要数据集协议；因此我们的证据涉及访问机制，而非自然场景感知。在 4,096-token 的决策预算下，ASP 达到了 75% 到 94% 的情景检索准确率，而同等预算的与查询无关采样的准确率为 3% 到 19%；并且在每个骨干网络上，预算重新分配都优于将采样预算增加四倍。然而，完整的三组件架构并未验证通道对偶性：去除压缩状态使旗舰平均分从 35.4 升至 58.0，ASP 在任何骨干网络上都不优于仅逐字逐句的基线，并且四个预先注册的证伪标准中有两个被触发。这些结果表明，在固定预算下，查询条件访问而非参数规模或上下文增长单独起决定作用，而在此设置中，提示式在线压缩并未挣得它的成本。

Original abstract: Embodied multimodal agents must answer from growing observation streams under a fixed per-decision token budget. We formalize this constraint through four resource walls: a perceptual Shannon wall for bounded state, a horizon wall for query-independent frame selection, a round wall for non-adaptive retrieval, and a conditional composition wall for fixed-depth inference. We introduce ASP, a training-free wrapper for frozen multimodal models that combines a capped structured state, a verbatim episodic index, and query-conditioned budget allocation with iterative access. Following a pre-registered protocol, we evaluate seven open-weight models from 3B to 31B on SEW-Bench, a license-free synthetic long-horizon walkthrough benchmark constructed to instantiate these walls. The registered natural-video benchmarks were not run because their frames require dataset agreements; our evidence therefore concerns access mechanisms, not natural-scene perception. Under a 4,096-token decision budget, ASP reaches 75 to 94% episodic retrieval accuracy, compared with 3 to 19% for equal-budget query-independent sampling, and budget reallocation outperforms quadrupling the sampling budget on every backbone. However, the full three-component architecture does not validate channel duality: removing the compressive state raises the flagship mean from 35.4 to 58.0, ASP does not outperform the verbatim-only baseline on any backbone, and two of four pre-registered falsification criteria fire. These results show that query-conditioned access, rather than parameter count or context growth alone, is decisive under a fixed budget, while prompted online compression does not earn its cost in this setting.

</details>


### [69] [PsychJail: Exploring Psychological Jailbreaks via Multi-Turn Persuasion of LLM Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23028)
*Zeyu Feng,Qingyu Wu,Yuzhe Luo,Hua Cheng*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 心理学驱动的多轮越狱攻击框架，涉及轨迹级强化学习与策略优化，可迁移到 agent 安全红队测试

TL;DR: 提出了一种心理学引导的多轮越狱框架PsychJail，利用说服知识模型和强化学习，在四个对齐LLM上实现了87.3%的平均攻击成功率，并揭示了模型的心理脆弱性指纹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有越狱研究多关注单轮或迭代攻击，忽视了多轮说服的心理机制。随着LLM在交互场景中的持续对话使用，心理多轮越狱漏洞成为一个重要的安全威胁。

Method: PsychJail将社会心理学说服技术映射为策略条件攻击策略，将每步动作分解为意义变更分析、策略选择和受害者可见消息，使用PKM门控奖励的轨迹级强化学习优化策略。

Result: 在四个对齐受害者模型上，PsychJail达到最高的平均攻击成功率87.3%，在每个模型上均优于单轮和多轮基线，并揭示了四种模型级心理指纹以解释跨模型迁移不对称性。

Conclusion: 心理越狱是日益交互式LLM的一个独特红队前沿，需要进一步验证心理画像解释。

中文摘要：大型语言模型（LLMs）越来越多地部署在教育、医疗、政策咨询以及其他交互式场景中，在这些场景中，用户将其视为持续的社交对话者，而非一次性的查询引擎。这一转变使得越狱攻击成为一种日益严重的安全威胁，然而大多数研究侧重于单轮提示优化或迭代攻击改进，使得基于心理学的多轮漏洞未被充分探索。我们提出了PsychJail，一种通过理论支撑的多轮说服对对齐LLM进行红队测试的心理学引导框架。PsychJail将既定的社会心理学说服技术映射为基于策略条件的攻击策略。它将每个攻击者动作分解为意义变更（Change-of-Meaning）分析、策略选择和对受害者可见的消息，从而操作化说服知识模型（PKM）。该策略通过轨迹级强化学习进行优化，使用PKM门控奖励，仅当每一轮都包含结构良好的意义变更分析时，才将早期的越狱成功归因于奖励。在四个对齐的受害者模型上，PsychJail实现了最高的平均攻击成功率（87.3%），并且在每个模型上都优于强大的单轮和多轮基线。我们还测量了使每个受害者被攻破的动作处的敏感性，揭示了四种不同的模型级指纹，这些指纹识别了哪些说服杠杆影响每个模型及其影响广度。这些指纹有助于解释跨模型迁移的不对称性。我们将其解释为四种候选心理画像——理性主义者、可信度驱动型、叙事单一文化型和广泛可说型——同时将此解释视为需要未来验证的猜想。我们的研究结果确立了心理越狱作为日益交互式LLM的一个独特红队前沿。

Original abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed in education, healthcare, policy advising, and other interactive settings, where users engage them as sustained social interlocutors rather than one-shot query engines. This shift makes jailbreaks a growing safety threat, yet most research emphasizes single-turn prompt optimization or iterative attack refinement, leaving psychologically grounded multi-turn vulnerabilities underexplored. We present PsychJail, a psychology-guided framework for red teaming aligned LLMs through theory-grounded, multi-turn persuasion. PsychJail maps established social-psychological persuasion techniques into a tactic-conditioned attack policy. It factorizes each attacker action into a Change-of-Meaning analysis, tactic selection, and victim-visible message, operationalizing the Persuasion Knowledge Model (PKM). The policy is refined with trajectory-level reinforcement learning using a PKM-gated reward that credits early jailbreak success only when every turn contains a well-formed Change-of-Meaning analysis. Across four aligned victim models, PsychJail achieves the highest average attack success rate (87.3%) and outperforms strong single-turn and multi-turn baselines on every model. We also measure susceptibility at the action that breaks each victim, revealing four distinct model-level fingerprints that identify which persuasion levers affect each model and how broadly. These fingerprints help explain cross-model transfer asymmetry. We interpret them as four candidate psychological profiles-rationalist, credibility-driven, narrative-monoculture, and broadly persuadable-while treating this interpretation as a conjecture requiring future validation. Our findings establish psychological jailbreaks as a distinct red-teaming frontier for increasingly interactive LLMs.

</details>


### [70] [Artificial Empathy: Towards a Framework for Unsupervised Agency Detection and Policy Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23030)
*Peter Kuhn,Chris Pang,Sonakshi Chauhan*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 提出从观测中检测与重建其他 agent 策略的通用框架，对多 agent 协作场景有潜在启发

TL;DR: 提出一个利用独立任务训练的强化学习智能体作为先验，通过观察识别和建模其他智能体的框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界中AI系统需要识别和建模其他智能体以实现合作，而现有方法（如逆强化学习）约束更强且该问题尚未被充分探索。

Method: 提出一个框架，使用在独立任务上训练的强化学习智能体作为智能体动态的先验，进行智能体检测和策略重建。

Result: 摘要中未提供具体实验结果或性能评估。

Conclusion: 该框架为从观察中识别和建模其他智能体提供了新途径，但需要进一步实验验证其有效性。

中文摘要：我们研究AI系统如何仅凭观察来识别和模拟其环境中的其他智能体，这是在现实世界中实现合作行为所必需的能力。这个问题比逆强化学习的约束更少，且在很大程度上尚未被探索。我们提出了一个框架，该框架使用一个在独立任务上训练的强化学习智能体作为智能体动态的先验，来执行智能体检测和策略重建。

Original abstract: We study how an AI system can identify and model other agents in its environment from observation alone, which is a capability necessary for cooperative behaviour in the real world. This problem is less constrained than inverse reinforcement learning and remains largely unexplored. We propose a framework that uses a reinforcement learning agent, trained on an independent task as a prior about agentic dynamics, to perform agency detection and policy reconstruction.

</details>


### [71] [MobilePA-Bench: Benchmarking Mobile Planner Agents on Complex Real-World Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23035)
*Yi Zhu,Xiongwei Wu,Qiyi Wang,Tingyu Qu,Jiajun Liu,Sihan Cao,Long Chen,Weigao Sun,Feida Zhu,Yiran Zhong,Steven Hoi*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 移动 planner agent 的交互式 benchmark，覆盖工具调用、子 agent 协作、记忆与技能复用，方法论可直接迁移到 coding agent

TL;DR: MobilePA-Bench是一个交互式、有状态、以工具为中心的基准，用于评估移动规划代理的工具调用和规划能力，覆盖13个领域212个工具，并测试子代理协作、记忆使用和技能使用三个高级维度，发现现有LLM在严格约束下性能显著下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有移动代理基准存在两类盲点：GUI基准忽略后台工具和长程规划，静态函数调用基准脱离真实运行时约束。需要弥合这一差距的基准。

Method: 构建可执行沙盒，维护实时数据库并返回结构化反馈；基于13个功能领域和212个移动工具，设计中心代理评估框架，包含子代理协作、记忆使用和技能使用三个高级维度；结合交互式函数调用环境与基于证据的验证。

Result: 实验表明前沿LLM在移动设置中仍不可靠，在严格工具顺序、权限限制和意外运行时错误下性能急剧下降。

Conclusion: MobilePA-Bench既是实际诊断基准，也是智能体强化学习的交互基础，可加速可靠移动代理的开发。

中文摘要：随着设备端LLM代理演变为个人助手，移动操作系统已成为该范式的关键测试平台，因此严格的能力评估至关重要。然而，现有基准分为两类，各自存在关键盲点：以GUI为中心的基准测试表面级的屏幕操作，而忽视了后台工具使用和长程规划；而静态函数调用基准则依赖离线API匹配，脱离了真实运行时约束。为弥补这一差距，我们提出了MobilePA-Bench，一个交互式、有状态、以工具为中心的基准，用于评估移动规划代理的工具调用和规划能力。MobilePA-Bench运行在可执行的沙盒环境中，该沙盒维护实时应用数据库并返回结构化反馈，覆盖13个功能领域和212个真实移动工具。除了基础工具使用外，它从三个高级维度评估中心规划代理：（1）子代理协作——将复杂任务分解并委派给有能力的子代理；（2）记忆使用——回忆存储的记忆、用户档案和过往偏好以解决隐式请求；（3）技能使用——调用预打包的复合技能，而非从头规划每一步。大量实验表明，当前前沿LLM在移动环境中仍不可靠：在严格的工具排序、权限限制和意外运行时错误下，性能急剧下降。通过将交互式函数调用沙盒与基于证据的验证相结合，MobilePA-Bench既可作为实用的诊断基准，也可作为智能体强化学习的交互基础，加速可靠移动代理的开发。

Original abstract: As on-device LLM agents evolve into personal copilots, the mobile operating system has become a key testbed for this paradigm, making rigorous capability evaluation essential. Yet existing benchmarks fall into two camps, each with a critical blind spot: GUI-centric benchmarks test surface-level screen manipulation while overlooking background tool use and long-horizon planning, whereas static function-calling benchmarks rely on offline API matching that is detached from real runtime constraints. To close this gap, we present \textbf{MobilePA-Bench}, an interactive, stateful, and tool-centric benchmark for evaluating the tool-calling and planning abilities of mobile planning agents. MobilePA-Bench runs on an executable sandbox that maintains live application databases and returns structured feedback, spanning $13$ functional domains and $212$ realistic mobile tools. Beyond basic tool use, it evaluates a central planning agent along three advanced dimensions: \emph{(1)~Sub-agent Collaboration}---decomposing a complex task and delegating specialized work to capable sub-agents; \emph{(2)~Memory Usage}---recalling stored memories, user profiles, and past preferences to resolve implicit requests; and \emph{(3)~Skill Usage}---invoking pre-packaged composite skills instead of planning every step from scratch. Extensive experiments show that current frontier LLMs remain unreliable in mobile settings: performance drops sharply under strict tool ordering, permission limits, and unexpected runtime errors. By pairing an interactive function-calling sandbox with evidence-based verification, MobilePA-Bench serves as both a practical diagnostic benchmark and an interactive foundation for agentic reinforcement learning---accelerating the development of dependable mobile agents.

</details>


### [72] [From Inertia to Objectivity: Improving Deep Research Agents with Noise Isolation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23045)
*Xiangxin Zhang,Zhanwei Zhang,Zhihang Fu,Binbin Lin,Wenxiao Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: EMNLP 2026

Selection reason: 深度研究 agent 的惯性偏差分析与上下文隔离方法，降低 token 成本并提升客观性，可迁移到长程 coding agent

TL;DR: 本文识别了LLM网络搜索智能体中的“惯性偏差”问题，提出IBIS基准进行测量，并设计NIS-Agent通过上下文隔离和内在鲁棒性训练来缓解，在多个深度研究基准上实现与GPT-4o相当的性能且显著降低令牌成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度研究任务中，LLM智能体容易因“即兴”行动历史而产生判断偏差，即在评估自身先前决策结果时失去客观性，导致搜索和推理质量下降。现有方法未系统性地测量或解决这一问题，因此需要可量化的基准和有效的缓解策略。

Method: 首先提出IBIS基准，通过控制搜索观察来量化惯性偏差；然后提出NIS-Agent，在网页分类和最终答案验证两个关键决策点实施上下文隔离，切断智能体与自身先前行动的干扰；此外，还训练了一个8B规模的模型，使其在架构层面内在地抵抗惯性偏差，并与NIS-Agent框架协同工作。

Result: 实验表明，模型在评估自身行动时性能显著下降，证实了惯性偏差的存在。NIS-Agent在GAIA、WebWalkerQA、BrowseComp和BrowseComp-zh上达到竞争性性能，同时令牌成本相比基线降低33%；训练后的8B模型在相同框架下平均性能接近GPT-4o，证明了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 惯性偏差是深度研究智能体中的系统性缺陷，但通过上下文隔离和内在鲁棒性训练可以显著缓解。NIS-Agent提供了一种高效且性能优越的方案，为构建更客观、更经济的网络搜索智能体奠定了基础。

中文摘要：由大型语言模型（LLM）驱动的网络搜索智能体展现出强大的潜力，但深度研究任务暴露了一个反复出现的失败模式：一旦智能体生成了查询、计划或中间结论，它在之后判断同一行动的结果时就会变得不那么客观。我们将这种现象称为“惯性偏差”。为了使其可测量，我们引入了IBIS基准，该基准控制搜索观察结果，同时改变模型是否在评估自身先前行动的结果。我们发现，当模型“拥有”之前的搜索步骤时，其表现会明显更差，这表明自我撰写的行动历史会系统性地扭曲后续判断。我们进一步表明，这种偏差会传播为两种系统层面的退化：工人层面的搜索噪声和管理者层面的上下文噪声。为了解决这个问题，我们提出了NIS-Agent，它在最易受惯性偏差影响的两个决策点应用上下文隔离：网页分类和最终答案验证。在GAIA、WebWalkerQA、BrowseComp和BrowseComp-zh上，NIS-Agent在将令牌成本降低33%的同时实现了有竞争力的性能。我们还训练了一个8B模型，使其对惯性偏差具有内在更强的抵抗力；在相同的NIS-Agent框架下，它在深度研究基准上的平均性能可与GPT-4o相媲美。

Original abstract: Web search agents powered by Large Language Models (LLMs) show strong promise, but deep research tasks expose a recurring failure mode: once an agent has produced a query, plan, or intermediate conclusion, it becomes less objective when later judging the consequences of that same action. We term this phenomenon \textbf{inertia bias}. To make it measurable, we introduce the IBIS benchmark, which controls the search observations while varying whether the model is evaluating the outcome of its own prior action. We find that models are substantially worse when they ``own'' the preceding search step, showing that self-authored action history can systematically distort subsequent judgment. We further show that this bias propagates into two forms of system-level degradation: search noise at the worker level and contextual noise at the manager level. To address this problem, we propose NIS-Agent, which applies context isolation at the two decision points most vulnerable to inertia bias: webpage triage and final-answer validation. Across GAIA, WebWalkerQA, BrowseComp, and BrowseComp-zh, NIS-Agent achieves competitive performance while reducing token cost by 33\% compared to our baseline. We further train an 8B model to be intrinsically more resistant to inertia bias; under the same NIS-Agent framework, it attains average performance comparable to GPT-4o on deep research benchmarks.

</details>


### [73] [LLM-based Agents for Forecasting and Prediction: Methods, Training, Evaluation, and Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23058)
*Xiaogang Xu,Jiaqi Tang,Jianmin Chen,Yingying Yan,Zhenchao Tang,Xiangxin Zhou,Xiaobin Hu,Wei Wei,Jinfeng Wu,Qifeng Chen,Lu Zhou,Jiafei Wu,Zhe Liu,Jianwei Yin,Weimin Zheng*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: LLM 预测 agent 的综述，梳理 standalone/tool-augmented/hybrid 架构与评测协议，适合作为 agent 设计参考

TL;DR: 本文综述了基于LLM的预测智能体，将架构分为独立LLM、工具/检索增强和混合系统三类，并审查了训练、评估、证据及局限性，指出测量是核心限制，未来需关注校准、抗污染评估和成本准确性报告。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有预测系统结合LLM的推理能力和时间数据、工具等，但缺乏系统的组织和分析。本文旨在梳理LLM预测智能体的架构、训练方法、评估协议、证据和应用，以明确当前进展和核心挑战。

Method: 通过文献综述方式，将LLM预测智能体架构分为三类：独立LLM工作流、工具/检索增强智能体、LLM与统计/基础模型的混合系统；回顾训练方法、评估协议，并检查正负面证据（如扰动敏感性、消融无增益、基准污染），同时覆盖多领域应用和基准数据集。

Result: 证据表明测量是核心限制；存在LLM组件不提升准确性的消融情况，基准增益可能反映污染而非时间推理，且对输入扰动敏感。

Conclusion: 未来工作需解决分布偏移下的校准、开发抗污染的实时评估、同时报告成本和准确性，并处理预测反馈与预测结果之间的相互作用。

中文摘要：大型语言模型（LLM）现在支持将基于语言的推理与时间数据、证据检索、外部工具和迭代预测相结合的预测系统。我们研究了基于LLM的预测智能体，即语言模型有助于对未来或当前未观测目标进行评分预测的系统。我们将架构分为三组。独立的LLM工作流处理编码的时间序列或事件上下文。工具增强和检索增强的智能体整合外部证据。混合系统将LLM与统计模型或基础模型配对。然后我们回顾了训练方法和评估协议。我们检查了负面和正面证据，包括对小输入扰动的敏感性、LLM组件未能提高准确性的消融实验，以及可能反映污染而非时间推理的基准增益。我们涵盖了金融、天气、健康、能源和运营中的应用，并总结了用于评估的基准和数据集。证据表明，测量是一个核心限制。未来的工作需要分布偏移下的校准、抗污染的实时评估、成本和准确性显式一起报告，以及处理部署预测与所预测结果之间反馈的方法。

Original abstract: Large language models (LLMs) now support forecasting systems that combine language-based reasoning with temporal data, evidence retrieval, external tools, and iterative prediction. We investigate LLM-based forecasting agents, meaning systems in which a language model contributes to a scored prediction about a future or currently unobserved target. We organize architectures into three groups. Standalone LLM workflows operate on encoded time series or event context. Tool- and retrieval-augmented agents incorporate external evidence. Hybrid systems pair LLMs with statistical or foundation models. We then review training methods and evaluation protocols. We examine negative as well as positive evidence, including sensitivity to small input perturbations, ablations in which the LLM component does not improve accuracy, and benchmark gains that may reflect contamination instead of temporal reasoning. We cover applications in finance, weather, health, energy, and operations, and we summarize the benchmarks and datasets used for evaluation. The evidence indicates that measurement is a central limitation. Future work requires calibration under distribution shift, contamination-resistant live evaluation, explicit reporting of cost and accuracy together, and methods for handling feedback between deployed forecasts and the outcomes being forecast.

</details>


### [74] [AgentWeave: Routing Before Reasoning for Efficient Function Calling in Tool-Rich Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23078)
*Saurav Singla,Aarav Singla,Advik Gupta,Parnika Gupta*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 12 pages, 2 figures, 6 tables. Open-source implementation and reproducibility artifacts available in the AgentWeave repository

Selection reason: 在函数调用前进行确定性工具路由，显著降低 token 与延迟并改善行为，是 coding agent 可直接采用的方法

TL;DR: AgentWeave 在推理前通过确定性路由层缩减候选工具集，使得固定函数调用模型在BFCL V4任务上从0成功提升到12.5%，同时显著减少工具数、输入token和延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着工具和函数数量激增，函数调用模型需处理过多模式，导致更高token消耗和相似干扰项。现有方法多关注模型本身，忽略了在推理前主动缩减候选动作空间这一互补系统策略。

Method: 提出AgentWeave，一个确定性预推理路由层，利用资格、需求、能力和路由信号构建有界模型可见动作空间，并冻结下游模型不变。在BFCL派生路由压力协议下，使用MadeAgents/Hammer2.1-1.5b模型与所有工具、随机top-8、语义top-8基线对比。

Result: 在48个BFCL V4多函数任务中，AgentWeave获得6/48成功，基线均为0/48；配对提升+12.5个百分点，95%置信区间[+4.17,+22.92]，p=0.03125。同时工具数减少70.18%，输入token减少61.70%，延迟降低50.95%。

Conclusion: 候选空间构建能实质性改变固定模型的函数调用行为，即使绝对成功率仍低，也说明将路由作为模型推理之前独立评估阶段是有价值的。

中文摘要：大型语言模型越来越多地操作大量工具、函数、API和专门的智能体集合。随着候选动作空间的增长，函数调用模型必须处理更多模式、消耗更多提示词元，并在越来越相似或不相关的备选项中区分。我们研究一种互补的系统策略：在语言模型推理之前减少候选集，同时保持下游模型不变。我们引入了AgentWeave，一个确定性的推理前路由层，它使用资格、需求、能力和路由信号构建一个有界模型可见动作空间。我们使用冻结的BFCL派生路由压力协议，以及公开的MadeAgents/Hammer2.1-1.5b模型来评估AgentWeave。在48个新的BFCL V4多函数任务中，AgentWeave实现了6/48（12.5%）原生BFCL成功，而所有工具、确定性随机前8和语义前8基线均达到0/48。配对成功差异为+12.5个百分点，10,000次重采样配对自助法95%置信区间为+4.17至+22.92个百分点，精确McNemar p=0.03125。相对于所有工具暴露，AgentWeave减少了70.18%的工具，使用减少了61.70%的输入词元，并表现出50.95%更低的平均本地模型延迟。结果刻意狭窄：这是一项BFCL派生的路由压力研究，而非官方的完整BFCL排行榜分数，绝对任务成功仍然较低。然而，证据表明，候选空间构建可以实质性影响固定模型的函数调用行为，并激励将路由作为模型推理之前的一个独立阶段进行评估。

Original abstract: Large language models increasingly operate over large collections of tools, functions, APIs, and specialized agents. As the candidate action space grows, a function-calling model must process more schemas, consume more prompt tokens, and distinguish among increasingly similar or irrelevant alternatives. We study a complementary systems strategy: reduce the candidate set before language-model inference while leaving the downstream model unchanged. We introduce AgentWeave, a deterministic pre-inference routing layer that constructs a bounded model-visible action space using eligibility, requirement, capability, and routing signals. We evaluate AgentWeave with a frozen BFCL-derived routing-pressure protocol using the public MadeAgents/Hammer2.1-1.5b model. On 48 fresh BFCL V4 multiple-function tasks, AgentWeave achieves 6/48 (12.5%) native BFCL successes, whereas all-tools, deterministic random top-8, and semantic top-8 baselines each achieve 0/48. The paired success difference is +12.5 percentage points with a 10,000-resample paired bootstrap 95% confidence interval of +4.17 to +22.92 points and exact McNemar p=0.03125. Relative to all-tools exposure, AgentWeave presents 70.18% fewer tools, uses 61.70% fewer input tokens, and exhibits 50.95% lower mean local-model latency. The result is deliberately narrow: this is a BFCL-derived routing-pressure study rather than an official full BFCL leaderboard score, and absolute task success remains low. The evidence nevertheless shows that candidate-space construction can materially affect a fixed model's function-calling behavior and motivates evaluating routing as a distinct stage before model reasoning.

</details>


### [75] [POOL: Propagated Uncertainty Over Lookalikes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23086)
*Rounak Sharma,Ananya B. Sai,Soumyabrata Pal*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 面向黑盒 LLM 的低成本置信度估计框架，通过 lookalike 传播节省采样，可用于 agent 的可靠性选路与验证

TL;DR: 提出一个受群体测试启发的置信度估计框架 POOL 和混合估计器 Hy@p，在保持较高 AUROC 的同时大幅减少生成样本，从而缓解置信度估计中的成本-质量权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有置信度估计器存在成本-质量权衡：口头置信度便宜但易过度自信，采样不确定性更准确但成本随样本数线性增长；黑盒 LLM 需要一种既能有效区分正确/错误输出又成本高效的置信度方案。

Method: POOL 利用重叠聚类将查询分组，在代表性 medoid 上评估基础估计器，软传播置信度到邻近查询，并选择性处理高分歧案例；其 Hy@p 估计器结合口头置信度与基于负冯·诺依曼熵的谱答案多样性。

Result: 在 3 个数据集、6 个领域、5 个黑盒 LLM 上，Hy@5 平均 AUROC 高于口头置信度和 Vn@10，且样本量仅为 Vn@10 的一半；POOL-Hy@5 保留 93.5-97.9% 的 AUROC，节省 19.3-39.3% 的生成，释义密集任务上生成节省达 73-76%。

Conclusion: 语义冗余可以被有效利用来降低置信度估计成本，POOL 框架为黑盒 LLM 提供了一种成本高效的置信度估计手段，有助于在资源受限时实现可靠的不确定性管理。

中文摘要：黑盒大语言模型需要能够区分可能正确与可能错误输出的置信度分数，从而使系统能够优先进行人工审查、将不确定案例路由到更强模型，或在开发数据上选择弃权阈值。然而，现有的置信度估计器面临成本-质量权衡：口头置信度（verbal confidence）廉价但往往过度自信，而基于采样的不确定性更具信息量，但会随每个查询的样本数量线性扩展。我们提出 POOL（Propagated Uncertainty Over Lookalikes，基于相似体的传播不确定性），这是一个受群体测试（group-testing）启发的成本高效框架，用于解决这一权衡。POOL 对查询主干（query stems）进行重叠聚类，在代表性 medoid 上评估基础估计器，将置信度分数软传播到邻近查询，并选择性地评估高分歧案例。我们用 Hy@p 实例化该框架，这是一种混合估计器，将口头置信度与由采样答案嵌入的负冯·诺依曼熵（negative von Neumann entropy）计算出的谱答案多样性（spectral answer diversity）相结合。在来自三个数据集的六个领域和五个黑盒 LLM 上，Hy@5 实现了比口头置信度和 Vn@10 采样更高的平均 AUROC，同时使用的样本数量仅为 Vn@10 的一半。POOL-Hy@5 保留了其 AUROC 的 93.5--97.9%，同时节省了 19.3--39.3% 的生成量。在释义密集（paraphrase-dense）的工作负载上，生成节省提升至 73-76%，表明语义冗余可用于降低置信度估计成本。

Original abstract: Black-box large language models need confidence scores that can separate likely-correct from likely-incorrect outputs, enabling systems to prioritize human review, route uncertain cases to stronger models, or choose abstention thresholds on development data. Yet existing confidence estimators face a cost-quality trade-off: verbal confidence is cheap but is often overconfident, while sampling-based uncertainty is more informative but scales linearly with the number of samples per query. We propose \textsc{POOL} (\emph{Propagated Uncertainty Over Lookalikes}),a cost-efficient framework that addresses this trade-off taking inspiration from group-testing.\textsc{POOL} clusters query stems with overlaps, evaluates a base estimator on representative medoids, softly propagates confidence scores to nearby queries, and selectively evaluates high-disagreement cases. We instantiate this framework with \textsc{Hy@}$p$, a hybrid estimator that combines verbal confidence with spectral answer diversity computed from the negative von Neumann entropy of sampled answer embeddings.Across six domains from three datasets and five black-box LLMs, \textsc{Hy@}5 achieves higher average AUROC than verbal confidence and \textsc{Vn@}10 sampling while using half as many samples as \textsc{Vn@}10. \textsc{POOL}-\textsc{Hy@}5 retains 93.5--97.9\% of its AUROC while saving 19.3--39.3\% of generations. On paraphrase-dense workloads, generation savings rise to 73-76\%, showing that semantic redundancy can be leveraged to lower confidence-estimation costs.

</details>


### [76] [Cognitive Profiling of LRMs' Reasoning Traces Using Bloom's Taxonomy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23205)
*Maria-Eleni Zoumpoulidi,Georgios Paraskevopoulos,Alexandros Potamianos*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 基于 Bloom 分类法的推理轨迹认知画像，可辅助分析 agent 推理过程并定位正确性相关模式

TL;DR: 我们提出基于布鲁姆分类法的推理步骤自动标注框架，分析大型推理模型的思维模式，并发现思维类型与正确性相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解大型推理模型在推理过程中使用的思维类型（如记忆、应用、评估）对于洞察推理模式和实现可应用改进至关重要，但目前尚缺乏系统研究。

Method: 引入基于布鲁姆分类法的框架，将推理步骤自动标注为六个认知层次，并在多个模型和数据集上进行大规模分析。

Result: 揭示了不同模型和任务在思维模式上的相似性与差异性，并证明思维类型信息与推理正确性相关。

Conclusion: 建立了细粒度的思维分析框架，为增强推理质量提供了可操作的见解。

中文摘要：大型推理模型（LRMs）已彻底改变了LLM中的推理能力，而推理轨迹的日益公开可用性为研究模型行为提供了宝贵的机会，不仅可以在表面层面，还可以在单个推理步骤的粒度上进行研究。然而，理解推理过程中所采用的思维类型——这为模型的推理模式提供了关键见解，并支持可操作的应用——仍未得到充分探索。为了解决这一差距，我们引入了一个通过布鲁姆分类法（Bloom's Taxonomy）对推理步骤进行自动标注的框架，该分类法将思维分为六个认知层次，如记忆、应用和评估。使用该框架，我们在多个模型和数据集上进行了大规模分析，揭示了跨模型和任务在思维模式上的相似性和差异性。此外，我们证明了从推理轨迹中获得的思维类型信息与正确性相关，为改进推理铺平了道路。我们的发现建立了一个用于分析LRM中思维模式的细粒度框架，并为提高推理质量提供了可操作的见解。

Original abstract: Large Reasoning Models (LRMs) have revolutionized reasoning in LLMs, and the increasing public availability of reasoning traces creates valuable opportunities to study model behavior not only at the surface level but also at the granularity of individual reasoning steps. However, understanding the types of thinking employed during reasoning - which offers critical insights into models' reasoning patterns and enables actionable applications - remains underexplored. To address this gap, we introduce a framework for automatic annotation of reasoning steps through the lens of Bloom's Taxonomy, which classifies thinking into six cognitive levels, such as Remembering, Applying and Evaluating. Using this framework, we perform a large-scale analysis across models and datasets, revealing both similarities and differences in thinking patterns across models and tasks. Moreover, we demonstrate that thinking-type information derived from reasoning traces correlates with correctness, paving the way for improved reasoning. Our findings establish a fine-grained framework for analyzing thinking patterns in LRMs and provide actionable insights for enhancing reasoning quality.

</details>


### [77] [Is Next-Chunk Reasoning RL Really Better than SFT? Revisiting Training Strategies under no-CoT Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23256)
*Yinhao Tang,Youqing Fang,Yanan Sun,Jiangning Liu,Ziyi Wang,Xun Zhao,Weiming Zhang,Bin Liu,Kuikun Liu,Wenwei Zhang,Kai Chen*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 对比 no-CoT 数据下 next-chunk reasoning RL 与 Mixed SFT 的训练策略，对 agent 后训练的算力与效能权衡有启发

TL;DR: 混合监督微调（Mixed SFT）在后续强化学习中的性能上限显著优于下一块推理强化学习，且计算量减少60倍以上；强化学习前准确率不一定决定强化学习后准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有下一块推理强化学习（next-chunk reasoning RL）的评估仅与常规SFT基线对比，无法区分收益来自RL公式本身还是数据暴露方式；需要更严格的受控研究和一个简单但被忽视的基线来厘清问题。

Method: 提出混合监督微调（Mixed SFT）：在单一监督微调阶段同时使用无思维链（no-CoT）数据和长思维链（long-CoT）数据进行训练；并与下一块推理强化学习进行受控对比，测量它们在后续强化学习（post-RLVR）中的性能。

Result: Mixed SFT的post-RLVR性能上限明显高于next-chunk reasoning RL，且训练计算量减少超过60倍；该优势在领域内数学推理和领域外推理任务中一致；同时发现pre-RLVR准确率与post-RLVR准确率不直接相关。

Conclusion: 评估无思维链训练策略应在完整后训练流程中进行；Mixed SFT是一种简单高效的方法，优于复杂的RL方法，同时提示需要重新审视RL在利用无思维链数据中的实际作用。

中文摘要：近期工作提出了一种用于利用无思维链数据（no-CoT data）的下一块推理强化学习（next-chunk reasoning RL）方法，这类数据包括解答过程和教科书推导等语料，它们包含丰富的推理内容，但缺乏显式的思维链标注。该方法训练模型生成隐式推理轨迹，并根据其预测下一块文本的能力给予奖励。尽管前景可观，现有评估主要与常规的监督微调（SFT）基线进行比较，因此尚不清楚收益究竟来自强化学习（RL）本身，还是来自更有效地将模型暴露于无思维链数据。我们通过一项针对下一块推理强化学习的受控研究，并引入一个简单但此前被忽视的替代方案——混合监督微调（Mixed SFT），即单一监督微调阶段，同时使用无思维链数据和长思维链（long-CoT）数据进行训练，来回答这一问题。尽管方法简单，混合监督微调在后续强化学习（post-RLVR）中达到的性能上限明显高于下一块推理强化学习，同时训练计算量减少了60倍以上。该优势在领域内数学推理和领域外推理任务中均保持一致。此外，我们表明更高的强化学习前（pre-RLVR）准确率并不一定转化为更高的强化学习后（post-RLVR）准确率，这凸显了在完整后训练流程的背景下评估无思维链训练策略的必要性。

Original abstract: Recent work proposes next-chunk reasoning RL for leveraging no-CoT data---corpora such as worked solutions and textbook derivations that contain reasoning-rich content but lack explicit chain-of-thought annotations. The method trains a model to generate implicit reasoning traces and rewards them by their ability to predict the next chunk of text. While promising, existing evaluations primarily compare against conventional SFT baselines, leaving open whether the gains come from the RL formulation itself or from more effectively exposing the model to no-CoT data. We address this question with a controlled study of next-chunk reasoning RL and a simple but previously overlooked alternative: Mixed SFT, a single supervised fine-tuning stage that jointly trains on no-CoT and long-CoT data. Despite its simplicity, Mixed SFT achieves a clearly higher post-RLVR performance ceiling than next-chunk reasoning RL while requiring over 60 times less training compute. The advantage is consistent across in-domain mathematical reasoning and out-of-domain reasoning tasks. Moreover, we show that higher pre-RLVR accuracy does not necessarily translate into higher post-RLVR accuracy, highlighting the need to evaluate no-CoT training strategies in the context of the full post-training pipeline.

</details>


### [78] [Hidden in the Request: Explaining Unethical LLM Compliance through Token Relevance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23264)
*Or Biton,Tomer Krichli,Itai Allouche,Joseph Keshet*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 用 LRP 定位伦理合规失效的 token 归因并做解码干预，可迁移到 agent 安全对齐与行为控制

TL;DR: 本研究通过探测方法和LRP分析发现，LLM在面对直接请求帮助时不道德行为更容易被顺从，原因是模型对线索标记的归因不足，并提出了两种LRP引导解码方法以增强安全响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM对齐中帮助性与无害性目标可能冲突，导致对齐失败，需要系统理解模型不道德行为的底层机制。

Method: 引入三种结构模态的探测方法，使用LRP追溯归因偏差，并设计两种LRP引导的解码方法干预生成过程。

Result: 模型在请求帮助形式下性能下降，且存在对线索标记的低归因；所提干预方法能有效促进更安全的响应。

Conclusion: 线索标记归因不足是导致有害顺从的重要因素，LRP引导解码可作为缓解对齐失败的有效策略。

中文摘要：尽管大型语言模型（LLM）被对齐以同时优化有用性和无害性，但这两个目标可能相互冲突，不可避免地导致对齐失败。本研究系统地调查了LLM未能表现出道德行为的实例。为了理解这些漏洞的底层机制，我们引入了一种探测方法，以三种不同的结构模态向LLM呈现不道德场景：客观分类任务、主观第一人称陈述和直接请求帮助。我们发现模型在基于请求帮助的形式下性能下降。利用逐层相关性传播（LRP），我们将这种差异归因于一种归因偏差：模型更重视良性的任务框架标记（例如“你能帮我……”），而不是指示潜在不道德行为的标记（例如“不被抓住”），我们将其称为线索标记。我们假设这种低归因导致了有害的顺从。为了验证这一点，我们引入了两种LRP引导的解码方法，将生成引导到与线索标记更相关的轨迹。实证评估表明，这些干预措施促进了更安全的响应，支持了线索标记归因在顺从失败中的作用。

Original abstract: Although Large Language Models (LLMs) are aligned to optimize for both helpfulness and harmlessness, these dual objectives may conflict, inevitably leading to alignment failures. This work systematically investigates instances where LLMs fail to exhibit ethical behavior. To understand the underlying mechanics of these vulnerabilities, we introduce a probing methodology that presents unethical scenarios to LLMs in three distinct structural modalities: objective classification tasks, subjective first-person statements, and direct requests for assistance. We find that model performance degrades in the request-for-assistance-based form. Using Layer-wise Relevance Propagation (LRP), we trace this discrepancy to an attribution bias: the model places greater emphasis on benign task-framing tokens (e.g., "Can you help me...") than on tokens signaling the underlying unethical behavior (e.g., "without getting caught"), which we term cue-tokens. We hypothesize that this under-attribution contributes to harmful compliance. To test this, we introduce two LRP-guided decoding methods that steer generation toward trajectories more relevant to cue tokens. Empirical evaluations show that these interventions promote safer responses, supporting cue-token attribution's role in compliance failures.

</details>


### [79] [Apodex 1.1: Scaling Agentic Intelligence for Complex Work](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23283)
*B. An,B. Li,B. Wang,B. Zhang,B. L. Wang,C. Feng,C. Wei,C. Xue,C. Zhang,D. Ng,D. Ye,E. Min,F. Chen,F. Liu,F. Yang,F. Ye,G. Sun,H. Ji,H. Xu,H. Yang,H. Ye,H. Zhang,H. Zhao,J. Li,J. Lin,J. Xia,K. Jin,K. Wang,K. Yang,L. Bing,L. Lei,L. Su,Le. Wang,Lu. Wang,N. Wang,Q. Ren,Q. Yang,R. Li,S. Bai,S. Du,S. Li,S. Lin,S. Nie,S. Wang,S. Zhang,S. Z. Wang,T. Ge,Ta. Q. Fang,Ti. Q. Fang,W. Fang,W. Li,W. Zhang,X. Chen,X. Li,X. Tang,X. Wang,X. Xu,X. Zhang,X. Q. Wang,X. Y. Wang,Y. Deng,Y. Gao,Y. Hu,Y. Li,Y. Sui,Y. Wang,Y. Xiao,Y. Zhang,Y. Zhou,Z. Chen,Z. Cheng,Z. Feng,Z. Liang,Z. Liu,Z. Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 通用 agentic 系统，涵盖环境扩展、长程任务协调、状态维护与可验证交付，对 coding agent 构建有直接参考价值

TL;DR: Apodex 1.1 通过环境扩展和智能体协调扩展，在多个复杂领域达到领先性能，且小模型版本仍保持强大工作能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用语言模型虽能推理和综合知识，但复杂现实任务需要持续交互、状态维护、故障恢复和可验证交付；现有模型缺乏这种“工作能力”。

Method: 提出工作能力概念，沿环境扩展（扩大可执行文件、搜索、代码环境）和智能体协调扩展（训练分解任务、委派并行、整合异步结果、重新规划）两个维度训练模型，并借助共享执行框架AgentOS维护状态与来源。

Result: Apodex 1.1 在专业工作、金融、科学研究、数学、编码和搜索等领域达到领先性能，尽管模型较小；35B参数的Mini版本也保持强工作能力。

Conclusion: 将智能体智能落实为持续、可验证的现实工作，推进了构建“重型求解器”的目标，为长时程复杂任务提供了可行路径。

中文摘要：通用语言模型能够进行推理和综合知识，但复杂的工作还需要与文件、信息源和可执行代码进行持续交互，同时还要具备状态维护、故障恢复和可验证的交付能力。我们称之为“工作能力”：朝着现实世界目标持续、可验证地取得进展。Apodex 1.1 沿两个互补维度发展这种能力。“环境扩展”扩大了可执行文件、搜索和代码环境的多样性与可验证性，而“智能体协调扩展”则训练智能体分解长时程任务、委派并行工作、整合异步结果并重新规划。共享的执行框架和 AgentOS 在工具和智能体之间维护任务状态与来源，训练将环境轨迹和协调痕迹转化为可靠行为。在复杂的专业工作、金融、科学研究、数学、编码和搜索中，尽管 Apodex 1.1 使用的模型规模远小于许多前沿系统，它仍达到了领先性能区间。350 亿参数的 Apodex 1.1 Mini 进一步以可本地部署的形式保持了强大的工作能力。这些结果将智能体智能扎根于随时间完成的有用、可验证的工作，并推进了我们构建用于雄心勃勃、长期运行任务的“重型求解器”的目标。

Original abstract: General-purpose language models can reason and synthesize knowledge, but complex work also requires sustained interaction with files, information sources, and executable code, together with state maintenance, failure recovery, and verifiable delivery. We call this \emph{working capability}: sustained, verifiable progress toward a real-world objective. Apodex 1.1 develops this capability along two complementary dimensions. \emph{Environment Scaling} expands the diversity and verifiability of executable file, search, and code environments, while \emph{Agentic Coordination Scaling} trains agents to decompose long-horizon tasks, delegate parallel work, integrate asynchronous results, and replan. A shared execution harness and AgentOS maintain task state and provenance across tools and agents, and training turns environment trajectories and coordination traces into reliable behavior. Across complex professional work, finance, scientific research, mathematics, coding, and search, Apodex 1.1 reaches the leading performance band despite using a substantially smaller model than many frontier systems. The 35B-parameter Apodex 1.1 Mini further retains strong working capability in a locally deployable form. These results ground agentic intelligence in useful, verifiable work completed over time and advance our goal of building a \emph{Heavy-Duty Solver} for ambitious, long-running tasks.

</details>


### [80] [Agent-G$^2$: Gaussian Guidance for Agentic Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23318)
*Zixuan Wang,Yanrui Miao,Zhengxi Lu,Teng Pan,Yiwen Qiu,Hongxing Li,Peng Qiu,Ruiqing Zhang,Yongliang Shen*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: Code: https://github.com/ZJU-REAL/Agent-G2 ; Project page: https://zju-real.github.io/Agent-G2

Selection reason: 提出高斯引导的长程智能体强化学习方法，通过在线估计轨迹保留深度提升探索效率，可迁移到代码优化 agent 的训练与探索。

TL;DR: 提出 Agent-G^2，一种高斯引导框架，将引导深度建模为在线估计的高斯分布，无需额外 rollout 即可自适应任务难度，在 ALFWorld 上超越多种基线且成本更低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现存的基于提示的强化学习方法将引导深度视为固定标量或需要额外探测，忽略了任务间的异质性，且有用引导并非单一最优深度而是具有高斯形状的信息带。

Method: Agent-G^2 从策略优化已收集的 rollout 中在线估计每个任务的高斯深度分布，中心结合全局基线与簇难度，散布跟踪簇内方差，无需额外探测或预测器。

Result: 在 ALFWorld 上，Agent-G^2 比最强基于提示、无提示和 Aux-RL 基线分别提升 2.3/3.9/7.4 分，且 rollout 成本低于逐样本探测的三分之一。

Conclusion: Agent-G^2 验证了引导深度服从高斯分布的假设，以低成本自适应任务差异，有效提升奖励稀疏长周期任务的性能。

中文摘要：基于提示的强化学习通过在每次 rollout 之前保留专家轨迹的前缀，让策略从更接近成功的状态进行探索，从而解决长周期智能体任务中的奖励稀疏问题。其有效性取决于引导深度：即保留轨迹的多少。现有方法将此深度视为确定性标量。调度方法在样本间共享一个值，忽略了每个任务的异质性；逐样本探测则分别估计每个样本的深度，但代价是额外的 rollout。我们发现，有用的引导占据一个深度带，其信息量分布近似于带中心周围的高斯分布，而非集中在单一最优点上。我们提出了 Agent-G$^2$，一种高斯引导框架，它从已收集的用于策略优化的 rollout 中在线估计每个任务的深度，该深度服从高斯分布，其中心和散布均在线更新，无需探测 rollout 或学习的深度预测器。中心结合了全局基线和每簇难度，散布则跟踪簇内方差。我们在 ALFWorld 和 WebShop 上使用 Qwen2.5-1.5B / 7B-Instruct 评估了 Agent-G$^2$。在 ALFWorld 上，Agent-G$^2$ 比最强的基于提示、无提示和 Aux-RL 基线分别高出 2.3 / 3.9 / 7.4 分，而 rollout 成本仅为逐样本探测的三分之一以下。

Original abstract: Hint-based reinforcement learning addresses reward sparsity in long-horizon agentic tasks by retaining a prefix of an expert trajectory before each rollout, letting the policy explore from a state closer to success. Its effectiveness hinges on the guidance depth: how much of the trajectory to keep. Existing methods treat this depth as a deterministic scalar. Scheduled approaches share one value across samples and ignore per-task heterogeneity; per-sample probing estimates it separately at the cost of extra rollouts. We find that useful guidance occupies a band of depths whose informativeness profile is approximately Gaussian around the band center, rather than concentrating at a single optimal point. We propose Agent-G$^2$, a Gaussian guidance framework that draws the depth per task from a Gaussian whose center and spread are estimated online from rollouts already collected for policy optimization, requiring no probe rollouts or learned depth predictor. The center combines a global baseline with per-cluster difficulty, and the spread tracks within-cluster variance. We evaluate Agent-G$^2$ on ALFWorld and WebShop on Qwen2.5-1.5B / 7B-Instruct. Agent-G$^2$ outperforms the strongest hint-based, hint-free, and Aux-RL baselines on ALFWorld by 2.3 / 3.9 / 7.4 points at under one-third the rollout cost of per-sample probing.

</details>


### [81] [Walking on the DARKSIDE](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23370)
*Aldo Gangemi,Emanuele Bottazzi*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 20 pages, 2 figures, several tables

Selection reason: 提出用于 LLM 输出的一致性审计与防伪机制，可作为 agent 可靠性验证和结果安全的参考方法。

TL;DR: 提出DARKSIDE，一种在POLANYI++基础上通过显式累积排除轨迹和保证轴审计LLM输出连贯性的方法，实验表明其能部分弥合模式与路径之间的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM虽能识别模式，但无法追踪推理中的排除路径，容易将虚构权威、误用机制或隐秘类比等无意义输入当作有根据内容并固化为结构化输出；现有LAG方法（如POLANYI++）生成的XKG也会一并纳入错误假设，自动化推理器难以检测。

Method: 在POLANYI++之上引入DARKSIDE，将排除轨迹形式化为随时间累积的显式数据结构，并增加保证轴将命名指称分为Warranted、Unattested、Misattributed、Fabricated四类；当捏造率为正或未支持率超阈值时，将DelegationRiskAssessment升级为UNSAFE。在Gemini 3上作为引导层，使用BSBench对抗语料库和Claude Sonnet 4.6独立评判进行评估。

Result: 在BSBench 100项对抗性语料上的实证表明，本体介导的负轨迹装置能部分搭建LLM前向传播中结构性的模式-路径差距；XKG提供缺失记忆，保证轴起到认知防火墙作用。

Conclusion: DARKSIDE通过显式排除轨迹和保证轴增强了LLM输出的连贯性审计，可缓解LLM将无意义输入具体化的问题；架构性证据表明，外部知识图谱与认知防火墙的结合有助于缩小模式识别与路径追踪之间的差距。

中文摘要：大型语言模型（LLM）能够识别模式，但并不能原生地追踪连贯话语所要求的排除路径。当输入依赖于虚构的权威、误用的机制或隐秘的类比时，未经引导的LLM倾向于将其视为有根据的内容进行交互，并将这一错误具体化到其生成的任何结构化输出中。使用POLANYI++的逻辑增强生成（LAG）是一种利用启发式方法、本体论和问题求解方法进行隐性知识提取的LLM引导方法，它生成OWL2格式的扩展知识图谱（XKG），但同样继承了这一漏洞：复杂的无意义输入会与合法三元组一起被具体化到图谱中，并且由于XKG是与错误假设共同生成的，自动化推理器很难检测到它。我们提出了DARKSIDE，一种基于POLANYI++的连贯性审计方法。它将路径形式化为一种显式数据结构，即在话语时间上累积的排除记录，并辅以一个保证轴（warrant axis），将每个命名指称分类为有保证的（Warranted）、未经证实的（Unattested）、错误归属的（Misattributed）或捏造的（Fabricated），同时设有一条升级规则：当捏造率为正或未支持率超过阈值时，将委托风险评估（DelegationRiskAssessment）推至UNSAFE。我们将DARKSIDE作为Gemini 3之上的一个引导层，在BSBench（一个包含软件工程、金融、医疗、物理和法律等领域100个条目的、听起来复杂而实则荒谬的对抗性语料库）上进行评估，并由Claude Sonnet 4.6作为独立评判员。实证证据支持一个架构性主张：当LLM的前向传播被包裹在一种本体介导的负路径装置中时，结构上的模式-路径差距可以被部分地搭建（scaffolded）。XKG充当缺失的记忆，而保证轴则充当认知防火墙。

Original abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) recognise patterns but do not natively track the path of exclusions that a coherent discourse demands. When an input rests on a fabricated authority, a misapplied mechanism, or a surreptitious analogy, an unsteered LLM tends to engage with it as if it were grounded, and to reify the misstep into any structured output it generates. Logic-Augmented Generation (LAG) with POLANYI++, an LLM-steering method that uses heuristics, ontologies and problem solving methods for tacit knowledge extraction, produces an Extended Knowledge Graph (XKG) in OWL2, but inherits the same vulnerability: a sophisticated nonsensical input is reified into the graph alongside the legitimate triples, and is hardly detectable by automated reasoners since the XKG is generated jointly with the wrong assumptions. We introduce DARKSIDE, a coherence auditing method on top of POLANYI++. It formalises the trail as an explicit data structure of accumulated exclusions over discourse time, complemented by a warrant axis that classifies each named referent as Warranted, Unattested, Misattributed or Fabricated, with an escalation rule that pushes the DelegationRiskAssessment to UNSAFE when the fabricated rate is positive or the unsupported rate exceeds a threshold. We evaluate DARKSIDE as a steering layer over a Gemini 3 on BSBench, a 100-item adversarial corpus of sophisticated-sounding nonsense across software engineering, finance, healthcare, physics and law, with Claude Sonnet 4.6 as an independent judge. The empirical evidence supports an architectural claim: when an LLM forward pass is wrapped in an ontology-mediated negative-trail apparatus, the structural pattern-vs-path gap can be partially scaffolded. The XKG functions as the missing memory, and the warrant axis as an epistemic firewall.

</details>


### [82] [MediSkill-Evo: Process-Constrained Self-Evolution for Evidence-Grounded Clinical Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23397)
*Ruoyu Wu,Shenfu Xie,Yinqian Sun,Haibo Tong,Feifei Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 医疗 agent 的自进化框架，利用过程约束与偏好 harness 提升决策可靠性，方法论可迁移到 coding agent 的技能验证与自我改进。

TL;DR: MediSkill-Evo 通过自演化过程知识和过程约束偏好机制，在不微调主干的情况下显著提升了临床智能体的诊断准确率和安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床智能体因部分可观测性需要基于证据的安全交互，但现有方法难以将经验转化为有明确出处和权威的可复用过程知识。

Method: 提出 MediSkill-Evo，通过四个类型化知识库（临床、过程、符号、视觉）在类型特定验证和范围规则下自我演化治理知识，并使用过程约束偏好机制将验证知识转化为安全行动。

Result: 在 FullChain 上诊断准确率提升7.81%，治疗意图覆盖率提升70.67%，关键失败减少43.04%；压力测试下过程复合分提升7.77%，必要行动完成度提升12.41%；NEJM 诊断准确率提升2.56%，核心分提升18.96%。

Conclusion: MediSkill-Evo 验证了无需微调即可通过受治理的自演化知识显著提升临床决策质量与安全性，为可追溯、负责任临床智能体提供了新范式。

中文摘要：交互式临床智能体在部分可观测性下运行，因此可靠的护理依赖于通过基于证据且安全的交互来达成正确诊断。然而，现有智能体难以将经验转化为具有明确出处和权威性的可复用过程知识。为解决这一差距，我们引入了 MediSkill-Evo，它无需对主干模型进行微调即可自我演化受治理的过程知识。它通过更新四个类型化知识库中的临床、过程、符号和视觉知识，并在类型特定的验证和范围规则下实现这种自我演化。然后，过程约束偏好机制（Process-Constrained Preference Harness）通过将候选方案锚定于证据并优先考虑更安全的决策，将验证后的知识转化为行动。我们在 300 个源自 MIMIC-IV 的 FullChain 诊疗记录、180 个覆盖六种过程义务的硬隔离条件以及 100 个多模态 NEJM 图像诊断病例上进行了评估。在 Qwen FullChain 上，MediSkill-Evo 相比之前表现最好的智能体，诊断准确率提高了 7.81%，治疗意图覆盖率提高了 70.67%，同时关键失败减少了 43.04%。在压力测试下，相比各指标上表现最好的智能体，它的压力过程综合得分提高了 7.77%，必要行动完成度提高了 12.41%，并且在患者事实、时间证据和分诊红旗恢复方面表现更强，且在不可用证据、治疗和分诊安全检查中未出现控制器评分的错误。在多模态 NEJM 诊断中，使用可选 MedSAM 定位的 MediSkill-Evo 相比表现最好的记忆智能体，诊断准确率提高了 2.56%，核心得分提高了 18.96%。代码可在 https://anonymous.4open.science/r/mediskill-evo_anonymous-68E7 获取。

Original abstract: Interactive clinical agents operate under partial observability, so reliable care depends on reaching the correct diagnosis through evidence-grounded, safe interactions. Yet existing agents struggle to convert experience into reusable process knowledge with explicit provenance and authority. To address this gap, we introduce MediSkill-Evo, which self-evolves governed process knowledge without fine-tuning the backbone. It realizes this self-evolution by updating clinical, process, symbolic, and visual knowledge in four typed banks under type-specific validation and scope rules. The Process-Constrained Preference Harness then turns validated knowledge into action by grounding candidates in evidence and prioritizing safer decisions. We evaluate on 300 MIMIC-IV-derived FullChain encounters, 180 hard-isolation conditions covering six process obligations, and 100 multimodal NEJM image-diagnosis cases. On Qwen FullChain, MediSkill-Evo improves diagnosis accuracy by 7.81% and treatment-intent coverage by 70.67% over the best-performing prior agent, while reducing critical failures by 43.04%. Under stress, it improves the stress-process composite by 7.77% and required-action completion by 12.41% over the best-performing agent for each metric, with stronger patient-fact, temporal-evidence, and triage-red-flag recovery and no controller-scored errors in unavailable-evidence, treatment, and triage safety checks. On multimodal NEJM diagnosis, MediSkill-Evo with optional MedSAM localization improves diagnosis accuracy by 2.56% and core score by 18.96% over the best-performing memory agent. Code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/mediskill-evo_anonymous-68E7.

</details>


### [83] [SkillAlchemy: Open-World Agent Skill Creation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23417)
*Hengjun Wang,Shuyue Wei,Boyi Liu,Jun Yang,Yongxin Tong*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 33 pages, 7 figures, 8 tables. Includes appendices

Selection reason: 面向开放世界的 agent 技能创建框架，通过对比证据和语法引导自动构建可靠技能，直接适用于代码 agent 的技能生成与复用。

TL;DR: SkillAlchemy是一个从开放世界材料中创建Agent技能的框架，通过对比性证据识别隐含需求并基于证据支持范围准入候选程序，在87个任务上将通过率相比无技能执行提高19.9个百分点，接近人工精选技能水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有技能创建依赖人工编写、模型先验或执行轨迹，这些来源在陌生任务上不可用，需要从开放世界材料中自动发现被简报遗漏的行为相关需求并判断程序合理性。

Method: SkillAlchemy采用准入中心框架：通过对比性证据识别隐含需求，根据证据支持的范围准入候选程序，并将准入内容编译为语法引导的技能包。

Result: 在87个SkillsBench v1.1任务上，SkillAlchemy相比无技能执行提高通过率19.9个百分点，相比最强自动化基线提高8.6个百分点，性能与人工精选技能相当。

Conclusion: SkillAlchemy能够有效从开放世界材料中创建可靠的技能，显著提升语言代理在陌生任务上的表现，达到人工精选技能的水平。

中文摘要：Agent技能是可重用的程序化工件，在推理时通过专门的工作流程、工具约定和领域行为扩展语言代理。然而，创建可靠的技能仍然主要依赖于人工编写、模型先验或执行轨迹。这些来源通常不适用于不熟悉的任务，这表明有必要从开放世界的材料中创建技能。在本文中，我们研究了开放世界技能创建：给定一个欠明确的技能简报和一个源访问规范，创建者必须发现简报遗漏的行为相关需求，并确定每个源自程序的合理性范围。我们提出了SkillAlchemy，一个以准入为中心的源基础技能创建框架。SkillAlchemy通过对比性证据识别隐含需求，基于证据支持的范围准入候选程序，并将已准入的内容编译成语法引导的技能包。在87个SkillsBench v1.1任务上的大量实验表明，SkillAlchemy相比无技能执行将通过率提高了19.9个百分点，相比最强的自动化基线提高了8.6个百分点，同时达到了与人工精选技能相当的性能。

Original abstract: Agent skills are reusable procedural artifacts that extend language agents with specialized workflows, tool conventions, and domain behaviors at inference time. However, creating reliable skills still depends largely on human authorship, model priors, or execution traces. These sources are often unavailable for unfamiliar tasks, suggesting the need to create skills from open-world materials. In this paper, we study open-world skill creation: given an underspecified skill brief and a source-access specification, a creator must discover behavior-relevant requirements omitted by the brief and determine how broadly each source-derived procedure is justified.
