We propose SkillAlchemy, an admission-centered framework for source-grounded skill creation. SkillAlchemy identifies implicit requirements through contrastive evidence, admits candidate procedures based on evidence-supported scope, and compiles the admitted content into a grammar-guided skill package. Extensive experiments across 87 SkillsBench v1.1 tasks demonstrate that SkillAlchemy improves pass rate over no-skill execution by 19.9 percentage points and the strongest automated baseline by 8.6 percentage points, while achieving performance comparable to human-curated skills.

</details>


### [84] [StrategyBench: Evaluating Explicit Strategy Induction in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23475)
*Jinghan Tan,Yuanzheng Wang,Lu Chen,Zijun Chen,Yuqian Wang,Maosong Sun*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 提出策略归纳评测基准，衡量 LLM 从示例中抽象规则的能力，对 agent 的经验复用与自适应策略有启发。

TL;DR: 提出了StrategyBench基准，用于评估大型语言模型从少样本示例中归纳任务策略的能力，并分析了策略质量与下游效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接ICL对示例构造敏感，缺乏显式规则抽象，而人类通过先总结规则再应用来降低敏感性。现有方法未系统评估这种策略归纳能力。

Method: 从BIG-Bench选择策略可诱导任务，构建参考策略，定义策略质量和下游效用两个维度的评估指标；并从任务变化、模型配置、适应设置三个角度分析，包括类别差异、生成器-执行器选择、演示设计及SFT适应。

Result: 显式策略效用在不同任务类别间差异显著，且依赖于策略生成和执行条件。

Conclusion: StrategyBench提供了一个评估LLM策略归纳能力的基准，揭示了策略效用的条件依赖性，为改进少样本ICL提供了方向。

中文摘要：随着大型语言模型越来越多地用于数据稀缺和不断演变的任务场景，少样本上下文学习（ICL）已成为任务适应的关键范式。然而，直接ICL通常使用少量示例而不显式抽象任务规则，使其对示例构建敏感。相比之下，人类学习者往往通过首先从示例中总结任务规则，然后将其应用于新实例来降低这种敏感性。为了评估这种能力，我们提出了StrategyBench，它从BIG-Bench中选择策略可诱导任务，构建参考策略，并沿两个维度定义评估指标：策略质量和下游效用。我们进一步从三个角度分析策略诱导：任务变化、模型配置和适应设置，涵盖类别差异、生成器-执行器选择、演示设计和基于SFT的适应。实验表明，显式策略效用在不同任务类别间存在显著差异，并取决于策略生成和执行条件。该基准已发布于：https://anonymous.4open.science/r/StrategyBench-D53C。

Original abstract: As large language models are increasingly used in data-scarce and evolving task scenarios, few-shot in-context learning (ICL) has become a key paradigm for task adaptation. However, direct ICL often uses a small set of examples without explicitly abstracting task rules, making it sensitive to example construction. In contrast, human learners often reduce such sensitivity by first summarizing task rules from examples and then applying them to new instances. To evaluate this ability, we propose StrategyBench, which selects strategy-inducible tasks from BIG-Bench, constructs reference strategies, and defines evaluation metrics along two dimensions: strategy quality and downstream utility. We further analyze strategy induction from three perspectives: task variation, model configuration, and adaptation setting, covering category-wise differences, generator-executor choices, demonstration design, and SFT-based adaptation. Experiments show that explicit strategy utility differs substantially across task categories and depends on both strategy generation and execution conditions. The benchmark is released at: https://anonymous.4open.science/r/StrategyBench-D53C.

</details>


### [85] [SRPO: Self-Reflective Policy Optimization for Long-Horizon Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23493)
*Jialong Liu,Yuling Shi,Ning Yang,Xiaodong Gu,Zuchao Li*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: Accepted to ICML 2026

Selection reason: 提出自反思策略优化，将稀疏奖励转化为稠密 token 信号，显著提升长程 agent 任务表现，可直接用于代码优化 agent 的后训练。

TL;DR: 提出SRPO框架，通过自我反思将稀疏奖励转化为令牌级密集信号，在数学和智能体任务中实现SOTA且数据效率极高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM训练后优化通常依赖外部奖励模型或人工反馈，未能充分利用模型自身的反思能力来改进学习，且监督微调在长轨迹任务中往往数据效率低下。

Method: SRPO让模型自我分析轨迹，生成反思补丁，并利用基于反思条件的学生策略采样与教师评分，构建密集的令牌级训练信号，无需额外模型或外部批评者。

Result: 基于Qwen3-8B，AIME'24准确率73.3%（仅用8% FLOPs），WebShop成功率64.7%，ALFWorld 76.8%，SWE-Bench-Lite 31.2%，全面超越基线。

Conclusion: SRPO验证了自我反思作为内置信用分配机制的有效性，为高效后训练LLMs提供了新范式，能显著降低计算成本并提升复杂任务表现。

中文摘要：自我反思是人类学习中一种强大的信用分配机制，能将稀疏的结果反馈转化为可操作的指导。然而，其在训练后大型语言模型（LLMs）中的潜力仍未得到充分探索。我们提出了自我反思策略优化（SRPO），这是一个将这种能力内化的框架。SRPO使LLMs能够分析自身完成的轨迹，将错误综合为简洁的“反思补丁”，并将基于反思条件的学生在策略回滚的教师评分作为密集的令牌级训练信号。这一过程有效地将稀疏的终端监督转化为密集的、令牌级的学习信号，无需外部批评者、单独奖励模型或更大的教师模型。我们证明SRPO在数学推理和长视野智能体基准上取得了最先进的性能，并具有出色的数据效率。使用Qwen3-8B基础模型，SRPO在仅使用缩放监督微调所需训练FLOPs的8%（0.08倍）的情况下，在AIME'24上达到73.3%，同时在WebShop（64.7%）、ALFWorld（76.8%）和SWE-Bench-Lite（31.2%）上的成功率显著提高。代码可在https://github.com/Galleons2029/SRPO获取。

Original abstract: Self-reflection is a powerful mechanism for credit assignment in human learning, converting sparse outcome feedback into actionable guidance. However, its potential for post-training Large Language Models (LLMs) remains underexplored. We propose Self-Reflective Policy Optimization (SRPO), a framework that internalizes this capability. SRPO enables LLMs to analyze their own completed trajectories, synthesize errors into concise "reflection patches," and use reflection-conditioned teacher scores on student on-policy rollouts as dense token-level training signals. This process effectively transforms sparse terminal supervision into dense, token-level learning signals without requiring external critics, separate reward models, or larger teacher models. We demonstrate that SRPO achieves state-of-the-art performance across mathematical reasoning and long-horizon agentic benchmarks with exceptional data efficiency. Using a Qwen3-8B base model, SRPO attains 73.3% on AIME'24 using only 8% (0.08x) of the training FLOPs required by scaled supervised fine-tuning, while significantly improving success rates on WebShop (64.7%), ALFWorld (76.8%), and SWE-Bench-Lite (31.2%). Code is available at https://github.com/Galleons2029/SRPO

</details>


### [86] [EarthVerse: Benchmarking Scientific Agents Across Dynamic Earth Systems and Natural Hazards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23525)
*Zhiqing Cui,Xinxiang Yin,Yihong Tang,Xinglang Zhang,Yuanzhe Hu,Siru Zhong,Weidong Tang,Yuxuan Liang,Weijia Li,Ming Jin,Shirui Pan,Yuhao Kang,Dingyi Zhuang,Jinhua Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 科学 agent 的细粒度过程评测基准，通过可执行真值和失败定位方法评估 agent 的检索、工具使用与推理，可迁移到 coding agent 的可靠速度验证。

TL;DR: 提出了EarthVerse基准，用于评估科学智能体在地球系统分析中的端到端可靠性，发现当前系统在保持证据链一致性上存在明显不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地球系统分析依赖异构观测数据，自然灾害等场景中不完整证据会影响结论；现有智能体缺乏系统性评估其科学推理和全过程一致性的基准。

Method: 构建了405个基于199个事件和19个灾害类别的可复现任务，提供可执行基准真相和任务特定评分细则；在受控工具使用协议下评估25个模型/智能体系统，并通过受控研究定位失败环节。

Result: 最佳平均答案单元准确率为84.65%，最高Strict@95仅为34.81%；差距表明智能体在跨证据、尺度、单位、计算和解释的链条维持上存在缺陷。

Conclusion: EarthVerse能够可靠衡量动态地球系统中端到端的科学推理能力，揭示当前智能体虽能完成局部步骤但缺乏一致性链条，为改进科学智能体提供了可复现的基础。

中文摘要：地球系统分析从来源、尺度、时间和模态各不相同的观测中重建不断变化的物理过程。自然灾害使这项工作具有重要影响，因为不完整的证据可能会改变对严重程度、暴露程度和机制的估计。我们引入了EarthVerse，一个通过包级（package-scoped）调查来评估科学智能体的基准。其405个可复现任务基于199个有记录的事件和19个灾害类别。智能体检查异构事件包，选择兼容的证据，执行透明的计算，协调来源差异，并在最终答案中保留来源信息。我们提供了可执行的基准真相，将每个任务分解为细粒度的答案单元，以及特定于任务的评分细则，这些细则评估支持性研究过程，同时允许多种有效路径。我们在受控工具使用协议下评估了25个模型和智能体系统，然后使用受控研究来定位证据获取、工具选择、记忆、推理、交互和科学执行方面的失败。在各类系统中，最佳平均答案单元准确率为84.65%，而最高Strict@95仅为34.81%。这一差距表明，当前智能体经常完成单个步骤，却未能在证据、尺度、单位、计算和物理解释之间保持一致的链条。EarthVerse为衡量动态地球系统中端到端的科学可靠性提供了可复现的基础。

Original abstract: Earth-system analysis reconstructs changing physical processes from observations that differ in source, scale, timing, and modality. Natural hazards make this work consequential because incomplete evidence can change estimates of severity, exposure, and mechanism. We introduce EarthVerse, a benchmark that evaluates scientific agents through package-scoped investigations. Its 405 reproducible tasks are grounded in 199 documented events and 19 hazard families. Agents inspect heterogeneous event packages, choose compatible evidence, execute transparent calculations, reconcile source differences, and preserve provenance in the final answer. We provide executable ground truth that decomposes each task into fine-grained answer units, together with task-specific rubrics that assess the supporting research process while allowing multiple valid paths. We evaluate 25 model and agent systems under a controlled tool-using protocol, then use controlled studies to locate failures in evidence access, tool selection, memory, reasoning, interaction, and scientific execution. Across systems, the best mean answer-unit accuracy is 84.65%, while the highest Strict@95 is only 34.81%. The gap shows that current agents often complete individual steps without maintaining a consistent chain across evidence, scales, units, calculations, and physical interpretation. EarthVerse provides a reproducible basis for measuring end-to-end scientific reliability in dynamic Earth systems.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [87] [Execution-Anchored Hallucination Calibration Reranking for Verilog Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22938)
*Guang Yang,Xing Hu,Xiang Chen,Terry Yue Zhuo,Xin Xia*

Main category: cs.SE



Selection reason: cs.SE 论文按全量策略收录。

TL;DR: 提出EAHC框架，通过独立获取执行与推理信号并在决策阶段融合，校准LLM在Verilog代码生成中的推理幻觉，提升重新排序性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在Verilog代码生成中性能下降，多候选采样后自动选择最优候选困难。现有执行型重排方法因测试台质量差而迁移性差，LLM评判者则因推理幻觉对等价代码判断不一致。

Method: EAHC框架包含双通道：EAHC-R（4B推理判别器）与EAHC-T（基于RAG的测试台生成器），独立获取执行信号与推理信号，仅在决策阶段融合，并用执行行为锚定推理判断以校准幻觉。

Result: 实证发现执行与推理信号误差正交，但直接让推理者观察执行结果会导致判断锚定；EAHC通过独立融合两信号，使执行等效候选获得一致评分，提升了重排效果。

Conclusion: EAHC有效结合执行与推理信号，克服了现有方法的领域迁移性和幻觉问题，为低资源HDL代码生成中的候选选择提供了新思路。

中文摘要：大型语言模型（LLM）在代码生成方面展现出了卓越的能力，但在Verilog等低资源硬件描述语言上，其性能显著下降。尽管多候选采样提高了生成正确解决方案的可能性，但自动选择最优候选仍然是一个开放的挑战。通过对九个模型和两个基准的系统性实证研究，我们发现了两个关键局限性：（1）现有的基于执行的重新排序方法，依赖于测试台通过/失败结果，由于生成的测试台质量低而表现出较差的领域迁移性；（2）LLM作为评判者遭受推理幻觉，对执行等效的代码产生不一致的判断。这些发现揭示了两种具有正交错误的信号类型：执行信号（确定性但测试台覆盖有限）和推理信号（语义丰富但易产生幻觉）。它们的正交性表明可以结合这两种信号，然而在我们的实验中，让推理者直接观察执行结果仅仅使其判断锚定于测试结果；因此，我们独立获取这两种信号，仅在决策阶段进行融合。基于这些见解，我们提出了EAHC，一种执行锚定的幻觉校准重新排序框架，它将推理判断锚定到执行行为上，使得执行等效的候选获得一致的分数，该框架实现了双通道架构：EAHC-R，一个4B推理判别器；以及EAHC-T，一个利用RAG进行执行验证的测试台生成器。

Original abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated remarkable capabilities in code generation, yet their performance degrades significantly on low-resource Hardware Description Languages such as Verilog. While multi-candidate sampling improves the likelihood of generating correct solutions, au-tomatically selecting the optimal candidate remains an open challenge. Through a systematic empirical study across nine models and two benchmarks, we identify two critical limitations:(1) existing execution-based reranking methods, which rely on testbench pass/fail outcomes, exhibit poor domain transferability due to low-quality generated testbenches; and (2) LLM-as-a-Judge suffers from reasoning hallucination, producing incon-sistent judgments for execution-equivalent code. These findings reveal two signal types with orthogonal errors: execution signals(deterministic but testbench coverage limited)and reasoning signals (semantically rich but hallucination-prone). Their orthog-onality suggests combining the two signals, yet in our experiments letting the reasoner directly observe execution results merely anchors its judgments on test outcomes; we therefore acquire the two signals independently and fuse them only at the decision stage. Based on these insights, we propose EAHC, an Execution-Anchored Hallucination Calibration reranking framework that anchors reasoning judgments to execution behavior so that execution-equivalent candidates receive consistent scores, which implements a dual-channel architecture: EAHC-R, a 4B reasoning discriminator; and EAHC-T, a testbench generator leveraging RAG for execution verification.

</details>


### [88] [ARGUS: MCP-Grounded Root Cause Analysis for Kubernetes Incidents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23084)
*Ergi Senja,Seyed Mohammad Reza Razavi Zadegan,Philipp Leitner*

Main category: cs.SE

Comments: 11 pages, 5 figures

Selection reason: cs.SE 论文按全量策略收录。

TL;DR: ARGUS 是一个基于 MCP 的 Kubernetes 根因分析助手，在十种故障场景中全部正确识别根因，但推荐修复措施的可信度较低，呈现诊断与处方的不对称性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kubernetes 事件排查流程碎片化，现有 LLM 驱动的 RCA 系统依赖定制数据访问层，缺乏跨组织可复用性。

Method: 提出 ARGUS，通过标准 MCP 服务器连接商业 LLM 与 Kubernetes 可观测性数据（状态、Prometheus 指标、Loki 日志、NATS 消息），在 Slack 中输出结构化诊断摘要；采用受控故障注入（10 种场景）、量规评分（三维度）和半结构化访谈（6 名工程师）进行评估。

Result: 在所有十个场景中正确指出根因，MCP 成功率为 0.91；实践者信任诊断但怀疑修复建议；发现诊断/处方不对称性。

Conclusion: ARGUS 在根因识别上可靠，但自动修复建议信任度不足，该不对称性对未来自主事件处理系统设计有重要启示。

中文摘要：Kubernetes 事件排查需要关联来自指标、日志、容器状态和消息传递系统等多个监控工具的信号，这种碎片化的工作流程会减慢诊断速度并加剧告警疲劳。大型语言模型（LLM）在自动化根因分析（RCA）方面展现出潜力，但现有系统依赖自定义的、特定于系统的数据访问层，无法跨组织复用。我们提出了 ARGUS，一个基于 MCP 的 RCA 助手，通过标准化的 MCP 服务器将商业 LLM 连接到实时 Kubernetes 可观测性数据，涵盖 Kubernetes 状态、Prometheus 指标、Loki 日志和 NATS 消息传递，并在值班工程师已在使用的 Slack 事件通道内提供结构化的诊断摘要。我们使用三种互补方法对 ARGUS 进行了初步评估：在十个 Kubernetes 事件场景中进行受控故障注入，对生成的 RCA 摘要进行基于量规的三个维度评分，以及与工业合作伙伴的六名值班工程师进行半结构化访谈。ARGUS 在所有十个场景中都正确指出了根因，总体 MCP 成功率为 0.91。实践者信任诊断输出，但对推荐的修复措施持续表现出怀疑。我们的核心发现是诊断/处方不对称性：ARGUS 能可靠地识别出了什么问题，但在指定下一步该做什么方面被认为不太可靠或值得信赖。这种模式在三种评估方法中均可观察到，对未来自主智能体事件处理系统具有重要意义。

Original abstract: Kubernetes incident triage requires correlating signals from metrics, logs, container state, and messaging systems across multiple monitoring tools, a fragmented workflow that slows diagnosis and contributes to alert fatigue. Large language models (LLMs) have shown promise for automated root cause analysis (RCA), but existing systems rely on custom, system-specific data access layers that cannot be reused across organisations. We present ARGUS, an MCP-grounded RCA assistant that connects a commercial LLM to live Kubernetes observability data through standardised MCP servers covering Kubernetes state, Prometheus metrics, Loki logs, and NATS messaging, and delivers structured diagnostic summaries inside the Slack incident channel where on-call engineers already work. We conduct a preliminary evaluation of ARGUS using three complementary methods: controlled fault injection across ten Kubernetes incident scenarios, rubric-based scoring of the resulting RCA summaries on three dimensions, and semi-structured interviews with six on-call engineers at an industrial partner. ARGUS named the correct root cause in all ten scenarios with an aggregate MCP success ratio of 0.91. Practitioners trusted the diagnostic output but consistently expressed scepticism toward the recommended fixes. Our central finding is a diagnostic/prescriptive asymmetry: ARGUS reliably identifies what went wrong, but is perceived as less reliable or trustworthy at specifying what to do next. This pattern can be observed across all three evaluation methods, and has important implications for future autonomous agentic incident handling systems.

</details>


### [89] [From Metrics to Improvement: A Lifecycle-Aware LLM Feedback Framework for Research Software Quality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23118)
*Nafis Tanveer Islam,Nafiseh Soveizi,Yutong Li,Zhiming Zhao*

Main category: cs.SE



Selection reason: cs.SE 论文按全量策略收录。

TL;DR: 提出一种将生命周期感知质量模型与LLM迭代精炼相结合的框架，用于改进研究软件质量，并在笔记本型研究软件上验证了其对代码重复和结构质量的提升，同时揭示了多目标权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究软件缺乏软件工程专业知识导致质量低下，现有静态分析工具输出需要专家解读且难以转化为具体改进措施，因此需要更自动化和可操作的框架。

Method: 构建两阶段框架：先依据标准与需求定义含5个维度、25个指标的质量模型，其中14个通过工具和定制测量实现；再利用质量诊断作为结构化反馈，驱动LLM进行迭代代码精炼，并与单步反馈和非结构化提示对比。

Result: 在笔记本型研究软件上使用多个LLM评估，发现代码重复和结构质量显著改善，但可维护性、代码规模、文档和复杂性之间出现权衡。

Conclusion: 指标驱动的LLM反馈能有效提升研究软件质量，但需注意其多目标优化本质，为未来研究软件质量改进提供了新方向。

中文摘要：研究软件在科学工作流程中日益核心，但通常由软件工程专业知识有限的研究人员开发。这可能导致阻碍可维护性、可复现性、可重用性和可持续性的质量问题。现有的静态分析工具可以识别此类问题，但其输出通常需要专家解读，并且在将质量评估转化为可操作的改进方面提供的支持有限。为了弥补这一差距，我们提出一个生命周期感知框架，将定量软件质量评估与基于大型语言模型（LLM）的代码精炼相结合。该框架包含两个阶段。首先，根据既定的软件质量标准和从业者需求开发生命周期感知质量模型。该模型定义了五个质量维度和25个候选指标，其中14个使用现有分析工具和定制测量进行可操作化。其次，将所得质量诊断用作迭代LLM精炼过程中的结构化反馈，使生成的改进能够根据质量模型反复重新评估。我们在以笔记本为中心的研究软件上使用多个LLM评估该框架，并将迭代结构化反馈与单步反馈和非结构化提示进行比较。结果显示在特定质量属性上有所改进，尤其是代码重复和结构质量，同时也揭示了可维护性、代码规模、文档和复杂性之间的权衡。这些发现证明了指标驱动的LLM反馈在研究软件质量改进方面的潜力，同时突出了其固有的多目标性质（注：源代码和实验数据可在 https://github.com/QCDIS/Software_Quality_Control_LLM 公开获取）。

Original abstract: Research software is increasingly central to scientific workflows, yet it is often developed by researchers with limited software engineering expertise. This can lead to quality issues that hinder maintainability, reproducibility, reuse, and sustainability. Existing static analysis tools can identify such issues, but their outputs often require expert interpretation and provide limited support for translating quality assessments into actionable improvements. To address this gap, we propose a lifecycle-aware framework that integrates quantitative software quality assessment with Large Language Model (LLM)-based code refinement. The framework comprises two stages. First, a lifecycle-aware Quality Model is developed from established software quality standards and practitioner requirements. The model defines five quality dimensions and 25 candidate metrics, of which 14 are operationalized using existing analysis tools and custom measurements. Second, the resulting quality diagnostics are used as structured feedback within an iterative LLM-based refinement process, enabling generated improvements to be repeatedly reassessed against the Quality Model. We evaluate the framework on notebook-centric research software using multiple LLMs and compare iterative structured feedback with single-step feedback and unstructured prompting. The results show improvements in specific quality attributes, particularly code duplication and structural quality, while also revealing trade-offs among maintainability, code size, documentation, and complexity. These findings demonstrate the potential of metric-driven LLM feedback for research software quality improvement while highlighting its inherently multi-objective nature \footnote{The source code and experimental data are publicly available at https://github.com/QCDIS/Software_Quality_Control_LLM . }

</details>


### [90] [An AI-Assisted Migration Framework for Transforming Legacy Scientific Applications into Reusable Cloud-Based Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23146)
*Nafiseh Soveizi,Sven Tesselaar,Hero Robinson Brouwer,Zhiming Zhao*

Main category: cs.SE

Comments: 8 pages, 2 figures, 5 tables. Accepted at the 22nd IEEE International Conference on e-Science (eScience 2026)

Selection reason: cs.SE 论文按全量策略收录。

TL;DR: 提出一个AI辅助框架，结合RM-ODP、LLM和DSM分析，将遗留科学应用迁移为可重用的云原生工作流，并在两个不同领域的应用中验证可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 遗留科学应用与特定执行环境耦合，难以复用、复现和部署到现代工作流系统，而现有迁移方法仅针对单个工件，缺乏系统性转换支持。

Method: 框架首先利用RM-ODP指导LLM识别工作流组件、接口和依赖；然后通过DSM分析迭代评估和优化工作流结构；最后用基于LLM的生成器实现组件并生成容器化环境和可执行工作流定义。

Result: 在两个不同科学领域的遗留应用上成功转换为可复用云原生工作流，功能保持不变，证明了方法可行性。

Conclusion: 该AI辅助迁移框架能有效现代化遗留科学软件，使其适应云原生工作流系统和VRE部署。

中文摘要：遗留科学应用程序仍然是有价值的研究资产，但通常与特定于项目的执行环境紧密耦合，限制了它们在现代科学工作流系统和云原生虚拟研究环境（VRE）中的重用、可复现性和部署。现有的迁移方法主要针对单个工件（如笔记本或容器），并且对将异构遗留应用程序系统性地转换为可重用的云原生工作流的支持有限。本文提出了一个AI辅助迁移框架，该框架结合了开放分布式处理参考模型（RM-ODP）引导的架构分析、大型语言模型（LLM）和设计结构矩阵（DSM）分析。该框架首先使用RM-ODP引导LLM从异构遗留应用程序中识别可重用的工作流组件、它们的接口和执行依赖关系。然后使用DSM分析对生成的工作流结构进行迭代评估和细化。最后，基于LLM的工作流生成器实现验证过的工作流组件，并生成容器化执行环境和可执行的工作流定义，以部署在云原生工作流系统（包括VRE）中。该框架在两个来自不同科学领域的遗留科学应用程序上进行了评估。在这两种情况下，应用程序都成功转换为可重用的云原生工作流，同时保留了其原始功能，证明了所提出的方法用于现代化遗留科学软件的可行性。

Original abstract: Legacy scientific applications remain valuable research assets but are often tightly coupled to project-specific execution environments, limiting their reuse, reproducibility, and deployment within modern scientific workflow systems and cloud-native Virtual Research Environments (VREs). Existing migration approaches primarily target individual artifacts, such as notebooks or containers, and provide limited support for systematically transforming heterogeneous legacy applications into reusable cloud-native workflows. This paper presents an AI-assisted migration framework that combines the Reference Model of Open Distributed Processing (RM-ODP)-guided architectural analysis, Large Language Models (LLMs), and Design Structure Matrix (DSM) analysis. The framework first uses RM-ODP to guide an LLM in identifying reusable workflow components, their interfaces, and execution dependencies from heterogeneous legacy applications. The resulting workflow structure is then iteratively evaluated and refined using DSM analysis. Finally, an LLM-based workflow generator implements the validated workflow components and produces containerized execution environments and executable workflow definitions for deployment in cloud-native workflow systems, including VREs. The framework was evaluated on two legacy scientific applications from different scientific domains. In both cases, the applications were successfully transformed into reusable cloud-native workflows while preserving their original functionality, demonstrating the feasibility of the proposed approach for modernizing legacy scientific software.

</details>


### [91] [TianoForge: An Automated Bug Triage Approach for the TianoCore UEFI Firmware Development Community](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23259)
*Nazanin Siavash,Terrance E. Boult,Armin Moin*

Main category: cs.SE

Comments: ACM International Workshop on Firmware Testing and Analysis (FTA) 2026, Co-located with ISSTA 2026

Selection reason: cs.SE 论文按全量策略收录。

TL;DR: TianoForge利用GPT大语言模型和RAG实现自动化缺陷分类，将平均缺陷分类时间从11天缩短至约7分钟，效率提升99.95%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 缺陷分类在软件维护中至关重要，而TianoCore社区（尤其是EDK II项目）有大量未分类的问题，传统人工分类耗时耗力，亟需自动化解决方案。

Method: 提出集成方法TianoForge，使用多种GPT大语言模型（LLMs），结合或不结合检索增强生成（RAG），实现无效报告检测、重复检测、优先级排序和分配等自动化缺陷分类任务。

Result: 实验表明，TianoForge将平均缺陷分类时间从约11天降低至约7分钟，减少了99.95%。

Conclusion: TianoForge可显著提升TianoCore软件维护流程的效率，对缺陷分类与解决产生重大影响。

中文摘要：我们提出了一种新颖的缺陷分类方法，用于TianoCore开源UEFI固件开发生态系统。这种集成方法称为TianoForge，部署了最先进的人工智能，特别是机器学习，以实现自动化缺陷分类。这包括无效缺陷报告检测、重复缺陷报告检测、缺陷报告优先级排序和缺陷报告分配。我们使用各种生成式预训练Transformer（GPT）大语言模型（LLM），结合或不结合检索增强生成（RAG）来自动化这些任务。鉴于缺陷分类在软件维护中的关键作用以及TianoCore社区中大量未分类的问题，特别是在其主要项目EDK II中，我们预计这将显著影响TianoCore软件维护流程的效率，主要是缺陷分类和解决。我们的实验研究表明，TianoForge将平均缺陷分类时间从约11天减少到约7分钟，减少了99.95%。

Original abstract: We propose a novel approach to bug triage in the TianoCore open-source UEFI firmware development ecosystem. This integrated approach, called TianoForge, deploys the state of the art in artificial intelligence, specifically machine learning, to enable automated bug triage. This includes invalid bug report detection, duplicate bug report detection, bug report prioritization, and bug report assignment. We use various Generative Pretrained Transformer (GPT) Large Language Models (LLMs) with and without Retrieval Augmented Generation (RAG) to automate these tasks. Given the crucial role of bug triage in software maintenance and the huge number of untriaged issues in the TianoCore community, in particular, their primary project, EDK II, we expect a significant impact on the efficiency of TianoCore software maintenance processes, primarily bug triage and resolution. Our experimental study shows that TianoForge reduces the average bug triage time from around 11 days to approximately 7 minutes, which is a 99.95% reduction.

</details>


### [92] [From Natural Language Policies to Executable Obligations: A Verification Harness for Dependable In-Car LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23282)
*Radouane Bouchekir,Damir Safin,Tomas Bueno Momcilovic*

Main category: cs.SE



Selection reason: cs.SE 论文按全量策略收录。

TL;DR: AgentGuardUtil通过运行时策略编译器将自然语言策略编译为可执行规则，结合确定性义务引擎、25个确定性门和LLM批评者，在验证-修订循环中修正LLM规划器的输出，以在CAR-bench Track 1上满足严格操作策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体在车辆场景中必须逐轮满足书面操作策略，任何微小的错误（如幻觉标识符、遗漏副作用、过早完成）都导致失败，因此需要一种可靠的机制来验证和修正LLM的规划输出。

Method: 将LLM作为可出错的提议者，采用基于事实的验证-修订循环。核心是运行时策略编译器，将自然语言策略编译为类型化的机器可检查规则，部分规则可执行。确定性义务引擎结合实时工具结果和模拟写入后状态，产生精确的修正调用。同时使用25个确定性门和LLM批评者生成分层发现，驱动有界修订循环。

Result: 作为CAR-bench Track 1的参赛系统，AgentGuardUtil利用上述验证-修订循环满足每轮操作策略要求，并通过25个确定性门和LLM批评者协同驱动修订，针对pass k指标进行优化。

Conclusion: AgentGuardUtil展示了将自然语言策略编译为可检查规则，并利用确定性引擎与LLM批评者相结合的验证-修订循环，可有效解决LLM智能体在严格策略约束下的可靠性问题。

中文摘要：在车辆中部署的大型语言模型（LLM）智能体必须在每一轮都满足书面操作策略：单个幻觉标识符、遗漏的强制性副作用或过早的完成声明都会导致任务失败。我们提出了AgentGuardUtil，这是我们参加CAR-bench Track 1的参赛作品，它将AI规划器（LLM）视为一个可出错的提议者，置于一个基于事实的验证-修订循环中。其核心新颖之处在于一个运行时策略编译器：随每次对话提供的自然语言策略被一次性编译成类型化的机器可检查规则，其中一部分规则获得可执行形式。一个确定性的义务引擎根据实时工具结果和草稿本身的模拟写入后状态来解释这些规则，发出带有计算参数的确切修正调用，而不是自然语言提醒。围绕该引擎，25个确定性门（标识符来源、模式和枚举有效性、先收集后行动、确认和未来时间协议）以及一个LLM批评者产生分层发现，驱动一个有界的修订循环，该循环针对pass k指标进行了调优。

Original abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) agents deployed in vehicles must satisfy a written operating policy on every turn: a single hallucinated identifier, omitted mandatory side-effect, or premature completion claim fails the task. We present AgentGuardUtil, our entry to CAR-bench Track~1, which treats the AI planer (LLM) as a fallible proposer inside a grounded verify-and-revise loop. Its core novelty is a runtime policy compiler: the natural-language policy shipped with each conversation is compiled, once per policy, into typed machine-checkable rules, a subset of which receive an executable form. A deterministic obligation engine interprets these rules against live tool results and the simulated post-write state of the draft itself, emitting the exact remedial calls with computed arguments rather than natural-language reminders. Around this engine, 25 deterministic gates (identifier provenance, schema and enum validity, gather-before-act, confirmation and future-time protocols) and an LLM critic produce tiered findings that drive a bounded revision loop tuned for the pass k metric.

</details>


### [93] [DPIAgent: Divide, Protocol, Isolate for Agentic Reproduction Test Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23341)
*Hao Liu,Steven Liu,Xin Zhang,Jane Luo,Yu Kang,Jie Wu,Fangkai Yang,Yangyu Huang,Pengfei Gao,Scarlett Li,Yan Lu*

Main category: cs.SE



Selection reason: cs.SE 论文按全量策略收录。

TL;DR: DPIAgent通过划分、协议、隔离三原则将复现测试生成任务分解为单目标阶段，显著提升成功率，在SWT-Bench Verified上达到81.76%（GPT-5），加测试选择后达86.17%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体方法将复现测试生成视为单体循环，未分离诊断和测试生成两个不同子任务，导致复合目标模糊和中间目标不明确，引发目标漂移。

Method: DPIAgent基于三条原则：划分（Divide）——将任务分为缺陷探索和测试生成两个单目标阶段；协议（Protocol）——通过交接协议记录诊断和测试计划，防止上下文丢失；隔离（Isolate）——为每个阶段定制工具集，隔离动作空间，避免无关工具干扰。

Result: 在SWT-Bench Verified上，DPIAgent在三个骨干LLM上优于七个基线。仅用DPI在GPT-5上达81.76%成功率，为开源方法最高；在GPT-5-Mini上比最强基线高11.88个百分点；加入测试选择后进一步提升至86.17%。

Conclusion: 架构结构和骨干能力是互补的而非替代关系，DPI框架在不同模型类别上具有泛化性，证明了结构化分解对复合任务的有效性。

中文摘要：复现测试生成，即生成一个先失败后通过的测试以捕获所报告的错误，是自动化软件工程中的关键步骤。现有的基于智能体的方法将此视为一个单体循环，尽管该任务本质上包含两个性质不同的子任务：诊断根本原因和编写失败-通过测试。在没有明确分离的情况下，智能体面临着一个具有不明确中间目标的复合目标，导致目标漂移。我们提出了DPIAgent，一个基于三条原则——划分（Divide）、协议（Protocol）、隔离（Isolate）——的结构化智能体框架，以缓解复合目标模糊性和目标漂移：它将任务划分为缺陷探索和测试生成两个单目标阶段；强制执行交接协议（handoff Protocol），记录诊断和测试计划，防止上下文丢失；并通过为每个阶段定制工具集来隔离其动作空间，防止不相关的工具误导执行。在SWT-Bench Verified上，DPIAgent在三个骨干大语言模型上优于七个基线。仅使用DPI，在GPT-5上达到81.76%的成功率，这是开源方法中报告的最高值，在GPT-5-Mini上比最强基线高出11.88个百分点；添加测试选择后进一步将其提升至86.17%。我们的分析表明，架构结构和骨干能力是互补轴而非替代关系，证明了DPI在不同模型类别中的泛化性。

Original abstract: Reproduction test generation, producing a failing-then-passing test that captures a reported bug, is a critical step in automated software engineering. Existing agentic methods treat this as a monolithic loop, despite the task inherently comprising two subtasks of distinct nature: diagnosing the root cause and writing a fail-to-pass test. Without explicit separation, the agent faces a compound objective with underspecified intermediate goals, leading to goal drift. We propose DPIAgent, a structured agentic framework built on three principles, Divide, Protocol, Isolate (DPI), that mitigates compound-objective ambiguity and goal drift: it Divides the task into single-objective phases of defect exploration and test generation; enforces a handoff Protocol that records the diagnosis and test plan, preventing context loss; and Isolates each phase's action space by tailoring the toolset to its task, preventing irrelevant tools from misleading execution. On SWT-Bench Verified, DPIAgent outperforms seven baselines across three backbone LLMs. With DPI alone it reaches 81.76% success rate on GPT-5, the highest reported among open-source methods, gaining up to 11.88 points over the strongest baseline on GPT-5-Mini; adding test selection further raises it to 86.17%. Our analysis shows that architectural structure and backbone capability are complementary axes rather than substitutes, demonstrating DPI's generalizability across model classes.

</details>


### [94] [Formalizing and Automating Fine-Grained Move Refactorings Across Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23377)
*Kota Yasuhara,Shinpei Hayashi*

Main category: cs.SE

Comments: (C) 2026 IEEE. Personal use of this material is permitted. Permission from IEEE must be obtained for all other uses, in any current or future media, including reprinting/republishing this material for advertising or promotional purposes, creating new collective works, for resale or redistribution to servers or lists, or reuse of any copyrighted component of this work in other works

Selection reason: cs.SE 论文按全量策略收录。

TL;DR: 本文形式化了 Move Statement 重构的五个变体，基于四个基本条件，并在真实项目上迭代补充了二十个额外条件，在十个项目上验证了其适用性和可编译性（93.3-97.0%），并通过案例研究确认行为变化来自副作用重排序而非静态检查缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 类和方法级别的 Move 重构已自动化，但语句和表达式级别的移动仍缺乏自动化，因此需要形式化定义和检查条件来支持这种重构。

Method: 形式化五个 Move Statement 重构变体，基于四个基本条件（数据可达性、执行次数、副作用、语法约束），除副作用外静态检查；结合现有技术支持表达式和部分表达式移动；针对真实项目迭代增加二十个额外前置条件和步骤以处理 Java 语法多样性。

Result: 在十个项目上评估：适用案例中 93.3%-97.0% 产生可编译代码；案例研究显示行为变化源于副作用重排序而非静态检查条件缺陷。

Conclusion: Move Statement 重构可以通过静态检查大部分条件实现自动化和行为保持，副作用重排序需开发者判断，该形式化可有效处理 Java 实际语法多样性。

中文摘要：开发者使用自动化的 Move 重构来改进源代码的模块结构和职责分配。类和方法的 Move 重构在现代 IDE 中已自动化，但调整方法边界的语句级和表达式级移动在很大程度上仍未自动化。我们将 Move Statement 重构的五个变体形式化为前置条件和步骤，这些条件和步骤基于四个基本条件：数据可达性、执行次数、副作用以及编译所需的语法约束，其中除副作用条件外均可静态检查。结合现有技术，这还能实现表达式和部分表达式的更细粒度移动。我们进一步针对一个真实项目迭代地优化该形式化，推导出二十个额外的前置条件和步骤，以处理 Java 语法多样性。我们在十个项目上评估了适用性和可编译性，并对其中的一个项目进行了行为保持的案例研究：Move Statement 重构在适用案例中产生可编译代码的比例为 93.3%-97.0%，案例研究表明观察到的行为变化源于留给开发者判断的副作用重排序，而非静态检查条件的缺陷。

Original abstract: Developers use automated Move refactorings to improve the modular structure of source code and the assignment of responsibilities. Class- and method-level Move refactorings are automated in modern IDEs, but statement- and expression-level moves that adjust method boundaries remain largely unautomated. We formalize five variants of Move Statement refactoring as preconditions and steps grounded in four basic conditions covering data reachability, execution count, side effects, and syntactic constraints required for compilation, of which all but the side-effect condition are checked statically. Combined with existing techniques, this also yields finer-grained moves of expressions and partial expressions. We further refine the formalization iteratively against a real project, deriving twenty additional preconditions and steps that handle Java syntactic diversity in practice. We evaluate applicability and compilability on ten projects, and behavior preservation in a case study on one of them: Move Statement refactorings yield compilable code in 93.3-97.0% of applicable cases, and the case study shows that the observed behavioral changes stem from side-effect reordering left to developer judgment, not from defects in the statically checked conditions.

</details>


### [95] [An Interactive Agent for Requirement-Driven Candidate Sourcing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23501)
*Yuanpeng He,Fangjing Li,Xiangyu Ru,Kexin Sun,Kun Yang,Lijian Li,Chi-Man Pun,Qingsong Wen,Wenpin Jiao,Mingkai Guo,Yirong Feng,Daiheng Gao,Zhi Jin*

Main category: cs.SE

Comments: 12 pages

Selection reason: cs.SE 论文按全量策略收录。

TL;DR: 本文提出需求驱动的候选人寻源智能体\sys{}和基准\bench{}，证明在21个系统、691个需求上以100%覆盖率和2.5倍产出占优，并召回0.241的并集池相关人选，是次优系统的1.9倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然语言找人请求本质上是欠定的需求工程问题，而非纯检索：请求含隐式约束、多有效答案且无验收标准，必须先引导、验证和核实需求，搜索才有意义。

Method: 提出\sys{}，一个交互式需求驱动候选人寻源智能体，基于有界引导、工作流模板、两阶段提交协议和双向终止守卫完成需求引出、验证、检索与核验；同时构建\bench{}基准，覆盖标准锚定验证、多模型证据oracle构建和成本感知核实。

Result: 在21个系统、691个需求上，\sys{}达到100%覆盖率且产出为2.5倍；90%返回的人在20个LLM+网页基线组合中均未被发现；基于证据的评判显示其召回最相关真实人选0.241（并集池），为次优系统的1.9倍，bootstrap 95%置信区间与所有基线不相交。

Conclusion: 因此\sys{}是最强的候选人寻源引擎，能提供最深的真实可触达候选池；精确排序的LLM应作为互补的验证者，而非独立的检索器。

中文摘要：从自然语言描述（例如“从机器学习工程师转型到生物技术研发岗位”）中找人，正越来越多地被委托给LLM智能体，并被框定为信息检索任务。我们认为，这从根本上是一项需求工程任务：这样的请求是一个约束不明确的需求，包含隐式约束、许多有效答案，并且没有验收标准，因此在搜索发挥作用之前，有用的答案需要先进行需求引出（eliciting）、验证（validating）和核实（verifying）。我们提出了\sys{}——据我们所知，第一个交互式的、需求驱动的候选人寻源智能体（它通过有界引导、工作流模板、两阶段提交协议和双向终止守卫，将模糊的人员请求引出、验证、检索并核验为一个有依据的候选人名单），以及\bench{}——一个运行需求生命周期的基准（基于标准锚定的验证、多模型证据支撑的oracle构建，以及成本感知的核实）。在21个系统和全部691个需求中，\sys{}在广度上占优（以2.5倍的产出实现100%的覆盖率），并且与该领域近似正交：它返回的人中90%没有被20个强大的LLM+网页基线组合中的任何一个找到。除了广度之外，对每个系统进行基于证据的评判表明，\sys{}能召回最相关的真实人选：在并集池中为0.241，是下一个系统的1.9倍，且其bootstrap 95%置信区间与所有基线不相交。因此，\sys{}是最强的“寻源”引擎（最深的真实、可触达的候选池），而精确排序的LLM则充当互补的验证者。

Original abstract: Finding people from a natural-language description (``ML engineers transitioning to research roles in biotech'') is increasingly delegated to LLM agents and framed as information retrieval. We argue that it is fundamentally a requirements engineering task: such a request is an under-determined requirement with implicit constraints, many valid answers, and no acceptance criterion, so useful answers require eliciting, validating, and verifying the requirement before search can matter. We present \sys{}, to our knowledge the first interactive, requirements-driven candidate-sourcing agent (it elicits, validates, retrieves, and verifies a vague people-request into a justified slate through bounded elicitation, workflow templates, a two-stage commit protocol, and bidirectional termination guards) and \bench{}, a benchmark that runs the requirements lifecycle (criteria-anchored validation, multi-model evidence-grounded oracle construction, and cost-aware verification). Across $21$ systems and all $691$ requirements, \sys{} dominates breadth ($100%$ coverage at $2.5\times$ the yield) and is \emph{near-orthogonal} to the field, with $90%$ of the people it returns are surfaced by \emph{none} of $20$ strong LLM-plus-web baselines combined. Beyond breadth, an evidence-grounded judging of every system shows \sys{} \emph{recalls} the most relevant real people: $0.241$ of the union pool, $1.9\times$ the next system, with a bootstrap $95%$ interval disjoint from every baseline. \sys{} is thus the strongest \emph{sourcing} engine (the deepest real, reachable candidate pool), while precision-ranking LLMs serve as~complementary verifiers.

</details>


### [96] [Architecture as Capability Equalizer for Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21747)
*Arquimedes Canedo*

Main category: cs.SE



Selection reason: cs.SE 论文按全量策略收录。

TL;DR: 架构规范的格式对代码质量的影响随模型能力而变化：结构化、接近代码的规范（如TypeScript契约）能显著提升弱模型的表现，缩小与前沿模型的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管LLM编码智能体广泛使用，但架构规范格式对生成代码质量的影响及其与模型能力的交互关系尚不明确，需要系统化的受控实验来揭示这一关键因素。

Method: 开展受控实验，比较五种信息等价的规范格式（非正式散文、Mermaid图、OpenAPI、C4/Structurizr DSL、TypeScript接口契约），在90次多轮智能体试验中测试来自Anthropic、OpenAI、Google的六个模型，并量化生成代码的质量、token消耗和自验证率。

Result: 格式效应因模型而异：最强模型受格式影响极小（质量差距0.17-0.92），弱模型受格式影响大（0.83-2.42）；代码近邻格式（OpenAPI、TypeScript）显著恢复弱模型的能力差距；TypeScript契约使最弱模型API路由覆盖率从33%升至100%；弱模型自验证率低且易陷入编译调试循环。

Conclusion: 结构化架构规范是能力均衡器，其价值随模型能力增强而递减，对于成本优化部署和较弱模型尤为关键，能有效弥补模型能力不足并提升代码质量。

中文摘要：基于LLM的编码智能体能够从高层描述生成完整的软件系统，然而关于架构规范的格式如何影响生成代码的质量，或者这种影响是否依赖于模型能力，目前知之甚少。我们进行了一项受控实验，比较了五种信息等价的规范格式（非正式散文、带约束和ADR的Mermaid图、OpenAPI、C4/Structurizr DSL，以及带ArchUnit风格规则的TypeScript接口契约），涉及来自三个供应商家族的六个模型（Anthropic Claude、OpenAI GPT、Google Gemini）。在90次多轮智能体试验中，规范格式显示出强烈的格式×模型交互效应。在最强模型（Sonnet 4.6、GPT-5）上，格式几乎无关紧要（质量差距0.17-0.92）。在较弱模型上，格式产生了0.83-2.42分的差距，其中接近代码的格式（OpenAPI、TypeScript契约）弥补了大部分能力差距。中等模型在陷入编译调试循环时，可能比前沿模型消耗更多token却产生更差的输出，而更强模型则会避免这种循环。自我验证率从Sonnet的100%骤降至Gemini Flash的0%。TypeScript契约使最弱模型的API路由覆盖率提高了两倍（从33%提升至100%）。结构化架构规范起到了能力均衡器的作用，其价值与模型强度成反比，且对成本优化部署收益最大。

Original abstract: LLM-based coding agents generate complete software systems from high-level descriptions, yet little is known about how the format of architecture specifications affects the quality of generated code or whether this effect depends on model capability. We present a controlled experiment comparing five informationally equivalent specification formats (informal prose, Mermaid diagrams with constraints and ADRs, OpenAPI, C4/Structurizr DSL, and TypeScript interface contracts with ArchUnit-style rules) across six models from three vendor families (Anthropic Claude, OpenAI GPT, Google Gemini). Across 90 multi-turn agent trials, specification format shows a strong format x model interaction. On the strongest models (Sonnet 4.6, GPT-5), format barely matters (quality spread 0.17-0.92). On weaker models, format produces spreads of 0.83-2.42 points, with code-proximate formats (OpenAPI, TypeScript contracts) recovering most of the capability gap. Mid-tier models can consume more tokens than frontier models for worse output when they enter compilation debugging loops that stronger models avoid. Self-validation rates collapse from 100% (Sonnet) to 0% (Gemini Flash) across the capability spectrum. TypeScript contracts triple API route coverage for the weakest model (33% to 100%). Structured architecture specifications serve as a capability equalizer, with value inversely proportional to model strength and the largest returns for cost-optimized deployments.

</details>


### [97] [Evaluating Inference-Time Defenses Against Package Hallucination in LLM-Generated Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22652)
*Alberick Euraste Djire,Iyiola E. Olatunji,Melissa Tessa,Earl T. Barr,Jacques Klein,Tegawendé F. Bissyandé*

Main category: cs.SE

Comments: Accepted ASE 2026

DOI: [10.1145/3832783.3837555](https://doi.org/10.1145/3832783.3837555)

Selection reason: cs.SE 论文按全量策略收录。

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了LLM代码生成中的包幻觉问题，提出改进的评估方法、多种防御策略及效用指标，并发现对抗性提示下RAG和Self-Refine效果最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在代码生成中常幻觉出不存在的软件包，构成软件供应链安全风险，而现有评估方法存在高估问题，且缺乏对不同防御策略的系统比较。

Method: 提出四项贡献：纠正标准库误分类的评估偏差；评估七种推理时防御（五种解码策略、Self-Refine、RAG）；引入包效用（PU）指标；并在对抗性提示下压力测试所有策略。

Result: 标准库误分类导致Python幻觉率高估9.4个百分点；RAG在18/32配置中降低PHR；Greedy解码取得最佳缓解-效用权衡；对抗提示使PHR最多上升45个百分点，Ruby最脆弱（80.9%-95.2%），RAG和Self-Refine优于纯解码策略。

Conclusion: 包幻觉既是测量问题也是解码控制问题，防御选择需匹配威胁模型和推荐效用；对抗性环境下需要外部知识检索或迭代自验证以获得稳健防御。

中文摘要：LLM越来越多地用于代码生成，但它们经常幻觉出不存在的软件包，从而在软件供应链中创建可利用的入口点。我们针对这个问题做出了四项贡献。首先，我们表明先前的评估方法通过在某些语言中将标准库模块错误分类为幻觉，系统性地夸大了幻觉率。对于Python，过度估计达到了9.4个百分点。其次，我们评估了七种用于缓解包幻觉的推理时防御方法，包括五种引导解码策略（Greedy、Contrastive、DoLa、Nudging和Active Layer-Contrastive Decoding）、一种迭代自我改进方法（Self-Refine）以及一种基于检索增强生成（RAG）的防御方法。跨越五个模型家族的八个模型和四种编程语言（Python、JavaScript、Ruby、Rust），RAG在32个模型-语言配置中的18个中降低了包幻觉率（PHR）。第三，我们引入了包效用（PU）来评估防御是否保留了有效且任务相关的推荐。在评估的策略中，Greedy解码提供了最强的平均缓解-效用权衡。第四，我们使用包含捏造包名的对抗性提示对所有策略进行了压力测试，发现相对于标准提示，PHR最高飙升了45个百分点，其中Ruby始终是最易受攻击的语言（80.9%-95.2%）。在对抗条件下，RAG和Self-Refine优于所有仅解码策略，表明当提示是主动敌对时，稳健的防御需要外部接地或迭代自我验证。我们的结果将包幻觉重新定义为既是测量问题又是解码时间控制问题，并表明防御的选择必须与威胁模型和推荐效用相匹配。

Original abstract: LLMs are increasingly used for code generation, yet they frequently hallucinate non-existent software packages, creating exploitable entry points into the software supply chain. We make four contributions to this problem. First, we show that prior evaluation methodologies systematically inflate hallucination rates by misclassifying standard-library modules as hallucinations in some languages. For Python, the overestimation reaches 9.4 percentage points. Second, we evaluate seven inference-time defenses for mitigating package hallucinations, including five guided decoding strategies (Greedy, Contrastive, DoLa, Nudging, and Active Layer-Contrastive Decoding), an iterative self-refinement approach (Self-Refine), and a Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG)-based defense.. Across eight models spanning five families and four programming languages (Python, JavaScript, Ruby, Rust), RAG reduces the package hallucination rate (PHR) in 18 of 32 model--language configurations. Third, we introduce Package Utility (PU) to assess whether defenses preserve valid and task-relevant recommendations. Among strategies evaluated, Greedy decoding provides the strongest average mitigation--utility trade-off. Fourth, we stress-test all strategies under adversarial prompts seeded with fabricated package names and find that PHR surges by up to 45 percentage points relative to standard prompts, with Ruby consistently the most vulnerable language (80.9--95.2\%). Under adversarial conditions, RAG and Self-Refine outperform all decoding-only strategies, indicating that robust defense requires either external grounding or iterative self-verification when prompts are actively hostile.
  Our results recast package hallucination as both a measurement problem and a decoding-time control problem, and they demonstrate that the choice of defense must be matched to the threat model and recommendation utility.

</details>
